### [1] [PriorMaskMap: Robust Online Vectorized Map Construction with Biased Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39283)
*Haoming Xu,Wei Li,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PriorMaskMap，一种面向在线矢量化地图构建的鲁棒先验引导框架，通过置信度评估与掩码引导的选择性先验融合，在先验地图不完美时仍保持高精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线矢量化地图构建为自动驾驶提供实时语义与几何理解；利用先验地图可提升精度，但先验常因信息过时或临时道路变化而有偏。现有方法通常将先验视为完全可靠或仅考虑有限噪声模式，在先验不完美时性能严重下降。

Method: PriorMaskMap包含三项核心设计：Prior-Aware Confidence Estimator（PACE）将输入栅格化地图与当前视觉观测对比，对可靠、错误和空白区域赋予置信度，形成基础掩码并引导两个分支；Dual-Prior Query Generator（DPQG）选择性过滤并嵌入栅格化与矢量化先验，从可信地图结构中初始化信息丰富的查询；Confidence-Guided Prior Fusion（CGPF）在空间上传播并细化掩码，用细化后的掩码控制BEV特征与先验地图的融合，为地图解码器提供丰富上下文表示。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，PriorMaskMap的地图构建精度优于现有方法，并在输入先验存在明显错误时表现出显著鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PriorMaskMap通过显式评估先验可靠性并选择性融合可信先验信息，有效缓解不完美先验带来的性能退化，为鲁棒先验引导矢量化地图构建提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Online vectorized map construction provides autonomous vehicles with essential, real-time semantic and geometric scene understanding. While leveraging prior maps presents an opportunity to improve accuracy, they are often biased due to outdated information or temporary road changes. Existing methods, which typically treat priors as fully reliable or only consider limited noise patterns, suffer severe performance degradation when these priors are imperfect. To address this, we propose PriorMaskMap, a robust framework for prior-guided vectorized map construction. Its first innovation is a Prior-Aware Confidence Estimator (PACE) that evaluates the input rasterized map against current visual observations. It assigns confidence scores to regions classified as reliable, erroneous, or blank, creating a foundational mask that guides two subsequent branches. In one branch, the Dual-Prior Query Generator (DPQG) selectively filters and embeds both rasterized and vectorized priors to initialize informative queries from trustworthy map structures. In the other, the Confidence-Guided Prior Fusion (CGPF) module spatially propagates and refines the mask, using the refined version to govern the fusion of BEV features with the prior map, thereby providing rich contextual representations for the map decoder. Evaluation on the nuScenes dataset shows that PriorMaskMap achieves superior mapping precision over existing approaches and exhibits marked robustness when provided with priors containing apparent errors. Our code is released at this https URL .

</details>


### [2] [WorldFlow: Joint Video and 3D Motion Prediction via Large-Scale Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39278)
*Yuhao Lu,Jiongxu Chen,Hexiang Wei,Jiajin Wu,Juelin Li,Mingjian Liang,Fan Yang,Zhuowei Liang,Zihao Liu,Zhennan Wang,Sheng Wang,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldFlow 是一个联合预测未来视频与查询式3D轨迹的生成式世界模型，通过将3D运动表示为与视频网格对齐的连续位移token，并用统一MoT骨干在rectified-flow目标下联合去噪，实现几何动态与视觉演化的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型对3D动态建模是隐式的，基于动作的方法依赖特定机器人构型，3D运动预测模型仅用flow监督，难以为物理动态提供充分学习信号。

Method: WorldFlow将3D运动参数化为与视频网格对齐的连续位移token，采用统一Mixture-of-Transformers骨干，在rectified-flow目标下联合去噪未来视频和基于查询的3D轨迹；并在涵盖仿真与真实域的144万对象级异构样本上训练，提出WorldFlowBench评估基准。

Result: 在三个评估集上，WorldFlow在所学方法中取得最低ADE和FDE；在SpatialCog集上ADE为0.060 m，较最强外部基线降低54.2%；相比仅flow监督变体，ADE和FDE分别降低55.9%和68.5%。

Conclusion: WorldFlow通过统一视频生成与显式3D运动预测，有效释放预训练视频模型中的物理常识，提升未来物理状态与空间推理预测性能，并优于现有学习型基线及flow-only变体。

Abstract: Predicting future physical states stands as a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Existing video world models leave 3D dynamics implicit, action-based formulations remain tied to specific robot configurations, and 3D motion prediction models use flow-only supervision, providing a limited learning signal for physical dynamics. To circumvent these limitations, we introduce \method{}, a generative world model that jointly predicts future video and query-based 3D trajectories. By parameterizing 3D motion as continuous displacement tokens aligned with the video grid, a unified Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbone jointly denoises both modalities under a rectified-flow objective. This formulation couples explicit geometric dynamics with visual evolution, effectively unlocking the physical commonsense embedded within pretrained video models. To empirically scale and validate this unified architecture, we train \method{} on a heterogeneous mixture of 1.44 million object-level samples spanning simulated and real-world domains. We further introduce WorldFlowBench, comprising 1,678 evaluation samples from 11 data sources across simulated and real-world interactions, including spatial reasoning tasks. Across all three evaluation sets, WorldFlow achieves the lowest average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) among the evaluated learning-based methods. Notably, on the challenging SpatialCog set, WorldFlow achieves an ADE of 0.060 m, a 54.2\% reduction compared with the strongest external baseline. It also reduces ADE and FDE by 55.9\% and 68.5\%, respectively, compared with its flow-only variant. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [3] [Seeing and Imagining as One: Unified Video Generation and 4D Perception for Embodied World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39232)
*Haitang Feng,Zhuoman Liu,Zhennan Wang,Sheng Wang,Juelin Li,Chengzhi Zhao,Hexiang Wei,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenSight 是一个统一的具身世界模型，在单一预训练视频扩散 Transformer 中同时实现未来视频生成与稠密 4D 感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身世界模型不仅需要想象合理的未来，还需要感知演化世界中的几何、语义和运动结构；而分离的模态专用流水线难以耦合外观、几何、语义与运动并保持跨模态一致性。

Method: GenSight 采用统一的时空表示覆盖视频、深度、具身分割和首帧相对 2D 光流，基于共享 DiT 主干，通过轻量任务调制在同一架构内预测所有模态，并在统一速度预测空间中联合优化；推理支持多步未来视频生成及对齐稠密预测，以及从观测视频进行单步稠密感知。

Result: 在涵盖多种机器人具身和人类手部交互的具身操作数据上训练后，GenSight 在未来视频生成和稠密 4D 感知两方面均表现强劲。

Conclusion: 统一模态表示、模型架构和预测空间可避免独立的模态专用流水线，促进跨模态一致性，并使稠密感知受益于视频生成学习到的时空先验，表明“看”与“想象”的统一对具身世界建模有效。

Abstract: Embodied world models should not only imagine plausible futures, but also perceive the geometric, semantic, and motion structure of the evolving world. We present GenSight, a unified embodied world model that brings future video generation and dense perception into a single pretrained video diffusion transformer. GenSight uses a unified spatiotemporal representation for video, depth, embodiment segmentation, and first-frame-relative 2D flow. Built on a shared DiT backbone, GenSight uses lightweight task modulation to predict all modalities within the same architecture. All tasks are jointly optimized within a unified velocity prediction space. At inference, GenSight supports two complementary modes: multi-step future video generation with aligned dense predictions and single-step dense perception from an observed video. By unifying modality representation, model architecture, and prediction space, GenSight avoids separate modality-specific pipelines and couples appearance, geometry, semantics, and motion in a shared representation. It promotes cross-modal consistency and allows dense perception to benefit from the spatiotemporal priors learned through video generation. Trained on embodied manipulation data spanning multiple robot embodiments and human-hand interactions, GenSight demonstrates strong performance in both video generation and dense 4D perception, highlighting the effectiveness of unifying seeing and imagining for embodied world modeling.

</details>


### [4] [TRIM: Temporal Run-Time Monitoring of 3D Object Detection via Activation Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38613)
*Hakan Yekta Yatbaz,Konstantinos Koufos,Mehrdad Dianati*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TRIM轻量级时序监控器，用于在不修改或重训3D目标检测器的情况下检测漏检故障，并在NuScenes上通过利用时序信息提升监控性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠感知对智能交通系统至关重要，但深度神经网络可能静默漏检安全关键目标且输出看似合理；现有3D目标检测运行时监控多逐帧独立评估，无法利用遮挡、点稀疏和场景复杂度的时序演化。

Method: TRIM用紧凑的通道级统计总结检测器中间激活，并将短序列的激活摘要输入时序卷积网络（TCN）进行时序监控；该方法不修改也不重训底层检测器。

Result: 在NuScenes上，相比单帧监控，时序监控将AUROC从0.686提升至0.704，并将95%召回率下的误报率从0.818降至0.759；骨干网络早期和中间特征提供最强监控信号，时序推理头在CPU上仅需0.159 ms。

Conclusion: 利用检测器中间激活的时序演化可有效检测3D目标检测漏检故障，且TRIM轻量、无需改动检测器，具备实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Reliable perception is essential to intelligent transportation systems, yet deep neural networks can silently miss safety-critical objects while continuing to produce apparently plausible outputs. Existing run-time monitors for 3D object detection generally assess each frame independently and therefore cannot exploit the temporal evolution of occlusion, point sparsity, and scene complexity. We present TRIM, a lightweight temporal monitor that detects missed-object failures without modifying or retraining the underlying detector. TRIM summarises intermediate activations using compact channel-wise statistics and processes short sequences of these summaries with a temporal convolutional network. Experiments on NuScenes show that temporal monitoring improves AUROC from 0.686 to 0.704 and reduces the false alarm rate at 95% recall from 0.818 to 0.759 compared with single-frame monitoring. Early and intermediate backbone features provide the strongest monitoring signal, while the temporal inference head requires only 0.159 ms on a CPU.

</details>


### [5] [GOFish: Full Field-of-View Gaussian Splatting and Mesh Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37818)
*Allen Tu,Meher Gitika Karumuri,Shifan Zhu,Hailing Wang,Yen-Liang Lin,David Held,Matthias Zwicker,Tom Goldstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GOFish 是首个直接在鱼眼图像上训练、可栅格化超过 180° 视场并提取对齐网格的 3D Gaussian Splatting 表面重建方法，用于构建无需 LiDAR 的实时仿真数字孪生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人、自动驾驶和 360° 相机依赖鱼眼镜头获得大视场，但现有鱼眼 Gaussian Splatting 仅能渲染至 180° 且不生成网格，而 Gaussian 表面重建方法又假设针孔训练图像，难以满足全视场高真实感渲染与碰撞网格对齐的仿真需求。

Method: 提出 GOFish：直接基于鱼眼图像训练 3D Gaussian Splatting，并栅格化超过 180° 的视场；从同一场景提取对齐网格。采用 Calibrated Densification 恢复全视场表面，结合 Calibrated Mip Filtering 与 Wide-Angle Appearance Modeling 抑制像素模糊和镜头衰减对场景及网格的影响。

Result: 在 200° FIORD 和 205° FullCircle 基准上，PSNR 分别比最佳先前方法提升 2.28 dB 和 1.97 dB；在其自身视场内优于鱼眼渲染基线，并在 FIORD 和 ScanNet++ 主集上取得最高网格 F-score。

Conclusion: GOFish 将 Gaussian 场景与网格结合，形成无需 LiDAR、可实时渲染、支持全视场光线追踪并提供碰撞几何的仿真就绪数字孪生。

Abstract: Robots, autonomous vehicles, and consumer 360° cameras use fisheye lenses for wide fields of view (FoVs). Simulation-ready digital twins require photorealistic rendering across the full FoV and an aligned collision mesh. However, prior fisheye Gaussian Splatting methods only rasterize up to 180° and do not produce meshes, while Gaussian surface reconstruction methods assume pinhole training images. We present GOFish, the first 3D Gaussian Splatting surface reconstruction method to train directly on fisheye images, rasterize FoVs beyond 180°, and extract an aligned mesh from the same scene. Calibrated densification recovers the surface across the entire FoV, while Calibrated Mip Filtering and Wide-Angle Appearance Modeling keep pixel blur and lens falloff out of the scene and mesh. Across the 200° FIORD and 205° FullCircle benchmarks, GOFish improves PSNR over the best prior method by 2.28 and 1.97 dB, respectively. It also outperforms fisheye rendering baselines within their own FoVs and achieves the highest mesh F-score on FIORD and the main ScanNet++ set. Together, the Gaussian scene and mesh form a LiDAR-free, simulation-ready digital twin that renders in real time, ray traces the full FoV, and provides collision geometry.

</details>


### [6] [HiFOR: Hierarchical Foresight with Action-Guided World Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40373)
*Rongxing Ding,Hongyu Qu,Kehong Wu,Xudong Wang,Fan Zhang,Xinguang Xiang,Xiangbo Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 HiFOR，一个带有动作引导世界检索的分层前瞻框架，用于提升 VLA 机器人在操作任务中对环境动态和任务进展的建模与利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 方法虽引入未来预测，但通常将短期动态与长期任务目标分开建模，并且直接把预测未来注入动作策略，未充分考虑其与当前动作需求的相关性。

Method: HiFOR 包含三部分：分层前瞻预测通过 Hierarchical Dependency Mask（HDM）联合预测短期状态演化、语义里程碑和最终目标状态，并建立跨时间尺度的依赖；结构感知潜在世界建模沿时间跨度、视角和空间维度组织未来潜在表示，以支持细粒度检索；动作引导因子化世界检索在去噪过程中根据不断演化的动作表示，从结构化潜在世界中自适应检索相关未来信息，为动作生成提供针对性指导。

Result: 在两个仿真基准和六个真实世界机器人操作任务上的大量实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HiFOR 通过分层未来预测与动作相关未来信息检索，增强了 VLA 模型对短期动态和长期目标的协同利用，从而提升机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising capabilities in robotic manipulation by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and action generation. Recent advances have further incorporated future prediction to enhance the understanding of environmental dynamics and task progression. However, existing methods often model short-term dynamics and long-term task objectives separately, while directly incorporating predicted futures into action policies without sufficiently considering their relevance to current action requirements. To address these limitations, we propose HiFOR, a hierarchical foresight framework with action-guided world retrieval. Specifically, Hierarchical Foresight Prediction jointly predicts short-term state evolution, semantic milestones, and final goal states through a Hierarchical Dependency Mask (HDM), establishing dependencies across temporal horizons. Building upon these predictions, Structure-Aware Latent World Modeling organizes future latent representations along the horizon, view, and spatial dimensions to facilitate fine-grained retrieval. Furthermore, Action-Guided Factorized World Retrieval adaptively retrieves relevant future information from the structured latent world based on evolving action representations during denoising, providing targeted guidance for action generation. Extensive experiments on two simulation benchmarks and six real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>

### [7] [Forward-Backward Map Matching for Airborne Magnetic Anomaly Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38047)
*Joseph O'Leary,Tapio Simula,Tom Kimpson,Corey Davey,Wenchao Li,Andrew D. Greentree,Allison Kealy*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该文将航空磁异常导航的地图匹配建模为链式因子图上的推断，用前向-后向算法逐段求解，得到候选位置的离散后验及其香农熵，并验证熵可作为无需真值的导航模糊度诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地图匹配算法或假设高斯后验（扩展卡尔曼滤波），或依赖蒙特卡洛采样（粒子滤波），或仅保留单一最优路径而丢弃后验不确定性（Viterbi算法），因此需要一种能保留后验不确定性并可用于导航模糊度诊断的方法。

Method: 将地图匹配表述为链式结构因子图上的推断，采用前向-后向算法逐段求解，从而在时刻 t 获得候选位置的离散后验分布及其香农熵 H_t。

Result: 在 Renfrew_395 地图上，10^3 次合成试验的中位位置误差为 32.7 m，比 362 m 的中位惯性漂移低 91%；在这些试验和 10^3 条飞行路径上，中位误差比扩展卡尔曼滤波和粒子滤波低 43%。基于分段熵的阈值在 10% 虚警率下检测到 47% 的误差超过两个地图单元的分段，以及 94% 的误差超过三个地图单元的分段。

Conclusion: 结果表明，H_t 提供了一种无需真值的诊断指标，可用于评估地图条件约束下的导航模糊度。

Abstract: Airborne magnetic anomaly navigation provides a passive, unjammable alternative to Global Navigation Satellite Systems positioning by matching magnetometer measurements against a lithospheric magnetic anomaly map. Existing map-matching algorithms either assume a Gaussian posterior (extended Kalman filter), require Monte Carlo sampling (particle filter), or discard posterior uncertainty in favour of a single optimal path (Viterbi algorithm). Here we formulate map matching as inference on a chain-structured factor graph and solve it segment by segment using the forward-backward algorithm, which yields a discrete posterior over candidate positions and its Shannon entropy $H_t$ at time $t$. Over the \texttt{Renfrew\_395} map, $10^3$ synthetic trials yield a median position error of $32.7$ m, $91\%$ below the median inertial drift of $362$ m. On these trials and $10^3$ flight paths, the median error is $43\%$ below the extended Kalman filter and particle filter. Across these paths, a threshold on segment-level entropy detects $47\%$ of segments with errors above two map cells and $94\%$ of those above three, at a $10\%$ false-alarm rate. The foregoing results indicate that $H_t$ provides a truth-free diagnostic of map-conditioned navigation ambiguity.

</details>

### [8] [Limit Theorems and Optimal Policies for Flock Flight through Poisson Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39378)
*Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文将单鸟穿越随机森林的速度极限推广为多鸟群体运输问题，在Poisson disk森林中构建有向走廊网络，刻画最大群体电流、最优调度及渐近电流密度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Karaman和Frazzoli表明单鸟在ergodic随机森林中存在临界速度：超过该速度几乎必然不存在无限无碰撞轨迹。本文旨在研究多鸟情形下的bulk-transport容量与可扩展性。

Method: 在Poisson disk森林中构造显式有向走廊网络，并在航路点施加公共时间车头时距；利用最小顶点割容量和顶点不相交路径分析最大电流。对固定深度森林带，证明横向超可加性；借助非对称平面对偶的均匀形状定理处理深度与宽度共同增长；并分析对角线走廊及随机需求下的上游准入队列。

Result: 最大电流等于最小顶点割容量，并由重复沿顶点不相交路径飞行实现；同一调度在每个有限时域最大化完成穿越数，保持恒定前向速度且无内部等待。固定深度森林带存在几乎必然的确定性电流密度极限；深度与宽度同增时由形状定理确定电流；当深度发散但受宽度常数倍约束时，电流等于先宽后深极限且与正宽高比无关。存在对角线走廊时，低障碍强度下群体电流为正，高障碍强度下为零。随机需求下，上游准入队列在所有低于有限网络容量的速率下稳定。

Conclusion: 本文给出了随机森林中群体高速运输的容量刻画与调度最优性，将单鸟速度极限拓展为多鸟网络流与排队稳定性框架。

Abstract: Inspired by birds flying through cluttered environments, Karaman and Frazzoli showed that high-speed flight through an ergodic random forest exhibits an ultimate speed limit: above a critical speed, almost surely no infinite collision-free trajectory exists for a single bird. This paper formulates a corresponding bulk-transport problem for many birds. We construct an explicit directed corridor network in a Poisson disk forest and impose a common temporal headway at its waypoints. The maximum current equals a minimum vertex-cut capacity and is achieved by repeated flight along vertex-disjoint paths. The same schedule maximizes completed traversals at every finite horizon, with constant forward speed and no internal waiting. For a forest belt of fixed depth, exact transverse superadditivity yields an almost-sure deterministic current-density limit. A uniform shape theorem for an asymmetric planar dual identifies the current when depth and width grow together. For diverging depth bounded by a constant times width, this current equals the width-first, depth-second limit and is independent of the positive aspect ratio. When diagonal corridors are available, bulk current is positive at sufficiently low obstacle intensity and zero at sufficiently high intensity. For stochastic demand, an upstream admission queue is stable at every rate below the finite-network capacity.

</details>


### [9] [Fundamental Limits of Sampling-Based Reachability: Geometry and Flow Distortion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40473)
*Jixian Liu,Ihab Tabbara,Hussein Sibai,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文将基于采样的可达集端点云估计几何误差建模为流形畸变下的支撑集估计问题，并给出达到Hausdorff精度所需独立端点样本量及其匹配下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有采样可达性保证多控制漏检概率质量或相对于可达集凸包的近似误差，未充分刻画相对于真实可达集的几何误差。

Method: 在初始集合局部体积密度均匀下界、采样密度下界以及向量场单边Lipschitz常数 μ 的假设下，分析端点云的支撑估计误差；该框架允许带角初始集及可扩张或收缩动力学。

Result: 在细精度情形，使用端点云达到Hausdorff精度 r 需约 \tilde{\mathcal O}(2^n e^{n\mu T}(R/r)^n/(\rho\kappa)) 个独立端点样本；对 n≥2 且 κ 足够小，基于独立端点的所有估计器的极小极大下界在给定参数范围内匹配上述依赖，至多差维度相关因子 2·4^n 与对数因子。

Conclusion: 维度及扩张系统时域上的指数负担是该独立端点观测模型的内在限制；平面实验展示角点与收缩效应，机械臂实验显示经验精度随维度退化。

Abstract: Sampling-based reachability estimates reachable sets from finitely many simulated trajectory endpoints. Existing guarantees often control missed probability mass or approximation error relative to the convex hull of the reachable set, leaving the geometric error relative to the reachable set incompletely characterized. We study this error as a problem of support estimation under flow distortion. Our assumptions combine a uniform lower bound on the local volume density of the initial set, a lower bound on the sampling density, and a one-sided Lipschitz constant $\mu$ for the vector field. These conditions admit initial sets with corners and dynamics that may expand or contract. In the fine-accuracy regime, $\tilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(2^ne^{n\mu T}(R/r)^n/(\rho\kappa)\bigr)$ independent endpoint samples suffice for Hausdorff accuracy $r$ using the endpoint cloud. Here, $n$ is the state dimension, $T$ is the horizon, $R$ is the volume-equivalent radius, and $\rho$ and $\kappa$ quantify sampling density and local volume density. For $n\ge2$ and sufficiently small $\kappa$, a minimax lower bound over all estimators based only on independent endpoints matches these dependencies up to a dimension-dependent factor $2\cdot4^n$ and logarithmic factors, within the stated parameter range and when the state domain accommodates the construction. The exponential burden in dimension, and in the horizon for expanding systems, is therefore intrinsic to this observation model. Planar experiments illustrate the predicted effects of corners and contraction; robot-arm experiments show how empirical accuracy deteriorates with dimension.

</details>


### [10] [socx: GPU-Accelerated Chance-Constrained Stochastic Optimal Control using JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40427)
*Tanmay Dokania,Yashwanth Kumar Nakka*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出GPU加速的JAX求解器socx，用于机会约束线性随机最优控制的提升凸重构；利用问题稀疏独立结构将优化分解为可并行求解的LQT子问题与欧氏投影，并通过约束耦合，实现显著加速和长时域求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机会约束下线性随机最优控制的提升凸重构求解通常计算量大、内存需求高，现有通用求解器（如MOSEK、SCS）难以扩展到长时域问题。

Method: socx基于JAX实现GPU加速；利用问题的稀疏和独立结构，通过分布式优化将原问题归约为独立的线性二次跟踪（LQT）问题和集合上的欧氏投影，这些子问题可并行求解，再通过约束进行耦合；在悬停线性化3D四旋翼模型上验证，并进行消融实验分析关键加速组件。

Result: 相比MOSEK最高加速98倍，相比SCS最高加速2224倍；socx可求解至N=1120的时域，而MOSEK在N=320时即内存不足；消融实验隔离了带来加速的关键组成部分。

Conclusion: socx通过利用问题结构和GPU并行计算，有效提升了机会约束线性随机最优控制凸重构的求解速度与可扩展性，尤其适合长时域问题。

Abstract: In this article, we present socx, a GPU-accelerated solver for lifted convex reformulations of linear stochastic optimal control under chance constraints, implemented in JAX. We demonstrate that by exploiting the sparse and independent structure of the problem, distributed optimization can reduce it to simpler problems: independent linear-quadratic tracking (LQT) problems and Euclidean projections onto sets. These simpler problems can be solved in parallel and then coupled using the constraints. To validate our solver, we run it on a hover-linearized 3D quadrotor and show a speed-up of up to $98\times$ over MOSEK and up to $2224\times$ over SCS. socx also solves horizons up to $N=1120$, whereas MOSEK runs out of memory at $N=320$. We present ablations to isolate the key components that enable the speed-up.

</details>

### [11] [Smartphone Indoor Localization Enhanced by Hierarchical Multi-Sensor Context Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39691)
*Wenhui Feng,Muhan Weng,Jianyu Liu,Xiansheng Yang,Hao Zhang,Liang Wang,Yuan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种由层次化多传感器上下文识别增强的智能手机室内定位框架，将环境、场景和活动概率转化为场景事件与动作上下文片段，分别用于场景约束PDR校正和动作感知的RoNIN位置补偿，从而提升跨楼层与多携带模式下的定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机室内定位需低成本、鲁棒并适应场景转换和手机使用方式变化；PDR无需基础设施但受航向漂移、结构转换和携带模式变化影响而累积误差。现有方法常将上下文线索视为单一事件流或局部校正信号，难以针对不同类型的定位误差分别处理。

Method: 构建层次化多传感器上下文识别框架，采用数据与模型联合驱动模型预测环境、场景和活动概率，并转换为场景事件片段和动作上下文片段。场景事件片段通过走廊航向约束、地标吸引、固定滞后回溯和垂直转换处理实现场景约束PDR校正；动作上下文片段驱动基于RoNIN的动作感知位置补偿，以纠正携带模式相关漂移。

Result: 在跨楼层和多动作轨迹上，以LiDAR-SLAM为参考进行实验，所提框架优于PDR和代表性学习式惯性里程计基线，表明层次化语义可提供可靠的、面向定位的校正线索。

Conclusion: 层次化多传感器上下文识别能为智能手机室内定位提供定位特定的校正线索，并有效提升复杂场景转换和携带模式变化下的定位性能。

Abstract: Smartphone indoor localization requires low-cost, robust positioning across scene transitions and phone-use variations. Pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR) enables infrastructure-free deployment but accumulates errors from heading drift, structural transitions, and carrying-mode changes. Existing methods often treat contextual cues as a single event stream or local correction signal, limiting their ability to address distinct localization errors. This paper presents a framework enhanced by hierarchical multi-sensor context recognition. A jointly data- and model-driven recognition model predicts environment, scene, and activity probabilities, which are converted into scene-event and action-context segments. Scene-event segments guide scene-constrained PDR correction through corridor heading constraints, landmark attraction, fixed-lag backtracking, and vertical-transition handling. Action-context segments drive action-aware RoNIN-based position compensation for carrying-mode-dependent drift. Experiments on cross-floor and multi-action trajectories against LiDAR-SLAM references show that the proposed framework outperforms PDR and representative learned inertial odometry baselines, demonstrating that hierarchical semantics provide reliable, localization-specific correction cues.

</details>


### [12] [Smooth Action Chunking Via Movement-Primitive Representations for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39451)
*Shota Nomura,Takayuki Osa,Yasuhiro Kato,Kazuki Ota,Naoki Shitanda,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出动作分块策略生成轨迹在块内与块间存在抖动，提出用运动基元轨迹表示和推理时闭式校正来获得平滑动作块，并在ACT、DiT及真实双臂ALOHA上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习视觉机器人策略广泛采用动作分块，但扩散策略到大型VLA模型等骨干仅隐式学习时间结构，导致块内轨迹不平滑及相邻块边界不连续，在真实硬件上表现为振动和速度饱和。

Method: 将轨迹段表示为平滑基函数的线性组合，使轨迹构造上平滑，且不改变策略架构、训练目标和推理流程；同时利用闭式推理时校正，通过条件化预测系数使新动作块经过上一块最后指令动作的via-point，无需重训练，适用于单次查询策略；在单次ACT和迭代DiT骨干上实例化，并在仿真与真实双臂ALOHA平台评估。

Result: 该表示将单次骨干的块内抖动降低一个数量级，将迭代骨干降低2.4倍，任务成功率统计非劣；via-point条件化消除73%至84%的块边界不连续，且每步开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 基于运动基元的轨迹表示与推理时via-point校正可在不重训练、不牺牲成功率的情况下显著提升动作分块策略的块内与块间平滑性，并适用于单次和迭代式策略。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a widely adopted approach for vision-based robot policies, owing to its conceptual simplicity, scalability, and ability to leverage large-scale datasets. As these policies have grown more capable, action chunking has become their standard interface. At each decision step the policy outputs several future actions at once. Backbones from diffusion policies to large vision-language-action models all share it. Whatever backbone produces the chunk, its temporal structure is learned only implicitly. The commanded trajectory is therefore jerky both within a chunk and between successive chunks. On real hardware this appears as visible vibration and as repeated saturation of the follower's velocity limit. We address this issue by incorporating a movement-primitive-based trajectory representation into action chunking. Specifically, a trajectory segment is represented as a linear combination of smooth basis functions, yielding smooth trajectories by construction while leaving the policy architecture, training objective, and inference procedure unchanged. The same representation also yields a closed-form, inference-time correction for the second source of jerk. We condition the predicted coefficients so that the new chunk passes through a via-point at the previous chunk's last commanded action. This correction needs no retraining and, unlike asynchronous Real-Time Chunking, applies to policies queried in one shot. We instantiate it on a one-shot (ACT) and an iterative (DiT) backbone and evaluate in simulation and on a real bimanual ALOHA platform. The representation lowers within-chunk jerk by an order of magnitude on the one-shot backbone and by 2.4 times on the iterative one, with task success statistically non-inferior. The conditioning then removes 73-84% of the boundary discontinuity, at negligible per-step cost.

</details>


### [13] [Learning from Relative Action Credit for Escaping Local Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38034)
*Giovanni Minelli,Giulio Turrisi,Lorenzo Amatucci,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出PPO在长时程、接触密集的机器人操作任务中会因状态价值评论家缺乏动作区分能力而停滞，并提出动作条件价值评论家扩展PPO以恢复信用分配和学习信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习有望让机器人仅靠交互学习操作技能，但PPO等on-policy方法在长时程、接触密集任务中常停滞：密集奖励塑形虽能引导阶段，但阶段拼接和稀疏奖励事件发现仍难成功。作者将失败归因于状态价值信用分配缺少动作判别：状态价值评论家仅以回合结果评判进展，当策略收敛到部分解时所有结果相同，无法证明某动作更优，改进停止。

Method: 将PPO扩展为使用动作条件价值评论家（action-conditioned value critic），在同一状态下将已执行动作与采样替代动作进行比较，以增强学习信号；并结合广义优势估计（GAE）和对探索噪声的约束。

Result: 扩展算法能够逃离平台期，并在与相同探索设置的PPO失败的训练预算内完成精确peg insertion任务。

Conclusion: 动作条件价值评论家通过改善状态价值信用分配中的动作判别能力，使长时程、接触密集的机器人操作任务可在无预训练先验和示范数据条件下通过交互学习完成。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning holds the promise of robots that learn manipulation skills from interaction alone, without pretrained priors or demonstration data. In practice, on-policy methods such as PPO routinely stall on long-horizon, contact-rich tasks: dense reward shaping carries the policy through stages, yet stitching them together and discovering sparsely rewarded events rarely succeeds. We trace this failure to the missing action discrimination in state-value credit assignment: a state-value critic judges progress only by episode outcomes, so when the policy converges to a partial solution all outcomes coincide, and improvement stops because no action can prove better than another. We therefore extend PPO with an action conditioned value critic which, can compare the executed action with alternatives sampled at the same state and aid the learning signal. Combined with Generalized Advantage Estimation and a bound on exploration noise, we show how the extended algorithm escapes the plateau and completes precise peg insertion within a training budget where PPO with identical exploration fails.

</details>


### [14] [Is It the Curriculum or Just the Easy Start? Benchmarking Unsupervised Environment Design on Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40364)
*Jakub Gilewicz,Dominik Galus,Piotr Wojdas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的四足机器人地形课程学习基准，在MuJoCo MJX中比较14种无监督环境设计、域随机化和机器人课程方法，并发现不存在在所有机器人和测试集上均最优的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督环境设计方法多在2D迷宫、网格世界和BipedalWalker上比较，而足式机器人地形课程多为手工设计且仅与少量基线对比，缺乏统一、可解释且跨不同3D四足机器人的系统评测。

Method: 构建基准，在五种3D四足机器人（W01-TEK、Unitree Go1/Go2、Google Barkour vB、Boston Dynamics Spot）上以统一协议运行14种UED、域随机化和机器人课程方法；地形由八个可解释参数向量表示，所有课程以匹配环境步数预算训练同一PPO学生；评估随机与留出结构化地形成功率、分层bootstrap区间下的四分位均值、能力包络及逐关卡支配比较。

Result: 没有任何方法在所有机器人和关卡集上进入最优组；简单或低难度训练关卡的方法（无论是否精选）在Go1、Barkour及除PAIRED+BiBC外的W01-TEK分布内随机关卡上常居最优组；足式机器人实践中的成功驱动地形课程最常进入最优组，均匀关卡域随机化从未进入，PAIRED除Spot外也未进入；在三个机器人上，分布内随机关卡领先者并非留出地形领先者。

Conclusion: 四足机器人地形课程选择需按机器人分别考量，不能依赖单一最优方法；作者据此整理了逐机器人课程选择指南。

Abstract: Unsupervised environment design (UED) methods such as PLR, ACCEL, SFL and PAIRED are compared mostly on 2D mazes, grid worlds and BipedalWalker, while terrain curricula for legged robots are hand-designed and evaluated against a few baselines. We present a benchmark that runs fourteen UED, domain-randomisation and robotics curricula under one protocol on five 3D quadrupeds in MuJoCo MJX: the closed-chain W01-TEK and the open-chain Unitree Go1 and Go2, Google Barkour vB and Boston Dynamics Spot. Levels are vectors of eight interpretable terrain parameters, and all curricula drive the same PPO student at matched environment-step budgets. The evaluation reports success on random and held-out structured terrains, interquartile means with stratified bootstrap intervals, capability envelopes and a level-by-level domination comparison. We compare methods within each robot and report, per level set, the top group of methods not significantly below the best. No method is in the top group on every robot and level set. Methods with easy or low-difficulty training levels, curated or not, fill the top groups on in-distribution random levels of Go1 and Barkour, and of W01-TEK apart from PAIRED+BiBC. The success-driven terrain curriculum from legged-robot practice is in a top group most often, domain randomisation on uniform levels in none, and PAIRED in none outside Spot, where the fourteen methods lie within 3.8 points on random levels of difficulty 0.25. On three robots the leader on in-distribution random levels is not the leader on held-out terrain. We collect the results into a per-robot guide to choosing a curriculum.

</details>


### [15] [Learned Terminal Costs for Short-Horizon Model Predictive Control of Autonomous Trucks in Interactive Highway Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40257)
*Hooman Hamze,Benjamin Houshmand,Deepthi Pathare,Erik Börve,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Nikolce Murgovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种MPC与强化学习混合框架，用预训练RL模型估计有限时域MPC的终端代价，用于重型卡车高速自动驾驶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型卡车高速自动驾驶需在安全约束与长时域高效决策间平衡；纯MPC受短预测时域和终端代价设计限制，纯RL缺乏约束保证且稀疏碰撞惩罚下易过于保守。

Method: 构建有限时域MPC并由动态规划求解，同时使用预训练RL模型估计终端代价；在含反应性周围交通的仿真中，与零终端代价纯MPC、基于基策略rollout估计终端代价及纯RL进行对比。

Result: 混合方法实现零碰撞，并在所有对比配置中取得最低成本。

Conclusion: MPC与RL混合框架结合了MPC的约束满足能力和RL的长时域决策优势，可提升重型卡车高速自动驾驶的安全性与效率。

Abstract: Autonomous highway driving for heavy-duty trucks is a challenging control problem. The greater mass, dimensions and intricate dynamics increase the requirements for cooperation from surrounding traffic in critical maneuvers, requiring a careful balance between safety constraints and efficient long-horizon decision making. Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides rigorous constraint satisfaction but relies on terminal cost which can limit its effectiveness at short, computationally affordable horizons. Reinforcement Learning (RL) captures long-term driving behavior but offers no constraint guarantees and tends toward overly conservative policies when trained under sparse collision penalties. In this paper, we propose a hybrid MPC and RL framework for highway driving in autonomous trucks, where a finite-horizon MPC, solved via dynamic programming, uses a pre-trained RL model for terminal cost estimation. We evaluate the framework in a simulation environment with reactive surrounding traffic and compare it against pure MPC with zero terminal cost, rollout with terminal cost estimation using a base policy, and pure RL. Results show that the hybrid approach achieves zero collisions with the lowest cost of any configuration.

</details>


### [16] [Coupled Guidance for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40109)
*Mateo Clémente,Léo Maxime Brunswic,Rui Heng Yang,Amir Rasouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出将扩散模型中的引导方法直接迁移到流匹配模型会破坏训练时的耦合分布，导致采样控制不稳定；作者从几何角度提出面向流匹配的 Coupled Guidance，在机器人控制与图像生成任务上提升样本质量和引导稳定性且不增加额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导方法对扩散模型生成控制很重要，但流匹配与扩散虽相似，现有流匹配引导多为扩散引导的直接改编，存在不稳定性，且缺乏对流匹配中引导机制的理论理解。

Method: 作者对流匹配中的引导进行理论分析，发现朴素地将扩散引导迁移到流匹配会破坏训练阶段使用的耦合分布，从而削弱采样精度控制；据此提出 Coupled Guidance，一种基于几何视角、利用流匹配训练固有耦合结构的流匹配专用引导机制。

Result: 在 ManiSkill3 自定义机器人避碰任务、Adroit 基准的四项机器人灵巧操作任务，以及 ImageNet-128 和 CelebA-HQ-256 上的图像修复、超分辨率和去模糊三项图像生成任务中，该方法均提升了样本质量和引导稳定性，且不引入额外开销。

Conclusion: Coupled Guidance 为流匹配提供了更合适的引导机制，避免直接套用扩散引导带来的耦合分布破坏问题，在多种机器人与图像生成任务上验证了有效性和高效性。

Abstract: Guidance methods are essential tools for steering the generation process in diffusion models, improving the generalizability of stochastic robotic controllers and enabling high-quality controlled image synthesis. Despite strong parallels between flow-matching and diffusion, integrating guidance into flow-matching models remains quite challenging. Recent work has proposed general guidance frameworks for flow-matching, however, they are direct adaptations of diffusion guidance methods and can prove unstable. In this work, we conduct a theoretical analysis of guidance in flow-matching and show that a naive translation of diffusion-based guidance to flow-matching breaks the coupling distribution used during training, resulting in weaker control over sampling accuracy. Building on this insight, we propose Coupled Guidance, a flow-matching-specific guidance mechanism derived from a geometric perspective that leverages the coupling structure inherent to flow-matching training. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we conduct extensive experimentation on a custom robotic collision avoidance task using ManiSkill3, four robotic dexterous manipulation tasks from the Adroit benchmark, and three image generation tasks, namely inpainting, super-resolution, and deblurring, using the ImageNet-128 and CelebA-HQ-256 datasets. Our findings show that our framework improves sample quality and guidance stability across all tasks, without imposing any additional overhead.

</details>

### [17] [Where Planning Changes the Outcome of Orbital Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37686)
*Duncan Eddy,Mahdi Al-Husseini,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出基于JAX的OrbitalGym仿真环境，用于研究在对抗性轨道交会中，规划算法与系统能力分别何时决定结果，并在追逃与“lady-bandit-guard”两类近距博弈中比较多种控制/规划策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道博弈研究通常只在单一场景中评估一种方法，且对手常为被动或随机防御者，因此尚不清楚在考虑机动能力、感知、通信和对抗响应后，更好的规划是否仍然重要。

Method: 构建OrbitalGym，支持多智能体、位置/速度/姿态/燃料状态表示、显式或隐式通信、近距或锥形视场传感器，并将博弈编译为批处理GPU代码以大规模并行评估策略。研究追逃与lady-bandit-guard两类博弈，比较PD、LQR、模型预测路径积分控制和蒙特卡洛树搜索，并扫描推力/推进剂比例、初始间距、博弈时长、传感器能力与捕获定义等配置。

Result: 追逃中推进剂比例主导大多数遭遇：对6种逃逸策略中的5种，追击策略使捕获概率变化达到10%以上的配置最多为90个能力配置中的15个。lady-bandit-guard中策略更重要：面对能够到达受保护资产的bandit，更好的guard策略在90个配置中的56至74个里将防御概率提高至少10%。

Conclusion: OrbitalGym识别出策略选择影响结果的区间与系统能力主导的区间；规划在部分博弈和配置中显著重要，但其作用依赖于车辆能力、对抗响应和任务设置。

Abstract: This paper introduces OrbitalGym, a simulation environment for studying when planning algorithms or system design determine the outcome of adversarial orbital encounters. In an orbital game, two non-cooperative teams of satellites maneuver during rendezvous and proximity operations to win a differential-reward objective. Advances in terrestrial game playing have prompted many methods for orbital games, but studies typically evaluate one method in one scenario, often against a passive or random defender. It is unclear whether better planning matters once vehicle capability (maneuverability, sensing, communications) and adversarial responses are considered. OrbitalGym is a JAX-based environment supporting varying numbers of agents, state representations (position, velocity, attitude, fuel), explicit or implicit communication, and proximity or conical field-of-view sensors. It compiles games to batched GPU code, enabling large-scale parallel simulation of policies across many configurations. This identifies regimes where policy choice affects outcomes and regimes where system capability dominates. We study two close-range games: pursuit-evasion, where one spacecraft tries to capture another, and lady-bandit-guard, where a guard prevents an attacker from reaching a protected asset. We compare PD, LQR, model predictive path integral control, and Monte Carlo tree search over ratios of thrust and propellant, initial separation, game duration, sensor capability, and the definition of capture. In pursuit-evasion the propellant ratio decides most encounters: against five of six evader policies, pursuer policy changes capture probability by 10 percent or more in at most 15 of 90 capability configurations. In lady-bandit-guard, policy matters more: against bandits able to reach the asset, a better guard policy raises defense probability by at least 10 percent in 56 to 74 of 90 configurations.

</details>

### [18] [DexSuite: An All-in-One Framework for Dexterous Manipulation Across Arms, Hands, and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37799)
*Anas Houssaini,Hugo He,Junming Shi,Amin Abyaneh,Ricardo Chahine,Shuo Wen,Mohamad H. Danesh,Charles-Étienne Fréchette,Mariana Sosa Guzman,Mathias Desrochers,Rayan Houssaini,Adam Landa,Theophile Soulie,Charlotte Morissette,Annie Tims,Martino Russi,Jonathan Lussier,Gregory Dudek,Doina Precup,Julen Urain,Hsiu-Chin Lin,David Meger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexSuite 是一个基于 Genesis 仿真器的全流程灵巧操作仿真框架，覆盖机器人搭建、示教数据采集与策略评估，并开源发布任务、基准和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作仍是机器人领域的核心开放问题，现有仿真框架很少覆盖从机器人配置到灵巧示教采集再到策略评估的完整工作流。

Method: DexSuite 提供30个任务，将10种机械臂与12种末端执行器组合为100余种单臂机器人和数千种双臂组合；提供标准化环境API、集成遥操作和仿真触觉感知；定义涵盖不同接触复杂度与任务时域的16个任务-本体对基准；发布4,700条成功仿真回合数据集，来源包括人类遥操作、脚本专家和学习策略。

Result: 作者开源了 DexSuite，并发布16个任务-本体对基准及4,700条成功回合数据集，同时提供视频和数据集链接。

Conclusion: DexSuite 旨在降低灵巧操作研究的仿真与评测门槛，为统一环境、示教数据和基准评估提供基础设施，并希望社区在此基础上继续构建。

Abstract: Human-level dexterity remains a central open problem in robotics, yet few simulation frameworks cover the full workflow of dexterous manipulation, from setting up a robot to collecting dexterous demonstrations and evaluating policies. We present DexSuite, an all-in-one simulation framework for dexterous manipulation built on the Genesis simulator. DexSuite provides 30 tasks and assembles its 10 arms and 12 end-effectors, from parallel-jaw grippers to dexterous hands, into more than 100 single-arm robots and thousands of bimanual pairs. It also ships a standardized environment API, integrated teleoperation, and simulated tactile sensing. Moreover, we define a benchmark of 16 task-embodiment pairs spanning diverse contact complexities and horizons, and release a dataset of 4,700 successful episodes in simulation from human teleoperation, scripted experts, and learned policies. We release DexSuite as open source and hope others will build on it. Videos and datasets are available at this https URL

</details>


### [19] [SwapBot: Tool-Centric Robot Manipulation via Interchangeable End-Effectors and Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40646)
*Yunchu Zhang,Ray Song,Junhan Jiao,Liyiming Ke,Yuchen Yang,Ruihan Gao,Emma Romig,Siddhartha Srinivasa,Yiyue Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SwapBot提出一种模块化、以工具为中心的低成本机器人末端执行器系统，通过统一耦合接口与带触觉的工具适配器实现家用工具的快速自主更换，并结合示教学习完成富接触操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常机器人操作大量依赖合适工具，而单一通用夹爪难以覆盖 forceful cutting、delicate brushing、precise placement 等差异显著的富接触技能，因此需要能够自主更换并有效使用任务适配工具的系统。

Method: SwapBot采用固定机器人侧耦合接口加低成本工具侧适配器的统一设计，兼容多种工具（包括 articulated tools）和多机器人平台；每个适配器集成紧凑触觉传感器，支持快速自主 swapping，并接入直观遥操作系统以高效采集示教数据，再用模仿学习训练策略。

Result: 用户研究表明遥操作时SwapBot降低任务完成时间和操作员 workload；基于采集示教训练的模仿学习策略可完成煎豆腐、拌沙拉、翻肉等工具中心任务；消融显示触觉增强示教使富接触技能成功率较仅RGB策略提高15至25个百分点。

Conclusion: 模块化且配备触觉的工具系统是通向稳健、强能力机器人操作的一种实用且可扩展路径。

Abstract: Robot manipulation in everyday environments inherently involves substantial tool usage. Seemingly simple household tasks, such as cooking pan-fried tofu, require forceful cutting, delicate brushing, and precise placement using different tools under contact-rich interaction. Thus, the ability to autonomously swap and use task-appropriate tools is important for tool-centric manipulation. We introduce SwapBot, a modular, tool-centric end effector system featuring low-cost adapters that quickly convert common household tools into robot end effectors. Rather than pursuing a single universal gripper, SwapBot adopts a unified design principle that combines a fixed robot-side coupling interface with low-cost, tool-side adapters, enabling compatibility with diverse tools, including articulated ones, and multiple robot platforms. Each adapter integrates compact tactile sensors, supports fast autonomous swapping, and interfaces with an intuitive teleoperation system for efficient demonstration collection. User studies show that, during teleoperation, SwapBot reduces task completion time and operator workload. Using collected demonstrations on the robot, we train imitation learning policies to successfully perform tool-centric tasks like cooking tofu, stirring salad, flipping meat. Our ablation study shows that using tactile-augmented demonstrations improves policy performance across contact-rich skills, increasing success rates by 15 to 25 percentage points over RGB-only policies. These results highlight modular, tactile-equipped tools as a practical and extensible pathway toward robust and capable robot manipulation. Project website this https URL .

</details>


### [20] [SymAnt: Grounding Long-Horizon Action Anticipation with Adaptable Symbolic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40632)
*Fnu Aryan,Yaqi Xie,Joseph Campbell,Woojun Kim,Kwonjoon Lee,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Ehsan Moradi-Pari,Katia Sycara,Simon Stepputtis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SymAnt 是一种神经符号方法，通过联合搜索场景图与知识图构建符号上下文，并将其作为先验迭代细化未来动作序列，从而仅凭短时视频观测预测长时程动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与智能系统需在与人类交互中根据简短观测预判较长未来动作序列；纯神经网络通常依赖大量观测，且难以解释和调整，符号先验提供了结构化替代方案。

Method: 给定短视频，SymAnt 对场景图和知识图进行联合搜索以构建符号上下文，统一推理空间关系、物体属性、可供性与相关动作；该符号上下文作为先验驱动迭代细化过程，逐步生成并改进未来动作序列，并以参数高效方式将预测锚定于符号上下文，同时允许用户直接编辑知识以调整预测而无需再训练。

Result: 在 Breakfast、50 Salads、EPIC-KITCHENS、EGTEA Gaze+ 和 Assembly101 五个多样化数据集上的实验表明 SymAnt 有效，尤其在仅有短观测上下文时表现突出。

Conclusion: SymAnt 将神经网络的表达能力与场景图和知识图的结构化推理相结合，实现了短观测下的长时程动作预测，兼具参数高效、可解释和可通过知识编辑调整预测的特点。

Abstract: As robots and intelligent systems increasingly interact with humans, they must anticipate long sequences of future actions from only brief observations. While purely neural models typically require extensive observations to predict future actions and remain difficult to interpret and adjust, symbolic priors offer a structured alternative. To this end, we introduce SymAnt, a novel neuro-symbolic approach that unifies the expressiveness of neural networks with the structured reasoning of scene and knowledge graphs. Given a short video, SymAnt performs a joint-search over both graphs to construct a symbolic context, enabling unified reasoning over spatial relationships, object attributes, affordances, and associated actions. This symbolic context serves as a prior for an iterative refinement process that progressively generates and improves the sequence of future actions. By grounding predictions in symbolic context, SymAnt anticipates long-horizon actions from short observation windows in a parameter-efficient manner, while enabling users to directly edit the underlying knowledge to adjust predictions without further training. Through our experiments, we demonstrate the effectiveness of SymAnt, particularly when only short observation contexts are available, across five diverse datasets: Breakfast, 50 Salads, EPIC-KITCHENS, EGTEA Gaze+, and Assembly101.

</details>


### [21] [A Conveyor Belt Mechanism for Repositionable Magnetic Coupling Between Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40600)
*Ella Walsh,Eric Diller,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可集成于9 mm直径腱驱动连续机器人内的传送带机构，可连续、主动重定位功能元件，并以磁耦合为案例验证可按需调节多机器人系统有效刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为适应任务需求变化，需要在不更换机器人本体的情况下主动调整功能元件位置，并实现对多机器人耦合位置、数量及系统刚度的在线调节。

Method: 设计新型传送带机构，最多三条集成于9 mm直径连续机器人；对比传送带驱动力与机器人腱驱动力；在两条传送带上安装永磁体，构建可主动重构的机器人间磁耦合，并在运行中调整耦合点位置与数量以调节有效刚度。

Result: 传送带驱动力与腱驱动力相当或更低；耦合系统有效刚度随耦合位置呈可预测的反立方关系，并随耦合点数量增加而增大。

Conclusion: 传送带机构能够实现功能元件的主动重定位以适应变化任务需求，磁互耦合是可重构功能集成的概念验证。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel conveyor belt mechanism enabling continuous, active repositioning of functional elements along the body of a tendon-driven continuum robot. Up to three conveyor belt mechanisms are integrated within a 9 mm diameter continuum robot. We demonstrate equivalent or reduced conveyor belt actuation forces compared to robot tendon actuation forces. As a case study, we mount permanent magnets along two conveyor belts, and demonstrate actively reconfigurable inter-robot coupling. Coupling point location and quantity can be adjusted during robot operation to actively tune the effective stiffness of the coupled multi-robot system. We show that effective stiffness follows a predictable inverse-cubic relationship with coupling location, and increases with the number of coupling points. These findings demonstrate that the conveyor belt mechanism enables active functional element repositioning for changing tasks requirements, with magnetic inter-robot coupling as one proof-of-concept.

</details>


### [22] [Gym-Anything for Robotics: Scaling physical simulations with digital agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40565)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Gym-Anything for Robotics，用编码智能体自动构建真实、可扩展的机器人仿真，并基于经济价值筛选任务，构建 RoboWorld 基准；前沿模型在该基准上表现极差，但多种新训练与改进方法可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人数据与仿真覆盖不足，现有仿真仅涵盖有限任务和物理现象；核心瓶颈是高质量仿真依赖大量专家人工构建，难以规模化。作者认为若把仿真创建视为编码智能体任务，则编码智能体能力提升可直接转化为更丰富、更真实的仿真。

Method: Gym-Anything for Robotics 框架让编码智能体创建机器人仿真；依据美国 GDP 数据选择具经济价值物理工作，定义“好仿真”和“好任务”，并设计对抗式创建-审计循环逐项检查。由此构建 RoboWorld：25 个仿真、25 个测试和 175 个训练 RL 环境，难度不同。进一步提出多种前沿模型改进方式：重复尝试的上下文学习、读取仿真器状态的特权教师在线指导学生、从自身特权经验自辅助学习，以及用代码策略在多样场景生成示范以训练如 π0.5 的 VLA 模型。

Result: 即使最强前沿模型在 RoboWorld 所有测试任务上均失败，平均奖励仅 0.06；上述上下文学习、特权教师、自辅助学习和代码策略生成示范等方法均带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 仿真创建可作为编码智能体任务并随编码智能体进步而扩展；RoboWorld 暴露了前沿模型在真实机器人仿真任务上的巨大差距，同时提供了可改进路径。作者发布流水线、RL 环境和基准以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Realistic, fast robotics simulations could enable large-scale training of embodied AI models that transfer to the real world. However, despite decades of progress, available simulations cover only a narrow range of real-world tasks and physical phenomena. The core bottleneck is that creating better simulations requires significant expert effort and therefore does not scale. To address this, we introduce Gym-Anything for Robotics, a framework in which coding agents create realistic robotics simulations. Our core insight is that simulation creation is a coding agent task, so improvements in coding agents translate directly into richer and more faithful simulations. We select simulations based on U.S. GDP data to cover economically valuable physical work, define what makes a good simulation and a good task, and develop an adversarial creation-audit loop that checks each simulation and task against these definitions. Using this pipeline, we build RoboWorld, a set of 25 simulations with 25 test and 175 training RL environments of varying difficulty. Even the best frontier models fail on every RoboWorld test task, achieving an average reward of only 0.06. Building on RoboWorld, we introduce several new ways for frontier models to improve, including in-context learning across repeated attempts, a privileged teacher that reads the simulator state and teaches a student in context, self-assisted learning from the model's own privileged experience, and code policies that generate demonstrations across varied scenes to train vision-language-action models (VLAs) such as $\pi_{0.5}$. Each of these substantially improves performance. We release our pipeline, RL environments, and benchmark to facilitate future research. Code and data are available at this https URL

</details>


### [23] [A Tutorial on Shape Servoing of Deformable Objects-Part I: Fundamentals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40559)
*Ignacio Cuiral-Zueco,Alessio Caporali,Miguel Aranda,Gianluca Palli,Gonzalo López-Nicolás,Youcef Mezouar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文是两篇教程的第一部分，系统介绍基于数据驱动变形雅可比的可变形物体形状控制（形状伺服），包括问题建模、形状表示、雅可比估计与在线更新、标准控制律、闭环稳定性条件及不可达目标形状的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形物体的变形雅可比依赖材料属性、几何形状和边界条件（如抓取位置），这些因素通常难以精确建模，因此需要不依赖精细力学模型、可从机器人运动引起的形状变化中局部估计雅可比的数据驱动方法。

Method: 文章建立形状控制问题形式化，介绍形状表示方法，说明如何从观测到的形状变化估计并在线更新变形雅可比，采用文献中的标准控制律，并将这些要素集成为完整的形状伺服闭环；随后推导该闭环的局部稳定性条件，并分析部分目标形状不可达的原因。全文以两个机械臂操作弹性杆、相机观测的仿真平面示例说明概念。

Result: 给出了完整的数据驱动雅可比形状伺服闭环框架及局部稳定性条件，并解释了某些目标形状无法达到的原因；通过仿真平面示例展示了相关概念。

Conclusion: 该教程第一部分奠定了基于数据驱动雅可比的可变形物体形状控制基础，第二部分将讨论更高级的主题。

Abstract: In robotics, shape control (or shape servoing) refers to the automatic manipulation of a deformable object to achieve a desired shape. It involves a feedback loop that exploits the relation between robot motion and changes in the measured shape. This relation, known as the interaction Jacobian or, in this context, the deformation Jacobian, depends on the object's mechanical properties, geometry, and boundary conditions (e.g., where the object is grasped). Because these factors are often difficult to model accurately, the deformation Jacobian can be estimated locally from observed shape changes produced by commanded robot motions, without requiring a fine-tuned mechanical model. This article is the first of a two-part tutorial on data-driven Jacobian-based shape control of deformable objects. It introduces the central elements of the approach: the formulation of the shape-control problem, shape representation, estimation and online update of the deformation Jacobian, and a standard control law from the literature. It then assembles these elements into a complete shape-servoing loop, derives a local stability condition for this loop, and illustrates why some target shapes cannot be reached. Part II covers more advanced topics. Throughout both parts, a simulated planar example in which two robot arms manipulate an elastic rod observed by a camera illustrates the concepts.

</details>


### [24] [PhysVLMM: Interaction-Conditioned Physical Belief Maps for Articulated Objects in Robot Scene Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40539)
*Dibyendu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVLMM 为视觉-语言-运动地图中的每个铰接部件维护物理信念，并通过地图更新与查询算法回答规划器关于拉动响应的风险感知查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人需要开门和抽屉，但现有场景地图只记录物体类别与是否移动，不能预测其对拉动的动态响应；且仅观察运动无法辨识动力学，单次测量只能识别被拉部件的一个方向。

Method: PhysVLMM 对每个铰接部件维护物理信念：将被拉部件的未知方向保留为后验而非点估计；以闭式形式把每次拉动的证据传播到未触碰的相似部件；并回答动作在给定风险下是否成功或选择弃权。

Result: 在18个真实家庭的门和抽屉上，留出拉动中关节类型识别率86%（单视图学习法48%）；拉动响应预测误差比可微仿真低41%（CRPS 0.323 vs 0.544），速度快四个数量级（13 ms vs 230 s/例）；后验将最终位置误差从0.460降至0.270，证据共享将未触碰部件力误差从56%降至42%；在0.02至0.30各风险水平下，错误安全回答均未超出给定风险；汇总家庭内所有测量部件与相似部件分组效果相当，简单变体预测被拉部件停止位置至少不差。

Conclusion: PhysVLMM 表明，在地图中显式维护并共享铰接部件的物理后验，可在真实家居环境中实现快速、风险可控且优于可微仿真的拉动响应预测。

Abstract: Mobile robots must open doors and drawers, yet their scene maps record what each object is and whether it has moved, not how it will respond to a pull. This paper proposes PhysVLMM, a method that maintains a physical belief for every articulated part of a Vision-Language-Motion Map and answers a planner's queries about it, with two algorithms: a map update and a query. Watching a part move provably cannot identify its dynamics, and a measured pull identifies only the pulled part, up to one direction. PhysVLMM therefore (i) keeps that direction as a posterior instead of a point estimate, (ii) passes the evidence of each pull in closed form to the untouched look-alike parts of the map, and (iii) answers whether an action will succeed at a stated risk, or abstains. On real doors and drawers in 18 homes, it identifies the joint type in 86% of held-out pulls (48% for a learned single-view method) and predicts the response to a pull with 41% lower error than differentiable simulation (CRPS 0.323 vs. 0.544), four orders of magnitude faster (13 ms vs. 230 s per case). The posterior lowers the final-position error from 0.460 to 0.270, sharing lowers the force error of untouched parts from 56% to 42%, and at every stated risk from 0.02 to 0.30 the false-safe answers stay within it. Pooling all measured parts of a home does as well as the look-alike groups, and simpler variants predict where a pulled part stops at least as well.

</details>


### [25] [DAWN: Diffusion-based Alignment and unfastening Workflow Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40537)
*Hemant Kumar,Piyush Goenka,Nikhil Chopra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DAWN 是一个基于扩散策略的分层机器人框架，通过多模态感知完成真实世界中的拧松/拆卸任务，并将流程分解为对准插入与拧松两个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人拧松对回收、安全与成本至关重要，但因螺丝刀精确啮合、力控执行和环境变化鲁棒性等挑战仍主要依赖人工；长时程、接触丰富操作学习困难。

Method: 提出 DAWN，包含 AlignNet（基于扩散的策略实现螺丝刀尖端精确啮合）、LatchNet（多模态分类器检测阶段转换是否成功）和 UnfastNet（实现稳定力控螺丝移除）；在 Franka Emika 机器人上验证，并进行输入模态与训练策略消融。

Result: 对准与插入成功率可与熟练人工操作员相当，阶段转换稳健，并在多种未见场景中取得较高的拧松成功率；消融实验揭示了关键输入模态和有效训练策略，体现可扩展性、鲁棒性和数据效率。

Conclusion: DAWN 通过分层多模态扩散策略有效应对接触丰富的真实世界拧松任务，为自动化拆卸与回收流程提供了可行且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: We present DAWN (Diffusion-based Alignment and unfastening Workflow Network), a hierarchical robotic framework designed to perform real-world, unfastening tasks through multi-modal sensory integration. Autonomous robotic unfastening is crucial for improving recycling processes, enhancing safety, and reducing operational costs; yet, it remains predominantly manual due to inherent challenges in precise screwdriver engagement with fasteners, detailed force execution, and robustness to environmental variability. To address the complexity of learning long-horizon, contact-rich manipulation tasks, we subdivide the unfastening task procedure into two sequential stages: (1) alignment & insertion, and (2) unfastening. DAWN consists of three key components: AlignNet, a diffusion-based policy ensuring precise screwdriver tip engagement; LatchNet, a multi-modal classifier reliably detecting successful stage transitions; and UnfastNet, which achieves stable, force-controlled screw removal. Experimental validation on a Franka Emika robot demonstrates that DAWN achieves alignment & insertion success rates comparable to trained human operators, robustly transitions between stages, and attains an impressive success rate in unfastening across various unseen scenarios. Ablation studies further elucidate critical input modalities and effective training strategies, highlighting DAWN's scalability, robustness, and data efficiency.

</details>


### [26] [BURGER: Benchmarking and Understanding Robotic Generalization via Embodied Drawing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40532)
*Xiangyu Xu,Bangshuai Peng,Ziyi Liu,Mengjie Zhao,Chenhao Lu,Zhengyang Fan,Huazhe Xu,Kaizhe Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 BURGER——语言条件机器人绘画基准与可扩展的提示驱动轨迹生成流程，用 28,200 个仿真演示和 282 个任务系统评估五类 VLA 模型在数据规模、语言 grounding 与多模态行为上的表现，并迁移到真实 Franka Panda 机械臂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 VLA 模型能否随训练数据扩展有效学习多样语言条件行为，需要大规模、多样化机器人数据，但真实机器人数据采集成本高，因此需要可扩展且可分析的基准。

Method: 构建 BURGER 语言条件绘画基准：智能体从同一空白画布开始，控制机械臂按语言指令绘制指定物体或空间构图；每条指令允许多种有效绘画结果与笔画顺序。提供提示驱动的连续绘画轨迹生成管线，支持仿真与真实世界，并基于 28,200 个仿真演示、282 个绘画任务评估五个代表性 VLA 模型。

Result: 实验从数据规模、语言 grounding 和多模态行为等维度进行细粒度分析，揭示不同 VLA 模型的失败模式；部分模型可从更高任务多样性与演示规模中受益，能捕获多模态绘画行为，并泛化到熟悉物体的未见组合。

Conclusion: BURGER 可作为分析 VLA 模型语言 grounding、空间构图与动作生成的绘画基准；训练策略可部署到 Franka Panda 机械臂，并在人类干预下实现反应式绘画。

Abstract: How effectively can vision-language-action (VLA) models learn diverse language-conditioned behaviors as training data scales? Investigating this question requires large and varied robot datasets, whose collection on physical robots remains costly. We introduce BURGER, a language-conditioned robotic drawing benchmark with a scalable, prompt-driven pipeline for generating continuous drawing trajectories, both in simulation and the real world. In BURGER, an agent starts from the same blank canvas and controls a robot arm to draw the object or spatial composition specified by language, while each instruction allows an open-ended variety of valid drawings and stroke orders. Using 28,200 simulated demonstrations spanning 282 drawing tasks, we evaluate five representative VLA models across data scales, language grounding, and multimodal behavior. BURGER supports fine-grained analysis of language grounding, spatial composition, and action generation, revealing distinct failure patterns across VLA models. Our experiments suggest that some VLA models can benefit from greater task diversity and demonstration scale, capture multimodal drawing behaviors, and generalize to unseen compositions of familiar objects. Finally, we deploy a trained policy on a Franka Panda arm, demonstrating the ability to draw reactively under human intervention. Project homepage and code can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [27] [A Two-Wheeled Quadrotor with All-Clockwise Tilted Rotors for Level-Attitude Surface Locomotnion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40502)
*Takumi Ito,Satoshi Nakano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种全顺时针倾斜旋翼的轮式四旋翼，通过地面接触约束在不倾斜机体的情况下产生沿接触面的推进力，实现空中悬停与地面水平运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统轮式四旋翼在横向运动时需倾斜机体以产生侧向力，难以在横向运动过程中保持期望姿态，限制其应用。

Method: 设计全顺时针倾斜旋翼的轮式四旋翼，利用表面接触作为约束生成沿接触面的推进力；分析接触条件下的可行力与系统可控性，并针对不同旋翼旋转配置优化飞行器设计；通过仿真验证自由空间悬停与地面水平运动。

Result: 优化结果表明，四旋翼全部顺时针旋转的配置比传统交替旋转配置能产生更大的切向力；仿真显示该飞行器可在保持水平姿态的同时实现自由空间悬停和地面水平运动。

Conclusion: 该方法无需额外旋翼倾转执行器或机构，即可兼顾空中飞行与接触式地面运动，为轮式四旋翼在保持机体姿态下的横向运动提供了新方案。

Abstract: Conventional wheel-equipped quadrotor UAVs must tilt their bodies to generate lateral forces, limiting their ability to maintain a desired attitude during lateral motion. This paper proposes a wheeled quadrotor with all-clockwise tilted rotors that uses surface contact as a constraint to generate propulsion along the contact surface without tilting the vehicle. We analyze feasible forces and system controllability under surface contact and optimize the vehicle design considering different rotor-spin configurations. The optimization results show that a configuration in which all four rotors spin clockwise can generate a larger tangential force than the conventional alternating-spin configuration. This approach enables both aerial and contact-based locomotion without additional rotor-tilting actuators or mechanisms. Simulation results demonstrate both hovering in free space and horizontal locomotion on a floor while maintaining a level attitude.

</details>


### [28] [Adaptive search with path-constrained mobile sensors using multi-arm bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40431)
*Abraham P. Vinod,Parth K. Thaker,Gautam Dasarathy,Stefano Di Cairano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究能量受限移动传感器团队在噪声在线测量下检测感兴趣区域的问题，将主动路径选择建模为多臂老虎机，提出基于置信界的自适应搜索算法和图基低基数路径覆盖构造，并给出概率正确性与近最优时间界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器受移动能力、能量约束（需返回充电站）和避障限制，需在可行路径集合中高效选择路径，以尽快检测所有感兴趣区域并标注节点。

Method: 将主动路径选择转化为多臂老虎机问题，提出迭代搜索算法，利用已收集数据计算置信界并自适应分配路径；同时提出基于图的方法，在团队约束下构造低基数路径覆盖。

Result: 建立有限时间和任意时间概率正确性保证，推导检测所有感兴趣区域及标注所有节点所需时间的非渐近上界，并证明在广泛问题实例上这些上界与算法无关下界仅差常数因子；仿真展示路径冗余和团队规模对搜索性能的影响。

Conclusion: 所提方法在复杂约束下实现理论近最优的自适应搜索，路径冗余与团队规模是影响性能的重要因素。

Abstract: We study a constrained sequential decision problem for detecting regions of interest from noisy online measurements collected by a team of energy-constrained mobile sensors. The sensor motion is restricted to a precomputed set of feasible paths that satisfy mobility constraints, limited available energy by requiring return to charging stations, and avoidance of known obstacles. We cast the resulting active path selection as a multi-armed bandit problem and propose an iterative search algorithm that adaptively assigns paths using confidence bounds computed from the collected data. We also propose a graph-based method to construct low-cardinality path covers under the team's constraints. We establish finite-time and anytime probabilistic correctness guarantees for the proposed algorithm, derive non-asymptotic upper bounds on the times required to detect all regions of interest and to label all nodes, and show that these bounds match algorithm-independent lower bounds up to constant factors for a broad class of problem instances. Using numerical simulations, we illustrate the behavior of the proposed approach and the effect of path redundancy and team size on search performance.

</details>


### [29] [Improving Fine-Grained Manipulation through Demonstration Expansion and Execution Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40430)
*Jingxu Gu,Dongze Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEED 通过演示扩展与执行设计，将预训练视觉-语言-动作策略适配到五个 MetaFine 细粒度操作任务，官方 Overall 得分为 85.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度操作要求机器人策略能够遵循指令、对视觉变化保持鲁棒，并将预测动作转化为精确的机器人控制，因此需要有效的适配与执行方案。

Method: DEED 针对同一初始场景为不同指令生成演示，并在不同相机位置和光照条件下渲染同一物理轨迹，同时保持原始状态与动作目标；采用分层采样策略在监督适配中平衡指令、轨迹和视觉条件；部署时将预测动作块通过逆运动学、任务特定夹爪映射和闭环重规划转换为机器人命令。

Result: DEED 获得官方 Overall 85.55% 的分数；固定策略评估表明，性能会随执行时域、去噪预算和夹爪映射而变化。

Conclusion: DEED 通过结构化演示扩展和执行设计，能够有效将预训练 VLA 策略适配到细粒度机器人操作任务，并取得具有竞争力的官方评测结果。

Abstract: Fine-grained manipulation requires a robot policy to follow instructions, remain robust to visual variations, and translate predicted actions into precise robot control. We present Demonstration Expansion and Execution Design (DEED), a framework for adapting a pretrained vision-language-action policy to the five MetaFine tasks through structured demonstration expansion and execution design. DEED generates demonstrations for different instructions from the same initial scene and renders each physical trajectory under different camera positions and lighting conditions while preserving its original states and action targets. A hierarchical sampling strategy balances instructions, trajectories, and visual conditions during supervised adaptation. At deployment, DEED converts predicted action chunks into robot commands using inverse kinematics, gripper mappings selected for each task, and closed-loop replanning. DEED achieves an official Overall score of 85.55%. Fixed-policy evaluations show that performance varies with execution horizon, denoising budget, and gripper mapping.

</details>


### [30] [Optimal Path Planning with Localization constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40429)
*Chinmay Mundane,Manav Mishra,PB Sujit*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究GPS拒止环境下无人机路径规划，提出一种将定位不确定性作为硬约束的A*规划器，在保证协方差不超过阈值的前提下寻找最短可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在无GPS区域易丢失定位，需依赖航位推算并偶尔经过蜂窝基站作为地标来重置和修正位置；因此路径规划必须同时考虑定位不确定性约束。

Method: 提出定位约束A*规划器，将不确定性作为搜索中的硬约束；使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）传播机器人状态与协方差，仅考虑满足协方差上限的路径；并证明该方法不影响A*启发式，保持可采纳性与一致性，从而在可行路径中保证最优性。

Result: 大量蒙特卡洛仿真和PyBullet四旋翼测试表明，与RRT*和PRM相比，该方法能找到更短、更可靠的路径，并且始终满足不确定性约束。

Conclusion: 将定位不确定性显式建模为A*搜索硬约束，可在GPS拒止场景下实现兼顾路径长度、定位可靠性和最优性的无人机路径规划。

Abstract: This paper studies UAV path planning in areas where GPS signals are not present. Because these drones can easily get lost when they don't have GPS, they must rely on dead reckoning and occasionally pass near cellular towers, which act as landmarks to reset and fix their position. The goal is to find the shortest path from a start to a goal location while keeping the estimation uncertainty within a given bound. To solve this problem, we propose a localization-constrained A* planner that treats uncertainty as a hard constraint during search. The robot state and covariance are propagated using an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF), and only paths that satisfy the covariance limit are considered. We also show that the proposed method does not affect heuristic used in A* and remains admissible and consistent, ensuring optimality among all feasible paths. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations and PyBullet quadrotor test show that the proposed method finds shorter and more reliable paths compared to RRT* and PRM, while always satisfying the uncertainty constraint.

</details>


### [31] [Continuous-Action World Models: Learning Cross-Embodiment Spatiotemporal Dynamics Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40401)
*Liangyu Li,Qingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CoAWM：通过学习跨具身、跨执行频率的时空动力学先验，在共享潜函数空间中生成机器人动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人轨迹在具身形态和采样/控制频率上高度异构，难以直接共享数据与迁移经验；需要一种能统一表示动作时空结构并支持跨具身、跨频率动作生成的基础框架。

Method: 分两阶段：1）函数预训练，将不同具身和采样频率的轨迹映射到共同表示，通过动作重建与跨时间采样视图的一致性学习共享动力学先验；2）将预训练函数空间连接至世界模型主干，以观测、语言指令和当前机器人状态为条件预测潜动作函数，并由单一解码器对任意查询时间输出对应末端执行器动作。该方法将共享动力学先验、任务条件动作选择和时间采样解耦。

Result: 摘要未报告定量实验结果；作者声称该设计可接纳异构机器人轨迹、扩大预训练数据来源并便于扩展至更大更多样数据集，同时为跨具身和跨控制频率迁移提供统一基础。

Conclusion: CoAWM 通过共享潜函数空间和两阶段学习，将共享动力学先验与任务条件动作生成及时间采样分离，为跨具身、跨执行频率的机器人经验迁移与动作生成提供统一框架。

Abstract: We introduce Continuous-Action World Models (CoAWM), a framework for learning cross-embodiment spatiotemporal dynamics priors that support robot action generation across embodiments and execution frequencies. At its core is a shared latent function space that captures the spatial and temporal structure of robot actions. We learn this space through two stages. First, function pretraining brings trajectories from different embodiments and sampling frequencies into a common representation, learning shared dynamics priors through action reconstruction and consistency across temporal sampling views. By accommodating heterogeneous robot trajectories, this stage broadens the range of data sources available for pretraining and facilitates scaling to larger and more diverse datasets. Second, we connect the pretrained function space to a world-model backbone, enabling observations, language instructions, and the current robot state to condition the prediction of a latent action function. A single decoder takes any query time and returns the corresponding end-effector action. By separating shared dynamics priors from task-conditioned action selection and temporal sampling, our approach provides a unified foundation for transferring robot experience across embodiments and control frequencies.

</details>


### [32] [Securing the Robotics Supply Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40385)
*Cindy L. Bethel,Henrik I. Christensen,Odest Chadwicke Jenkins,George Pappas,Holly Yanco,Haley Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文主张制定并持续实施为期约十年的联邦机器人研究与投资议程，以系统性弥合美国机器人领域的能力缺口，而非依赖单一年度预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人不应仅被视为人工智能投资的下游受益者；它拥有独立供应链和特有失效点，并存在验证与认证来源不明组件、基础硬件研究投入不足、规模化测试物理基础设施欠缺及 workforce 缺口等关键问题。

Method: 通过政策分析与议程设定，识别当前差距最显著的环节，并提出联邦层面需长期投入的重点方向：验证与认证方法、基础硬件研究、规模化测试基础设施和专业人才队伍建设。

Result: 摘要未报告实证或量化结果；其核心产出是提出一项跨预算周期、持续十年的联邦机器人研究与投资议程及优先领域。

Conclusion: 美国需要将机器人作为独立战略供应链给予持续联邦承诺，制定长期研究与投资议程，以补齐验证认证、硬件研究、测试基础设施和人才方面的结构性缺口。

Abstract: This paper sets out what a sustained federal research and investment agenda would need to include to close that gap--not within a single budget cycle, but over the decade such an undertaking will realistically require. It identifies where the gap is most pronounced today: in the methods needed to verify and certify components of uncertain origin, in the foundational hardware research that has long been underfunded relative to software, in the physical infrastructure researchers need to test systems at scale, and in the workforce required to build and sustain all of it. Robotics should not be treated merely as a downstream beneficiary of a broader artificial intelligence investment strategy. It constitutes its own supply chain, with its own points of failure, and merits its own sustained federal commitment.

</details>


### [33] [The Athlete as a Scientist of One: Agentic Bayesian Experimental Design for Individualized Physiology from Consumer Wearables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40370)
*Behnam Moradi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EpiCoach：将一名越野山地车运动员作为可问责N-of-1科学对象，通过证据门控假设注册表、数字孪生预测与约束实验处方，实现可审计、可证伪的个性化健康智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用于个人健康的LLM智能体虽可读取可穿戴数据并给出建议，但不会以可审计、可证伪的方式学习个体特征。

Method: 构建证据门控假设注册表：每条关于运动员的断言须在未来训练前给出概率预测，其信念仅依据记录在案的统计拟合计算证据更新。预测来自基于心率、GPS和气压海拔推断的机制性数字孪生，包括滞后心率响应模型、学习时间常数的体能-疲劳模型，以及在发现的路段上测量的潜在下坡技术状态。智能体在负荷与安全约束下，围绕两次仪器化骑行，将下一训练周设计为最大化信息增益的约束实验。用18个月数据进行滚动起点回放基准与前瞻性周循环评估。

Result: 回放基准中，数字孪生在心率预测上优于标准预测器，但路段用时未优于；智能体循环与静态模型相当。前瞻阶段前三周注册表封存20条预测并评分14条；下坡预测经一次修正后校准，爬坡预测因运动员进步而偏悲观；安全官两次否决智能体建议。所有修正均保留在注册表中。

Conclusion: EpiCoach提供了可审计、可证伪的数字健康个性化模板，并发布注册表、基准与协议。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents for personal health read wearable data and recommend, but do not learn about the individual in a way that can be audited or falsified. We present EpiCoach, an agentic system that treats one athlete, a cross-country mountain biker, as the subject of accountable N-of-1 science. Claims about the athlete live in an evidence-gated hypothesis registry: each must issue a probabilistic prediction about a future session before it occurs, and its belief moves only through computed evidence from a logged statistical fit. Predictions come from a mechanistic digital twin inferred from heart rate, GPS and barometric altitude: a lagged heart-rate response model, a fitness-fatigue model with learned time constants, and a latent technical-descending skill state, measured on discovered trail segments. The agent prescribes the next training week as a constrained experiment maximizing information gain subject to load and safety constraints, around two instrument rides. We evaluate the system two ways over eighteen months of data. A rolling-origin replay benchmark shows the twin beats standard forecasters on heart rate but not segment time, and the agentic loop matches a static model. A live prospective phase runs the weekly cycle: in its first three weeks the ledger sealed 20 predictions, scored 14, the descent forecast calibrated after one correction, the climb forecast pessimistic as the athlete improved, and the safety officer vetoed the agent twice. Every correction is preserved in the registry, a template for auditable, falsifiable personalization in digital health. We release the registry, benchmark and protocol.

</details>


### [34] [JEDI: Joint-Embedding Demonstration Compression for In-Context Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40356)
*Jiazhao Liang,Zhewen He,Shuaihang Yuan,Hao Huang,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出JEDI，将演示压缩为潜在槽，并以查询回合下一阶段关键帧在冻结视频-JEPA空间中的预测作为训练目标，从而在上下文模仿学习（ICIL）中支持多示例上下文并保留执行相关细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICIL通过测试时演示适配机器人策略，但长多模态轨迹占用大量上下文，限制可放入的示例数量；论文追问压缩演示应保留哪些信息才能有效支持模仿。

Method: JEDI将演示压缩为latent slots，训练这些槽预测查询episode的下一阶段关键帧，预测发生在冻结的video-JEPA空间；利用任务与过程反事实使槽保留执行细节，并用predictor从演示库中选择演示；通过关键点token后训练实现人手条件到机器人的迁移。

Result: 在LIBERO和真实机器人实验中，JEDI支持many-shot上下文；LIBERO目标控制实验表明，相比仅动作压缩，JEDI提高了成功率和演示跟随程度。

Conclusion: 以查询未来预测作为压缩演示的训练目标，可保留执行相关信息，是提升ICIL中压缩演示有效性的有力方向。

Abstract: In-context imitation learning (ICIL) adapts robot policies through test-time demonstrations, but long multimodal trajectories constrain how many examples fit in context. We ask what a compressed demonstration should preserve. We propose JEDI, which compresses demonstrations into latent slots trained to predict the query episode's next-stage keyframe in a frozen video-JEPA space. Task and process counterfactuals help the slots preserve execution details, while the predictor selects demonstrations from a bank. Post-training with keypoint tokens enables human-hand conditioning on robots. Experiments on LIBERO and real robots show that JEDI supports many-shot context; LIBERO objective controls show improved success and demonstration following over action-only compression. These findings support query-future prediction as a training objective for preserving execution-relevant information in compressed demonstrations.

</details>


### [35] [TouchBridge: Tactile-Conditioned Human-to-Robot Transfer for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40349)
*Haozhe Zhang,Gu Zhang,Zirui Zou,Yuzheng Chen,Jinlong Wu,Zixuan Hu,Jianke Zhang,Yucheng Hu,Zhecheng Yuan,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TouchBridge 是一个软硬件协同的人到机器人数据转换框架，通过视觉与指尖触觉跨具身对应及 Tac-Retarget 触觉条件重定向，将人类演示直接转换为机器人域训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示是灵巧机器人操作的高效可扩展数据源，但传感差异和手部形态差异使接触丰富交互的人到机器人迁移困难。

Method: 提出 TouchBridge：包含可穿戴人类演示接口与对应机器人接口，建立视觉和指尖触觉的跨具身对应；提出 Tac-Retarget，利用实测接触对齐指尖接触斑块并保持手内及跨手空间关系，无需物体网格、物体位姿估计或物理仿真；结合视觉合成与相机锚定动作转换，生成机器人域演示以微调预训练 VLA 模型。

Result: 在四个真实接触丰富任务中，仅用 TouchBridge 转换的人类演示微调的策略平均成功率为 72.5%，使用等量遥操作机器人演示为 77.5%，而人类演示采集速度快 7.0 倍；相同策略和训练设置下，Tac-Retarget 分别比 DexPilot 和 UniDex 高 25.0 和 42.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: TouchBridge 能在无需对象网格、位姿估计或物理仿真的情况下，将人类演示可扩展地转换为机器人域数据，有效支持接触丰富的灵巧操作策略学习。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer an efficient and scalable source of data for dexterous robot manipulation, but differences in sensing and hand morphology complicate the transfer of contact-rich interactions. We present TouchBridge, a hardware--software co-designed framework for human-to-robot data transformation. A wearable human demonstration interface and a corresponding robot interface establish cross-embodiment correspondence in visual and fingertip tactile sensing. We introduce Tac-Retarget, a tactile-conditioned retargeting method that uses measured contact to align fingertip contact patches and preserve their spatial relations within and across hands. Unlike object-aware or simulation-based transfer pipelines, Tac-Retarget requires no object mesh, object-pose estimate, or physics simulation for retargeting, enabling direct and scalable conversion of human demonstrations into robot-domain training data. Combined with visual synthesis and camera-anchored action conversion, TouchBridge produces robot-domain demonstrations for fine-tuning a pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) model. Across four real-world contact-rich tasks, policies fine-tuned solely on TouchBridge-converted human demonstrations achieve a 72.5% average success rate, compared with 77.5% using an equal number of teleoperated robot demonstrations, while human demonstration collection is 7.0x faster. Under the same policy and training setup, Tac-Retarget outperforms DexPilot and UniDex by 25.0 and 42.5 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [36] [MUSBRIC: LEGO-Compatible Pneumatic Muscle Bricks for Reconfigurable Musculoskeletal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40285)
*Qiyao Wang,Yinglei Zhu,Liang Zhou,Wenzhe Gong,Xin-Jun Liu,Huichan Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MUSBRIC 是面向可重构肌骨机器人的模块化硬件平台与 MuJoCo 仿真器：通过 LEGO 兼容端盖将纤维增强伸缩型气动人工肌肉做成可插拔“肌肉砖”，并用实验标定的驱动模型支撑仿真，完成肘关节、机械手、15 种腿部配置扫描与四足行走验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌骨机器人结合柔性驱动器与关节骨架，但探索替代构型往往需要反复制造，硬件迭代成本高、周期长，缺乏可复用模块与配套仿真。

Method: 设计 LEGO 兼容端盖，把纤维增强伸缩型气动人工肌肉封装为可复用、即插即用的肌肉砖，可直接连接标准 LEGO 件；建立基于 MuJoCo 的肌骨机器人仿真器，并用实验辨识的驱动器行为校准仿真；构建肘关节、机械手和四足等原型，开展硬件与仿真匹配的配置扫描。

Result: 肘关节原型验证了负载相关屈伸，滑轮导向布线实现可逆运动，机械限位稳定伸直姿态；机械手展示柔顺抓取与多关节构建；15 种腿部构型的硬件/仿真扫描系统探索连接几何；四腿四足机器人实现行走。

Conclusion: 该工作在同一可重构系统中贯通机构分析、构型探索与功能机器人构建，降低肌骨机器人迭代门槛；硬件设计与仿真代码将开源以促进复现和后续开发。

Abstract: Musculoskeletal robots combine compliant actuators with articulated skeletons, but exploring alternative configurations often requires repeated fabrication. We present MUSBRIC, a modular hardware platform and accompanying MuJoCo-based simulator for reconfigurable musculoskeletal robots. LEGO-compatible endcaps turn fiber-reinforced extensile pneumatic artificial muscles into reusable, plug-and-play muscle bricks that connect directly to standard LEGO components. Experimentally identified actuator behavior supports corresponding musculoskeletal simulations. An elbow-joint prototype validates load-dependent flexion and extension, showing that pulley-guided routing enables reversible motion and a mechanical stop stabilizes the straight posture. A robotic hand demonstrates compliant grasping and multi-joint construction. Matched hardware and simulation sweeps across 15 leg configurations demonstrate systematic exploration of attachment geometry, while a quadruped assembled from four legs demonstrates walking. Together, these demonstrations span mechanism analysis, configuration exploration, and functional robot construction within a single reconfigurable system. The hardware designs and simulation code will be released as open source soon to facilitate reproduction and further development.

</details>


### [37] [Gaussian Distillation of Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40271)
*Youssef Saied,Ziyad Sheebaelhamd,Claire Vernade,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出高斯动作读出（GAR），用高斯读出头替换流策略最后一层，在几乎不训练主体网络的情况下实现单次前向推理、闭式似然和最高2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略是强机器人控制器，适合建模多峰动作分布，但推理通常需要多步采样；作者发现所研究的流策略的连续动作分布可被高斯很好近似。

Method: GAR仅用0.02%的可用状态训练一个高斯读出层，替换原流策略最终层，并保持网络其余部分冻结。

Result: GAR通过一次动作网络前向完成采样，提供闭式似然，并在最高2倍推理速度下达到接近教师策略的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 连续动作流策略的最终输出可用轻量高斯读出高效近似，从而在保持任务性能的同时显著简化并加速推理。

Abstract: Flow policies are among the strongest robot controllers and are well suited to modelling multimodal action distributions. Our analysis shows that the continuous-action distributions of the studied flow policies are well approximated by Gaussians. We propose the Gaussian Action Readout (GAR), which replaces a flow policy's final layer with a Gaussian readout trained on just 0.02% of eligible states, while keeping the rest of the network frozen. It samples with one action-network pass, gives closed-form likelihoods, and achieves near-teacher task success at up to twice the inference speed.

</details>


### [38] [LLM-guided Planar Loco-Manipulation for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40248)
*Georges Jetti,Michael Khayyat,Jean-Pierre Sleiman,Stefano Arrigoni,Francesco Braghin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种语言条件任务规划器，联合选择技能、相关物体和几何目标，以处理需物理交互的杂乱环境导航；高层用LoRA在合成数据上微调，低层采用端到端全身平面推挤策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航通常假设存在无碰撞路径，但在含可移动障碍的杂乱环境中，到达目标可能需要物理交互；经典方法依赖几何规则和任务特定逻辑，学习型方法常受限于低维运动命令或预定义动作集。

Method: 将自然语言任务与结构化场景表示映射为变长参数化动作序列，联合决策技能、物体和几何目标，并由低层强化学习策略执行；高层规划器在合成数据上用LoRA微调，低层提出端到端全身平面推挤策略。

Result: 在交互式导航任务上优于经典规划器和提示式前沿模型；与从零训练模型相比，结构化表示下组合能力不依赖预训练，而预训练可产生更可靠的几何目标；仿真中全身推挤成功率达93.3%，优于分层基线的83.5%，完成时间减少60%以上；硬件上44/45次试验技能组合正确，34/45次端到端成功。

Conclusion: 该分层框架通过更广的决策空间实现交互式导航，表明结构化表示与预训练在几何目标可靠性上具有互补作用，并在仿真和硬件上验证了有效性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Robotic navigation typically assumes a collision-free route to the goal exists; in cluttered environments with movable obstacles, however, reaching the goal may require physical interaction. Classical solutions encode these behaviors through geometric rules and task-specific logic, while learning-based approaches often constrain the decision space to low-dimensional motion commands or predefined action sets.
