We instead consider a broader decision space, jointly selecting the skill, relevant object, and geometric target. Our language-conditioned task planner, fine-tuned with LoRA on synthetic data, maps natural-language tasks and a structured scene representation to variable-length sequences of parameterized actions, executed by low-level reinforcement-learning policies. The planner learns the geometric relations underlying interactive navigation and outperforms a classical planner and a prompted frontier model on interactive navigation tasks. Comparisons with models trained from scratch indicate that composition does not require pretraining when the representation is structured, whereas pretraining yields more reliable geometric targets. At the low level, we introduce an end-to-end whole-body planar pushing policy that achieves a 93.3% success rate in simulation, versus 83.5% for a hierarchical baseline, while reducing completion time by more than 60%. On hardware, the hierarchy yields the correct skill composition in 44/45 trials and end-to-end success in 34/45. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [39] [AcrossDEX 1.0: From Human Motion Pretraining to Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation across Hand Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40210)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AcrossDEX 1.0 是一个连接人类运动预训练、仿真专家学习、机器人演示采集与视觉闭环执行的双手灵巧操作学习系统，并在两种手部本体上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手灵巧操作需要持续协调手腕运动、手指接触与物体运动；利用人类视频经验控制机器人时，必须对齐人类姿态与机器人命令，并处理不同本体的运动学与接触动力学差异。

Method: 系统先在 HOT3D Aria 上预训练，用5帧图像和过去1秒的30个手部状态预测未来1秒双手运动；机器人适配保留视觉编码器、时序骨干和手腕参数，采用共享手腕表征、分离手指场和有效性掩码支持6通道 Inspire 手与16关节 Allegro 手。机器人策略从正面图像、命令历史和任务条件预测60个控制目标，执行动作前缀并基于新观测重规划，同时比较直接回归、扩散和 flow matching。

Result: 直接回归在开盖并放回任务成功率为40.6%，在 Inspire 闭盖物体重定向为100.0%，在 Allegro 为93.3%；flow matching 在 Allegro 重定向达到90.0%。配对比较表明，用前一计划约束重规划可提升生成策略的连续执行。

Conclusion: 研究提供了面向两种手部本体的通用适配管线，并突出接触协调与动作块连续性在双手灵巧操作中的作用。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous manipulation requires continuous coordination of wrist motion, finger contact, and object motion. Human videos provide rich manipulation experience. Using this experience for robot control requires aligning human poses with robot commands and accounting for embodiment-specific kinematics and contact dynamics. We present AcrossDEX 1.0, a learning system that connects human motion pretraining, simulation-based expert learning, robot demonstration collection, and visual closed-loop execution. Pretraining on HOT3D Aria predicts one second of bimanual motion from five images and thirty annotated hand states over the preceding second. Robot adaptation preserves the visual encoder, temporal backbone, and wrist parameters. Shared wrist representations, separate finger fields, and validity masks support both six-channel Inspire hands and sixteen-joint Allegro hands. The robot policy predicts sixty control targets from frontal images, command history, and task conditions. It executes a prefix and replans from updated observations. We compare direct regression, diffusion, and flow matching on lid opening and return and closed-lid object reorientation. Direct regression achieves success rates of 40.6\% for opening and return, 100.0\% for Inspire reorientation, and 93.3\% for Allegro reorientation. Flow matching achieves 90.0\% on Allegro reorientation. Paired comparisons further show that constraining replanning with the previous plan improves the continuous execution of generative policies. These results demonstrate a common adaptation pipeline for two hand embodiments and highlight the roles of contact coordination and action-chunk continuity.

</details>


### [40] [RoboAtom: Large-scale Atomic-Action Supervision for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40207)
*Xueyuan Wei,Xuanxu Wang,Xudong Li,Yuping He,Wanshui Gan,Jiaqi Peng,Wenqi Guo,Jianjun Zhou,Junting Dong,Chunhua Shen,Haoyu Guo,Mulin Yu,Tao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboAtom-Data，为多源机器人数据添加统一、时间对齐的原子动作标注，并构建基准与模型，验证其对技能识别、时序定位和机器人控制的促进作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度动作标注有助于技能复用和组合泛化，但现有机器人数据多为任务级指令，子任务标注格式与规范不一致，阻碍跨源联合训练。

Method: 构建统一标注协议，结合大规模人工标注、对具有相同有序技能序列的重复执行进行传播，以及多阶段质量控制；覆盖145万条轨迹、688万个技能标注片段、16882.1小时数据。构建 RoboAtom-Bench 标准化评估技能识别与时序边界定位，并训练 RoboAtom-Anno；在机器人控制中采用原子动作监督与分层执行。

Result: RoboAtom-Anno 在 RoboAtom-Bench 和外部 WGO-Bench 上的自动标注性能优于所有评估的开源与闭源模型；原子动作监督结合分层执行在 LIBERO-Long、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 上相比任务级基线提升任务成功率。

Conclusion: 统一的原子动作标注数据、基准、模型与构建流程可支持技能级机器人学习研究，并验证了细粒度时序标注对自动标注和策略执行的实用价值。

Abstract: Fine-grained action annotations can support skill reuse and compositional generalization in robot policies. However, robot datasets typically provide task-level instructions without subtask annotations. Although some include subtask labels, inconsistent formats and conventions hinder cross-source joint training. We propose RoboAtom-Data, which adds unified, time-aligned atomic-action annotations to existing data from multiple sources, covering 1.45 million trajectories, 6.88 million skill-labeled segments, and 16,882.1 hours. Our pipeline combines a unified protocol, large-scale manual annotation, propagation across repeated executions with the same ordered skill sequence, and multi-stage quality control. We evaluate the dataset through automatic atomic-action annotation and robot control. We construct RoboAtom-Bench to standardize evaluation of skill recognition and temporal boundary localization. Trained on the annotated data, RoboAtom-Anno achieves better automatic annotation performance than all evaluated open- and closed-source models on both RoboAtom-Bench and the external WGO-Bench. For robot control, atomic-action supervision paired with hierarchical execution improves task success over task-level baselines on LIBERO-Long, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin. We will release the data, benchmark, model, and construction pipeline to support further research on skill-level robot learning.

</details>


### [41] [Decision-Level Error Attribution and Depth-Aware Safety Evaluation of Monocular RGB Human Tracking for Autonomous Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40199)
*Abdulahi Taiwo,Chijioke Cyriacus Ekechi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文将单目RGB行人跟踪中的二元停/行决策误差分解为安全规则与感知误差，并比较横向距离规则与平面距离规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人靠近人运行时，单目RGB跟踪的感知误差会直接影响停/行决策，而几何精度指标无法充分反映这种决策层面的错误；需要量化安全规则与感知各自造成的决策误差。

Method: 作者证明仅使用横向距离是平面距离的下界：在位置准确时不会漏掉平面规则识别的危险，但会造成不必要的停止。提出双因素Shapley归因分离规则与感知贡献，并区分漏检与定位误差。实验使用ETH和UCY行人视频、模拟静止机器人、不相交的标定与评估行人及时间块bootstrap区间。

Result: 在三个场景898名行人中，横向规则在标注位置上未漏危险，但停止精度仅5.6–31.9%。两个场景的RGB感知下，标定地平面定位的平面RMSE为0.59 m和0.51 m，优于几何基线的1.72 m和1.39 m以及标定Depth Anything V2的0.81 m和0.68 m。检测召回率为0.43和0.26，漏检成为漏危险的主要来源。采用地平面定位的两倍标准差安全裕量在全部八个场景-阈值设置中匹配或改善横向规则的漏危险率，并在七个设置中减少不必要停止。

Conclusion: 决策误差需同时评估安全规则与感知；横向距离规则虽保守但停止精度低，感知漏检是主要风险来源，标定地平面定位结合安全裕量可在多数设置中减少不必要停止而不增加漏危险。所有机器人交互均为模拟，结果为离线决策分类，并非碰撞避免演示。

Abstract: Monocular RGB tracking is useful for robots operating near people, but perception errors affect binary stop/proceed decisions in ways that geometric accuracy metrics do not capture. We examine how much decision error arises from the safety rule versus perception. We prove that lateral-only distance is a lower bound on planar distance: with accurate positions, it cannot miss dangers identified by the planar rule, but it produces unnecessary stops. We introduce a two-factor Shapley attribution separating rule and perception contributions, and further distinguish missed detections from localization errors. Experiments use ETH and UCY pedestrian videos with simulated stationary robots, disjoint calibration and evaluation pedestrians, and time-block bootstrap intervals. Across 898 pedestrians in three scenes, the lateral rule missed no dangers on annotated positions but achieved only 5.6-31.9% stop precision. In two scenes with RGB perception, calibrated ground-plane localization achieved 0.59 m and 0.51 m planar RMSE, compared with 1.72 m and 1.39 m for a geometric baseline and 0.81 m and 0.68 m for calibrated Depth Anything V2. Detection recall of 0.43 and 0.26 made missed detections the leading source of missed danger. A two-standard-deviation safety margin with ground-plane localization matched or improved on the lateral rule's missed-danger rate in all eight scene-threshold settings and reduced unnecessary stops in seven. All robot interactions are simulated, and outcomes are offline decision classifications, not demonstrations of collision avoidance.

</details>


### [42] [Learning Safe Flow Matching Robot Policies from Binary Feasibility Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40075)
*Jianming Ma,Rongjun Jin,Yizhi Chen,Yue Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖黑盒可行性查询的 oracle 引导可微分安全防护机制，用于约束流匹配机器人策略，在训练与推理中同时保障物理约束并保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流匹配的机器人基础模型动作生成不保证满足关节限制、避碰等物理约束；当可行集无法解析描述且只能通过黑盒可行性 oracle 访问时，现有方法难以提供可微约束信号。

Method: 在 oracle-only 设定下，利用二值可行性查询在前向传播中纠正不安全更新，并从局部边界信息中提取约束感知学习信号用于反向传播；结合离散时间流匹配，使同一安全防护在训练和推理阶段复用，实现前向-反向对齐的策略学习，无需显式或可微约束模型。

Result: 在障碍物感知机器人操作实验中实现 100% 约束满足，同时保持任务性能。

Conclusion: 该 oracle 引导可微分安全防护可在缺乏解析约束模型的实际场景中，为流匹配机器人策略提供训练与推理一致的安全保障。

Abstract: Robot foundation models increasingly rely on flow-based action generation for general-purpose manipulation, yet the generated actions are not guaranteed to satisfy physical constraints such as joint limits and collision avoidance. This problem is especially challenging when the feasible set cannot be described analytically and is accessible only through black-box feasibility oracles. We study this practical oracle-only setting and propose an oracle-guided differentiable safeguard for safe flow-matching robot policies. The safeguard uses binary feasibility queries to correct unsafe updates in the forward pass, while extracting local boundary information to provide constraint-aware learning signals in the backward pass. Combined with discrete-time flow matching, the same safeguard is used during training and inference, enabling forward--backward aligned policy learning without explicit or differentiable constraint models. Experiments on obstacle-aware robotic manipulation demonstrate 100% constraint satisfaction while preserving task performance.

</details>


### [43] [Don't Risk It!: Trajectory-Conditioned Distributional Risk for LiDAR-Only Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40050)
*Mathai Mathew Pulicken,Laksh Nanwani,Basant Sharma,Phani Teja Singamaneni,Arun Kumar Singh,K. Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DRIP 是一个仅依赖 LiDAR 的端到端人群导航规划框架，将占据栅格预测、轨迹条件化间隙估计与不确定性建模结合，用于动态人群中的碰撞风险评估与规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人群导航需要提前预测动态无碰撞空间，但依赖目标跟踪的方法容易受跟踪失败影响；因此需要不依赖脆弱目标跟踪、能直接利用 LiDAR 感知信息并显式评估碰撞风险的方法。

Method: DRIP 直接基于 LiDAR 生成的占据栅格历史进行规划，耦合占据预测与轨迹条件化的最小机器人-障碍物间隙及不确定性估计；对候选控制序列预测最小间隙分布以评估碰撞风险，并使用将预测不确定性直接 grounding 到实际碰撞结果的风险代价训练，而非仅做几何回归。

Result: 在最多 45 名行人的闭环实验中，DRIP 相比现有最先进基线取得更高成功率、更少碰撞，并减少机器人冻结行为。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅依赖 LiDAR 的占据预测结合不确定性感知的风险估计，可提升拥挤动态环境中导航的成功率与安全性，并缓解冻结问题。

Abstract: Efficient crowd navigation requires anticipating dynamic collision-free space without relying on fragile object tracking. We propose the Don't Risk It Planner (DRIP), an end-to-end LiDAR-only planning framework that couples occupancy forecasting with trajectory-conditioned clearance and uncertainty estimation. Operating directly on LiDAR-derived occupancy grid histories, DRIP predicts a distribution over minimum robot-obstacle clearance for candidate control sequences to evaluate collision risk. The model is trained using a risk cost that grounds predictive uncertainty directly in empirical collision outcomes rather than geometric regression alone. In closed-loop experiments with up to 45 pedestrians, DRIP achieves higher success rates with fewer collisions and reduced freezing behavior compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [44] [RoboSeed: Multidimensional Evaluation of Vision-Language-Action and World-Action Models: Capability, Resource Affordability, and Research Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40026)
*Xin Ma,Furong Chen,Xinlong Man,Bo Zhang,Yalong Guo,Haolan Lei,Chaochen Cui,Hongyuan Zhang,Mulin Chen,Yi Wang,Lap-pui Chau,Chuang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboSeed，一个面向 VLA 与 WAM 的多维评测框架，用能力、训练可负担性、部署可负担性、本地可验证研究价值和复现证据五个维度生成 0-100 画像，并强调证据缺口与比较协议约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭成功率不足以支持机器人动作模型选择；研究者需要同时了解任务能力、适配成本、部署要求以及结论可靠性，且不同计算预算与目标下应有不同决策依据。

Method: RoboSeed 定义五个评测维度，将经验测量与审计准则通过固定归一化锚点映射为 0-100 分，并提供均衡、训练受限和部署受限三类偏好设置；通过接口与协议检查判断比较是否适当，缺失测量保持显式，不完整聚合分数不予发布，以加权输入覆盖率识别证据缺口。作者将其应用于代表性 VLA 与 WAM 模型，依据本地训练、发布权重评估、任务级审计和评测系统研究进行分析。

Result: 结果显示骨干模型选择依赖预算，对适配配方敏感，且语言与视觉任务接口存在差异；框架可给出维度级评估与后续测量优先级，并提供可复现评分代码和机器可读证据。由于服务轨迹不完整且协议异质，无法形成总体数值排名。

Conclusion: RoboSeed 提供了面向模型选择的多维、可审计评测路径，强调在证据不完整时避免过度聚合排名，并为 VLA/WAM 的开放评测与扩展提供可复现基础。

Abstract: Choosing a robot action model requires evidence about task capability, adaptation cost, deployment requirements, and the reliability of reported conclusions. Success rates alone offer limited guidance to researchers with different computing budgets and goals. We propose RoboSeed, a multidimensional evaluation framework for vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAM), within a continuing program to train, evaluate, and openly release robot models with verifiable evidence for model selection. The framework defines five dimensions: capability, downstream training affordability, deployment affordability, locally verified research value, and reproduction evidence. It maps empirical measurements and audit criteria to 0-100 profiles using fixed normalization anchors, with balanced, training-constrained, and deployment-constrained preference settings. Interface and protocol checks establish when comparisons are appropriate. Missing measurements remain explicit, incomplete aggregate scores are withheld, and weighted input coverage identifies evidence gaps. We apply the framework to representative VLA and WAM models, drawing on local training, released-weight evaluation, task-level audits, and an evaluation-system study. The results reveal budget-dependent backbone choices, sensitivity to adaptation recipes, and differences between language and visual task interfaces. Reproducible scoring code and machine-readable evidence support extension to additional models. The study provides dimension-level assessments and priorities for further measurement, while incomplete serving traces and heterogeneous protocols preclude overall numerical rankings.

</details>


### [45] [LOGIV: Graph-Verified Execution with Causal Local Repair for Long-Horizon Robot Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40016)
*Enqiao Lu,Yuxiang Xie,Xingrui Yu,Yuyang Wang,Yipeng Gu,Zhenglin Wan,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You,Yuxiang Gao,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOGIV 是一个基于显式任务图的机器人执行框架，将符号规划、基于观测的验证与具身动作连接起来，在执行中持续检查任务依赖并支持失败修复，从而提升双臂任务的成功率与条件恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体机器人需要把高层目标转化为动作，同时保持未来步骤所需的物理条件有效；单个动作完成并不意味着其效果会持续，后续扰动可能破坏剩余计划仍假设成立的支持关系。

Method: 提出 LOGIV 图工程框架：用显式任务图连接符号规划、基于观测的验证和具身动作；将已验证计划编译为条件支持边与资源排序边，在执行中检查其物理有效性；失败后通过依赖追踪确定需恢复的对象，修复受保留的未完成动作约束，并在恢复执行前对组合后的继续方案进行符号验证；框架位于现有 VLA 与 WAM 策略之上，不增加可训练组件。

Result: 在 RoboTwin V2 与 BiCoord 的 20 个双臂任务、三种执行骨干上，无注入扰动时平均任务成功率分别提升 10.67 和 15.33 个百分点；在预定义扰动下，两个基准上对每种骨干均比三种适配恢复基线取得更高的任务完成率与条件恢复率；在三个真实机器人任务上，成功试验从 12/30 提升到 17/30。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式且持续检查的任务依赖可作为实现可靠智能体机器人执行的实用接口。

Abstract: Agentic robots must turn a high-level goal into actions while keeping the physical conditions needed for future steps valid. A completed action does not ensure that its effects will persist: a later disturbance can invalidate support that the remaining plan still assumes. We present LOGIV, a graph engineering framework for agentic robot execution that uses an explicit task graph to connect symbolic planning, observation-based verification, and embodied action. LOGIV compiles a validated plan into condition-support and resource-ordering edges, checks their physical validity during execution, and uses dependency tracing to determine what must be restored after a failure. Repair is constrained by retained unfinished actions, and the combined continuation is symbolically validated before execution resumes. The framework operates above existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Model (WAM) policies without adding trainable components. Across 20 dual-arm tasks from RoboTwin~V2 and BiCoord with three execution backbones, LOGIV improves mean task success without injected disturbances by 10.67 and 15.33 percentage points, respectively. Under predefined perturbations, it achieves higher task completion and condition restoration than three adapted recovery baselines for every backbone on both benchmarks. On three real-robot tasks, successful trials increase from 12/30 to 17/30. These results support explicit, continuously checked task dependencies as a practical interface for reliable agentic robot execution.

</details>


### [46] [Humanoid Dodgeball: Learning Fast Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance Motions for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40003)
*Aoqian Zhang,Chunzheng Wang,Haoran Chen,Annan Tang,Cheng Xiang,Fan Shi,Shuzhi Sam Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Humanoid Dodgeball 强化学习框架，使类人机器人能够敏捷地进行动态全身躲避来袭抛射物，同时保持稳定、自然且物理可行的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在真实动态环境中需快速躲避高速移动障碍，但高维动力学使反应式避障、运动自然性与控制鲁棒性的联合学习十分困难。

Method: 采用强化学习框架，通过威胁感知奖励塑形实现高动态全身规避动作，并利用多个方向特定的对抗运动先验正则化学习行为，使其趋向稳定、多样且类人的运动。

Result: 大量仿真与硬件实验表明，该方法在不同障碍速度和发射距离下显著提升动态避障性能，同时保持鲁棒且物理合理的类人运动。

Conclusion: Humanoid Dodgeball 能有效实现人形机器人的敏捷动态避障，为人形机器人在动态真实场景中的安全运行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humanoids have become increasingly agile and capable in real-world environments. To operate safely in dynamic scenarios, they must rapidly evade fast-moving obstacles while maintaining stability and physically feasible whole-body motion. Learning such behaviors remains challenging due to the high-dimensional dynamics of humanoid systems and the need to jointly balance reactive obstacle avoidance, motion naturalness, and control robustness. In this work, we present Humanoid Dodgeball, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for agile dynamic obstacle avoidance in humanoids. Our method enables highly dynamic whole-body evasive maneuvers against incoming projectiles through threat-aware reward shaping, while multiple direction-specific adversarial motion priors regularize the learned behaviors toward stable and diverse human-like motions. Extensive simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework substantially improves dynamic obstacle avoidance performance across diverse obstacle speeds and launch distances, while preserving robust and physically plausible humanoid motions.

</details>


### [47] [How Much Should a VLA Trust Its Reasoning? Step-Level Guidance as a Measured Action-Value Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40002)
*Dabin Seo,Eunhyek Joa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 CoC-CFG 与 Q-adaptive guidance：在推理型 VLA 策略中，用 classifier-free guidance 在含推理片段与去除推理片段的上下文之间调节推理对动作生成的引导强度，并将每个 Euler 步的引导权重选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，用小型 critic 拟合精确可测的动作值以自适应调整权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型 VLA 先写推理轨迹再解码动作，错误推理会导致错误动作，甚至看似正确的推理也可能误导规划；现有方法主要判断推理是否正确，本文转而研究推理应以多强程度引导动作。

Method: CoC-CFG 在策略上下文与去掉推理 span 的同一上下文之间做 classifier-free guidance，每个 Euler 步用一个权重衰减或放大推理影响，w=1 恢复原策略；将权重选择视为 MDP，在专家冻结且噪声固定时，单步施加权重后由当前控制器完成规划可精确返回该权重的动作值，而非 bootstrapped 估计；Q-adaptive guidance 用小型 critics 拟合这些值并保守改进控制器。

Result: 在 Alpasim 驾驶模拟器中，开环优于所有固定权重及 SAC、GRPO 等在线 RL，闭环 300 个场景通过 262 个，原策略为 252 个；在 Waymo 端到端驾驶基准的 held-out 帧上，前三阶段优于所有固定权重、四种自适应引导方法、SAC 和 GRPO；隐藏测试集上，冻结的 Alpamayo 1.5 配合基于规则的相机与车辆转换及 Q-adaptive guidance 取得已发表方法中新 SOTA：rater feedback score 8.135159，对比最佳已发表方法 8.079。

Conclusion: 推理引导的关键不只是推理是否正确，而是引导强度应多大；按步自适应、基于精确动作值拟合的 Q-adaptive guidance 能保守提升推理型 VLA 驾驶策略，并在多个评测上超过固定权重、自适应引导基线和在线 RL，取得新的 SOTA。

Abstract: Reasoning vision-language-action (VLA) policies write a reasoning trace and then decode the action from it. A wrong trace yields a wrong action, and even a correct-looking trace can mislead the plan. Existing remedies ask whether the reasoning is right. We ask how strongly it should steer. CoC-CFG applies classifier-free guidance between the policy's context and the same context without the reasoning span. One weight per Euler step attenuates or amplifies the reasoning, and w=1 recovers the original policy. We treat the choice of this weight as a Markov decision process whose action values are measured, not bootstrapped. With the expert frozen and the noise fixed, applying a weight for one step and finishing the plan under the current controller returns that weight's value exactly. Q-adaptive guidance fits these values with small critics and improves the controller conservatively. In the Alpasim driving simulator it outperforms every fixed weight and online RL (SAC, GRPO) in open loop, and in closed loop it passes 262 of 300 scenes, against 252 for the original policy. On held-out frames of the Waymo end-to-end driving benchmark, its first three stages outperform every fixed weight, four adaptive-guidance methods, SAC and GRPO. On the hidden test set, a frozen Alpamayo 1.5 with rule-based camera and vehicle conversions and Q-adaptive guidance sets a new state of the art among published methods: 8.135159 rater feedback score, against 8.079 for the best published method.

</details>


### [48] [Gaze-Assisted Supervisory Control for Lunar Robotic Arm Teleoperation Under Concurrent Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39998)
*Jiayu He,Yifan Li,Oliver R. Runswick,Ernest N. Kamavuako,Yunjie Jia,Peter D. Hodkinson,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估一种注视辅助监督控制框架：操作者用注视提名岩石或放置目标，经显式确认后由机器人执行受限自主抓取-放置，以减少月面样本处理中连续摇杆遥操作的视觉-运动对齐负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摇杆连续遥操作需要反复进行视觉-运动对齐，且操作者还需监控系统状态；研究旨在降低操作负担并保持关键精度阶段的可靠性。

Method: 框架中注视用于任务级目标提名，显式确认授权动作，机器人执行有界自主抓取与放置。采用16名参与者的任务模拟VR研究，与手动摇杆遥操作对比，使用2×2×3被试内设计操纵控制模式、放置难度和并发监控负荷，并测量任务时间、注视切换、NASA-TLX、监控响应及放置误差/成功率。

Result: 平均任务完成时间由47.65 s/岩石降至18.43 s/岩石，其中72.5%的差异发生在抓取阶段；在测试的听觉监控条件下，注视辅助监督控制的平均比例性变慢更少，监控响应准确性和速度未检测到控制模式差异；核心兴趣区间注视切换由49.98降至27.73次/min，原始NASA-TLX由32.54降至18.26。但收益具有任务依赖性：精密放置中手动控制平均放置误差更低、成功率更高。

Conclusion: 结果支持混合主动式分配：注视用于快速指定任务级意图并触发自主执行，而精度关键阶段保留手动精细调整。

Abstract: Rover-mounted robotic arms can extend human reach during lunar sample handling, but continuous joystick teleoperation requires repeated visual-motor alignment while operators may also need to monitor system states. This paper presents a gaze-assisted supervisory control framework in which gaze nominates a rock or placement target, explicit confirmation authorizes the action, and the robot performs bounded autonomous grasp-and-place execution. The framework was evaluated against manual joystick teleoperation in a mission-analog virtual reality study with 16 participants. The study used a 2 x 2 x 3 within-subject design varying control mode, placement difficulty, and concurrent monitoring load. Mean recorded task completion time decreased from 47.65 to 18.43 s per rock, with 72.5% of this difference arising during grasping. Under the tested auditory monitoring conditions, gaze-assisted supervisory control showed less average proportional slowing, with no detected control-mode difference in monitoring-response accuracy or speed. The rate of gaze shifts between core areas of interest decreased from 49.98 to 27.73 shifts/min, while the raw NASA Task Load Index score decreased from 32.54 to 18.26. However, the benefit was task dependent. In precision placement, manual control yielded lower mean placement error and a higher placement success rate. These findings support a mixed-initiative allocation in which gaze enables rapid task-level intention specification and autonomous execution, while precision-critical stages retain manual refinement.

</details>


### [49] [Compressed Orientation Reachability Maps for Sub-Second Robotic Needle Entry Point Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39879)
*Prabhu Nithin Gollapudi,Andreas Maier,Franziska Mathis-Ullrich,Water Zhao,Jia Wang,Siming Bayer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于方向的关节可达性地图（ORM），用于机器人经皮肝介入的多阶段轨迹规划，将可行入口点识别从秒级优化搜索降至亚秒级查表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖穷举逆运动学或随机搜索，每个目标需13–27秒，难以满足术中实时自适应规划需求；可行入口点识别成为关键计算瓶颈。

Method: 预先计算6自由度串联机械臂在188,873个工作空间位置的运动学可行性，将方向可行性编码为紧凑索引化的83 MB ORM；运行时采用JIT编译流水线，以近即时数组查找替代在线优化。

Result: 规划延迟由13.5–26.9秒/目标降至0.289–0.349秒/目标；相对基线可达点云，平均凸包体积重叠比为0.933，平均alpha-shape体积比为0.976，保持较高几何保真度。

Conclusion: ORM框架实现了亚秒级入口点规划，为机器人经皮肝消融中的针刺插入提供了高效计算框架。

Abstract: In the context of multi-stage trajectory planning for robotic percutaneous liver interventions, identifying a kinematically feasible entry point remains a critical computational bottleneck. Existing methods rely on exhaustive inverse kinematics or randomized searches, incurring delays of 13 to 27 seconds per target and limiting real-time intraoperative adaptation. This paper introduces an Orientation-Based Reachability Map (ORM) that precomputes the kinematic feasibility of a 6 Degrees of Freedom (DoF) serial manipulator across 188,873 workspace positions and encodes orientation feasibility into a compact, indexed 83 MB map. A Just-In-Time (JIT) compiled runtime pipeline replaces live optimization with near-instantaneous array lookups. Experimental evaluation demonstrates a reduction in planning latency from 13.5-26.9 s to 0.289-0.349 s per target while maintaining high geometric fidelity, achieving a mean convex hull volume overlap ratio of 0.933 and a mean alpha-shape volume ratio of 0.976 relative to the baseline reachable point cloud. The proposed ORM framework enables sub-second entry point planning and provides an efficient computational framework for robotic needle insertion in percutaneous liver ablation.

</details>


### [50] [PASA-KV: Attacking Action-Facing Visual Routes in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39816)
*Zhongbo Wu,Jiakai Huang,Zheng Zhang,Siyu Xu,Kaixiang Chen,Xiaohong Liu,Shan You,Chang Xu,Tao Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究世界动作模型（WAM）中内部视觉/世界表示扰动何时真正影响闭环行为，发现表示可扰动性与控制脆弱性存在明显不匹配，并提出面向动作读出的脆弱性视角及 Tail-AV/PASA-KV 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 日益将内部视觉或世界表示与动作生成耦合，其 world-to-action 路径是鲁棒性的关键来源但理解不足；现有分析常以表示空间位移大小衡量攻击效果，需厘清内部表示扰动何时转化为行为层面的控制失败。

Method: 作者将不匹配归因于 action-conditioned readout：keys 决定动作查询读取何处，values 决定其接收何种内容，因此提出 action-facing vulnerability，即扰动影响取决于其如何被路由进动作生成。基于此，用 Tail-AV 作为短探针信号识别脆弱路由，并在 PASA-KV 中先选择面向动作的 K/V 目标，再训练新的任务共享攻击。

Result: 在多个 WAM 系统和物理机器人上的实验验证了 readout-informed 目标选择的有效性，展示了强闭环攻击；同时发现攻击效果在不同视觉 K/V 路由间差异显著，较大的表示位移并不稳定意味着更强的控制失败，且不同系统在抵抗性上存在显著系统级差异。

Conclusion: WAM 的脆弱性不应仅由表示空间扰动幅度判断，而应由扰动如何被路由进动作生成决定；面向动作读出的 K/V 目标选择能有效识别和利用闭环脆弱路径，并揭示系统级鲁棒性差异。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) increasingly couple internal visual or world representations with action generation, making their world-to-action pathways a critical yet poorly understood source of robustness. We investigate when perturbations to these internal representations actually become behaviorally consequential, and uncover a pronounced mismatch between representation perturbability and closed-loop vulnerability: attack effectiveness varies sharply across visual K/V routes, while larger representation displacement does not reliably imply stronger control failure. Our analysis attributes this mismatch to the action-conditioned readout, where keys determine where action queries read and values determine what content they receive. This motivates an action-facing view of vulnerability, in which the effect of a perturbation depends on how it is routed into action generation rather than on representation-space magnitude alone. We instantiate this view with Tail-AV, a short-probe signal for identifying vulnerable routes, and operationalize it in PASA-KV, which selects action-facing K/V targets before training a fresh task-shared attack. Experiments across multiple WAM systems and a physical robot validate readout-informed target selection, demonstrate strong closed-loop attacks, and reveal substantial system-level differences in resistance.

</details>


### [51] [Surrogate Uncertainty for Black-box Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39798)
*Hanbo Zhang,Tianrun Hu,Cedar Site Bai,Anxing Xiao,David Hsu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面向黑盒VLM视觉 grounding 的不确定性量化，提出外部代理贝叶斯估计器并用于主动视觉 grounding，提升校准、任务性能与真实机器人鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需将自由形式自然语言指令在视觉观测中 grounding 到目标物体；现有方法从VLM自身导出不确定性，但预测与不确定性共享参数和结构，继承相同偏差，导致不可靠。

Method: 将VLM预测视为观测，给出形式化贝叶斯定义；用外部代理贝叶斯模型在留出校准集的局部邻域内量化逐查询不确定性，并分解为偶然与认知不确定性；通过跨输入模态的层次化检索构建紧凑联合空间邻域以缓解维度灾难；估计器可结合保形预测或基于模型的规划进行主动视觉 grounding，允许机器人采取语言或物理动作。

Result: 在多个 grounding 基准和先进VLM上，方法持续改善校准与任务性能；在真实Fetch机器人开放世界指令下，实现自信行动与信息采集的原则性权衡，显著提升鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 外部代理贝叶斯不确定性量化可为黑盒VLM视觉 grounding 提供更可靠校准与决策依据，并支撑主动、稳健的机器人 grounding。

Abstract: Visual grounding is crucial for robots to understand language instructions with visual observations. This paper addresses the problem of visual grounding uncertainties, where a robot uses a black-box vision-language model (VLM) to ground free-form natural-language instructions to target objects. Prevailing methods derive uncertainty from the VLM itself. Yet, outputs of the VLM, e.g., generated uncertainties, are from the same parameters and structures producing the predictions, and inherit the bias. We give a formal Bayesian definition of the problem that treats the VLM prediction as an observation. An external surrogate Bayesian model then quantifies per-query uncertainty over a local neighborhood of a held-out calibration set, decomposed into aleatoric and epistemic components. A hierarchical retrieval across input modalities realizes a compact joint-space neighborhood, mitigating the curse of dimensionality. The estimator further integrates with conformal prediction or model-based planning for active visual grounding, where robots are allowed to take verbal or physical actions to ground targets. Across grounding benchmarks and state-of-the-art VLMs, it consistently improves both calibration and task performance. On a real Fetch robot under open-world instructions, it enables principled trade-offs between confident action and information gathering, substantially improving robustness.

</details>


### [52] [Data-Driven or Model-Constrained? Comparing Airflow Estimation Methods under Sparse Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39789)
*Dominik Aigner,Thomas Wiedemann,Javier Monroy,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文在稀疏传感条件下系统比较四类气流估计器，评估物理先验对二维稳态流场估计的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气流平流是气体与气溶胶扩散的主要机制，准确估计气流对通风监测、气体源定位和污染物扩散分析至关重要；但高分辨率空间传感通常不可行，因此需借助统计或物理先验从稀疏测量中补偿缺失信息。现有方法在引入流体约束和环境几何上差异较大，却缺乏系统性比较，物理先验在稀疏感知下的潜在收益尚不明确。

Method: 在三种模拟二维稳态场景和不同采样密度下，定量评估四种气流估计器：高斯过程回归（GPR）、基于图的高斯马尔可夫随机场（GMRF），以及两种基于Navier-Stokes方程的方法，其中包括一种新的强形式残差最小化变体。

Result: 结果表明，物理先验在低采样密度下收益最大，可补偿缺失的空间信息；随着传感器覆盖增加，数据驱动的GPR竞争力增强，但基于模型的方法在所评估场景中仍保持精度优势。

Conclusion: 物理先验对稀疏传感下的气流估计具有显著价值，基于模型的方法整体上优于纯数据驱动方法；该研究为气流估计方法选择及物理先验利用提供了系统依据。

Abstract: Airflow-driven advection is the primary mechanism for gas and aerosol dispersion in real-world scenarios, making accurate airflow estimation essential for ventilation monitoring, gas source localization, and contaminant spread analysis. Since obtaining airflow maps via high-resolution spatial sensing is usually infeasible in practice, flow field estimation methods must cope with sparse measurements by introducing statistical or physical priors to compensate for limited data. Existing approaches mainly differ in how they incorporate fluid-flow constraints and environmental geometry. However, due to the lack of systematic comparisons across this spectrum of methods, the potential benefits of physics-based priors for airflow estimation under sparse sensing remain underexplored. To address this question, we quantitatively evaluate four airflow estimators across three simulated 2D scenarios under steady-state conditions and varying sampling densities. The methods range from Gaussian Process Regression and graph-based Gaussian Markov Random Fields to two Navier-Stokes-based approaches, including a novel strong-form residual minimization variant. Our results show that physical priors provide the greatest benefit at low sampling densities by compensating for missing spatial information. Although data-driven Gaussian Process Regression becomes increasingly competitive as sensor coverage increases, model-based estimators retain an accuracy advantage across the evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [53] [Teaching a Tree to Recover: Online Distillation of an LLM Supervisor with Counterfactual Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39775)
*Kristian Ceder,Kilian Freitag,Knut Åkesson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将大语言模型（LLM）监督者在线蒸馏为决策树，用于移动机器人局部MPC冻结时的恢复决策，测试阶段无需查询LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局规划器加局部模型预测控制（MPC）在复杂布局中受非凸避障和有限时域影响，机器人可能冻结；决定重规划、等待或求助属于监督决策。每次异常都查询LLM监督者既慢又贵，而手写规则难以覆盖物理机器人会遇到的长尾情况。

Method: 在线将LLM蒸馏为决策树。每个训练回合后，通过反事实回放将恢复动作标注为必要、不必要或有害，用这些标签淘汰无帮助规则。测试时由决策树处理，LLM仅用于新情况，经校验的恢复动作再转化为新规则。

Result: 在仿真中20个未见布局上，MPC单独会冻结；决策树在所有随机种子下至少正确处理18个，或到达目标，或在无可行路径时求助。决策树匹配始终查询LLM的监督者和专家设计规则，且测试时零LLM查询。

Conclusion: 在线蒸馏是可行方案：决策树处理熟悉情况，LLM仅咨询新情况；LLM不仅可作为运行时监督者，也可作为监督者的设计者。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots commonly pair a global planner with a local model predictive controller (MPC), but nonconvex obstacle avoidance and a limited horizon can make the robot freeze in complex layouts. Deciding whether to replan, wait, or call for help is a supervisory decision. Recent work queries a large language model (LLM) supervisor on every anomaly, which is slow and costly. We instead distill the LLM online into a decision tree. After each training episode, counterfactual replay labels recoveries as needed, unnecessary, or harmful. These labels retire unhelpful rules. In simulation, on 20 unseen layouts where the MPC alone freezes, the tree handles at least 18 correctly across all seeds, either by reaching the goal or by calling for help when no feasible path exists. The tree matches both an always-query LLM supervisor and expert-designed rules, while issuing no LLM queries at test time. Handwritten rules cannot anticipate the long tail of situations a physical robot will encounter. Online distillation is a promising remedy: the tree handles familiar cases, and the LLM is consulted only for new ones, whose validated recoveries become new rules. Language models can thus also serve as designers of supervisors rather than only as runtime supervisors.

</details>


### [54] [AFast Robot Localization Method Based on Monte Carlo Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39708)
*He Zhaoqing,Hang Li,Xian Pu,Li Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于蒙特卡洛采样的在线粒子滤波定位方法，仅利用前后左右四个测距传感器，在无航向信息条件下实现移动机器人在迷宫环境中的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能机器人定位通常依赖外部定位系统，但在真实受限环境中往往无法可靠使用外部定位，因此需要一种不依赖外部系统、传感器配置简单的自主定位方法。

Method: 采用在线粒子滤波与蒙特卡洛采样进行定位，面向移动机器人在迷宫环境中的定位任务；机器人仅安装前、后、左、右四个测距传感器，不使用任何航向信息。

Result: 仿真实验表明，即使迷宫局部区域高度相似，车辆仍能获得相对准确的定位结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂环境中的机器人定位提供了一种快速、有效且传感器配置简单的解决方案。

Abstract: Localization is an essential capability of intelligent robots. However, in real-world constrained environments, robots often cannot rely on external localization systems. Therefore, this paper proposes an online particle-filter localization method based on Monte Carlo sampling. The method is applied to the localization of a mobile robot in a maze environment and requires only four range sensors mounted on the front, rear, left, and right sides of the vehicle, without the need for any heading information. Simulation experiments demonstrate that even in a maze where local regions are highly similar, the vehicle can still achieve relatively accurate localization, thereby providing a fast and effective approach for robot localization in complex environments.

</details>


### [55] [AI for Science in Space: Orbital Laboratories, Planetary Fieldwork, and Embodied Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39689)
*Zizhe Wang,Jia Deng,Xin Lu,Zehao Jia,Yi Huang,Yao Zhu,Jianing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述主张将空间AI4S从数据解释推进到实验设计、执行与证据有效性维护，并以有效科学回报、任务覆盖、干预成本和证据完整性衡量自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间微重力与外星环境提供地面难以复现的科学现象，但样本有限、资源受限、干预机会少，AI4S需从分析现有数据扩展到自主开展实验。

Method: 批判性综述轨道实验室与行星原位科学，覆盖生物学、材料、流体与热物理及自适应探索；区分空间数据学习、科学行动选择、物理执行与实验有效性保持，并借鉴地面自驱动实验室，提出连接样品历史、科学决策、物理执行和主张特定有效性的框架，辅以轨道设计案例、行星扩展和匹配评估方案。

Result: 有界AI系统已显示科学收益，但在观测、可执行动作和有效证据连续性方面仍存缺口；空间机器人可在必要干预超出经验证仪器能力且无及时人工支持时扩展自主科学范围，但其价值须与专用机制、冗余和人工支持运行相比较。

Conclusion: 未来议程应通过完整任务链中的有效科学回报、任务覆盖、干预成本和证据完整性来评估自主性，并分离传感、决策策略与物理行动的贡献。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence for science (AI4S) is extending from interpretation of existing data to the design and execution of experiments. Space offers distinctive scientific opportunities while restricting physical support. Microgravity and extraterrestrial environments expose phenomena that terrestrial experiments cannot fully reproduce; finite samples, constrained resources, and limited opportunities for intervention complicate sustained investigation. This critical review examines orbital laboratories and planetary in situ science across biology, materials, fluid and thermal physics, and adaptive exploration. We distinguish learning from space-derived data, selecting scientific actions, executing them, and preserving experimental validity. Terrestrial self-driving laboratories provide complementary evidence on optimization, dedicated automation, robotic manipulation, and the support needed for unattended operation. The synthesis connects scientific gains already demonstrated by bounded AI systems to gaps in observation, executable actions, and continuity of valid evidence. Space robots can expand the attainable scope of autonomous science when necessary interventions exceed validated instrument capabilities and timely human support is unavailable. Their value must be established against dedicated mechanisms, redundancy, and human-supported operation. We develop a framework connecting sample history, scientific decisions, physical execution, and claim-specific validity. A worked orbital design, a planetary extension, and matched evaluation schemes separate the contributions of sensing, decision policies, and physical actions. The resulting agenda measures autonomy through valid scientific return, task coverage, intervention costs, and evidence integrity across complete campaigns.

</details>


### [56] [Kinematic-Tactile Aligned Dexterous Retargeting with Contact Wrench Space Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39582)
*Xinyuan He,Ziheng Xu,Yueyuan Chen,Yufan Ye,Yuanshuo Tan,Huiming Pan,Jianjun Meng,Shuo Jiang,Peter B. Shull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层三阶段的手部运动学-触觉对齐重定向框架，利用以物体为中心的接触力旋量空间将人类演示中的运动学与触觉信息迁移到机器人灵巧手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重定向方法主要迁移人类运动学，物理交互背后的触觉信息很少在不同本体间保留，限制了接触丰富操作策略的学习。

Method: 框架先对齐人手与机器人手的运动学和接触几何，再根据触觉力与接触几何构建人类力旋量支撑剖面；随后优化机器人指尖力目标以覆盖该支撑剖面，并转换为关节位置修正，生成可直接回放的机器人手轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人三种倾倒条件下评估；真实机器人实验中回放成功率达96.7%，力旋量迁移保真度高于代表性重定向基线。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了从人类演示到机器人手的物理 grounded 运动学与触觉信息迁移，为可扩展学习接触丰富的灵巧操作提供了路径。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide a natural and scalable source of data for learning dexterous manipulation policies. However, existing retargeting methods primarily transfer human kinematics, while the tactile information underlying physical interaction is rarely preserved across embodiments. To address this limitation, we present a hierarchical three-stage framework for kinematic-tactile aligned dexterous retargeting. We use object-centric contact wrench space as a physical representation for tactile alignment between human and robot hands. The framework first aligns hand kinematics and contact geometry, and then constructs a human wrench support profile from tactile forces and contact geometry. Robot fingertip force targets are subsequently optimized to cover the human wrench support and converted into joint position corrections, yielding directly replayable robot hand trajectories. We evaluate the framework in simulation and real robot experiments across three pouring conditions. In the real robot experiments, our method achieves a 96.7\% replay success rate and higher wrench transfer fidelity than representative retargeting baselines. The framework enables physically grounded transfer of kinematic and tactile information from human demonstrations to robot hands, providing a path toward scalable learning of contact-rich dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [57] [SimTO-Robust: Automated Design of Soft Grippers Robust to Grasp Pose and Object Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39560)
*Kurt Enkera,Joshua Pinskier,Marcus Gallagher,David Howard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SimTO-Robust，将多物体、多抓取位姿的仿真抓取接触力聚合进拓扑优化，以机械形态设计提升软体夹爪在非结构化环境中的鲁棒性，并通过大规模设计与物理实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的机器人夹爪需在感知和定位误差下稳定抓取不同形状与尺寸的物体；现有软体夹爪计算设计框架未显式优化跨物体几何与抓取位姿的鲁棒性。

Method: 将仿真驱动拓扑优化SimTO推广为SimTO-Robust：在多个物体和抓取位姿上仿真抓取，聚合接触力以指导夹爪形态优化；生成6,646个设计，物理评估其中24个，并与10个专用SimTO设计、2个刚性基线夹爪及1个传统拓扑优化通用软体夹爪对比；37个夹爪在含5个特征丰富物体、6种抓取位姿及递增外部扰动的拾放任务中测试。

Result: 平均而言，SimTO-Robust设计完成拾放任务比专用SimTO设计和刚性基线更可靠；最佳SimTO-Robust设计承受的外部扰动多于其他所有测试夹爪。

Conclusion: 将多样抓取条件纳入仿真驱动拓扑优化是提升真实世界软体夹爪鲁棒性的简单有效方法；更广泛地表明，鲁棒机器人抓取可通过智能机械设计而非复杂感知与控制实现。

Abstract: Robotic grippers operating in unstructured environments must robustly grasp objects of different shapes and sizes despite perception and positioning errors. Soft grippers that can achieve this robustness through intelligent mechanical design rather than sophisticated control software offer a promising solution. However, no existing computational design framework explicitly optimises soft gripper morphology for robustness across object geometries and grasp poses. We introduce SimTO-Robust, a generalisation of simulation-driven topology optimisation (SimTO) that simulates grasps across multiple objects and poses and aggregates their contact forces to guide gripper morphology optimisation. We generate 6,646 designs and physically evaluate 24 of them alongside 10 specialised SimTO designs, two rigid baseline grippers, and a baseline generalist soft gripper produced using conventional topology optimisation. All 37 grippers are tested in a pick-and-place task involving five feature-rich objects and six grasp poses, with external disturbances of increasing magnitude applied during the task. On average, SimTO-Robust designs complete pick-and-place more reliably than specialised SimTO designs and the rigid baselines. The best SimTO-Robust designs also withstood more of the applied disturbances than any other tested gripper. These results show that incorporating diverse grasp conditions into simulation-driven topology optimisation is a simple and effective approach to improving real-world soft gripper robustness. More broadly, they show that robust robotic grasping can be achieved through intelligent mechanical design rather than sophisticated sensing and control.

</details>


### [58] [Assuring Learning-Enabled Cyber-Physical Systems with Foundation Models: A Neuro-Symbolic Agenda for Testing, Verification, and Runtime Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39547)
*Xi Zheng,Ivan Ruchkin,Hong Jin Kang,Tevfik Bultan,Vijay Ganesh,Taylor T. Johnson,Ziyang Li,Lei Ma,Aloysius K. Mok,Daniel Neider,Ruzica Piskac,Nalini Venkatasubramanian,Simon Thompson,Sanjoy Baruah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文主张：面向含基础模型的学习使能信息物理系统（CPS），应从模型中心评估转向系统级保障，并提出“基础模型保障栈”作为连接神经符号架构、语义接地一致性测试、组合式验证与运行时保障的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶、无人机等学习使能CPS越来越多地集成基础模型，但现有保障实践碎片化，难以提供系统级保证；随机神经网络、符号推理与物理系统边界之间缺乏工程控制与可组合证据。

Method: 提出基础模型保障栈，包含三条方向：(i) 用显式符号世界模型、推理层和接口约束基础模型的神经符号架构；(ii) 结合形式规范、符号世界模型与基础模型辅助的规范挖掘、场景生成和测试合成的语义接地、基于一致性（conformance）的测试；(iii) 通过模块化契约、等价性检查、经验证的综合、不确定性感知推理及运行时监控与执行，对形式化可处理组件和接口进行组合式验证与运行时保障。符号抽象用于建立随机学习与形式推理之间的保障边界，贯穿测试、验证和运行时证据。

Result: 论文提出概念性保障框架与研究议程，而非报告实证结果；其核心机制是将基础模型作为受约束的助手，在可形式化指定正确性的地方对其输出进行独立检查，并识别规范工程、抽象保真度、证据组合和生命周期认证等研究挑战。

Conclusion: 实现可信、可部署且可认证的学习使能CPS，需要以系统级保障替代孤立的模型评估，通过符号抽象和组合式证据连接测试、验证与运行时保障，并按阶段推进相关研究。

Abstract: Learning-enabled cyber-physical systems (CPS), including autonomous vehicles and UAVs, increasingly incorporate foundation models, yet fragmented assurance practices provide insufficient support for system-level guarantees. We argue that restoring engineering control across stochastic neural, symbolic, and physical boundaries requires a shift from model-centric evaluation to system-level assurance. We introduce the Foundation Model Assurance Stack, connecting three directions: (i) neuro-symbolic architectures that constrain foundation models through explicit symbolic world models, reasoning layers, and interfaces; (ii) semantically grounded, conformance-based testing that combines formal specifications and symbolic world models with foundation-model-assisted specification mining, scenario generation, and test synthesis; and (iii) compositional verification and runtime assurance that target formally tractable components and interfaces through modular contracts, equivalence checking, verified synthesis, uncertainty-aware reasoning, and runtime monitoring and enforcement. Symbolic abstractions establish assurance boundaries between stochastic learning and formal reasoning, connecting testing, verification, and runtime evidence across the system lifecycle. Foundation models serve as constrained assistants whose outputs are independently checked wherever correctness can be formally specified. We identify research challenges in specification engineering, abstraction fidelity, evidence composition, and lifecycle certification, and outline a staged agenda toward trustworthy, deployable, and certifiable learning-enabled CPS.

</details>


### [59] [From Reasoning to Action: Consequence-Grounded Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39543)
*Yixue Zhang,Wenke Xia,Kun Wu,Wenbo Yu,Pei Ren,Junnan Nie,Di Hu,Zhengping Che,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoRAct 提出一种以后果为依据的推理到动作对齐框架，通过在动作噪声空间进行奖励引导优化，显式对齐具身思维链推理与动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽引入 ECoT 指导动作生成，但指导是隐式的：动作仅以 ECoT 为条件，并未显式优化以与其对齐。

Method: CoRAct 将两类物理 grounded 的 ECoT 组件作为可验证信号：movement 表示意图运动，waypoints 编码预期空间后果；用统一 Mixture-of-Transformers 联合建模 ECoT 推理、动作生成和动作条件未来预测；对从采样噪声生成的动作候选，基于运动一致性和预测结果与 waypoints 的一致性计算对齐奖励，并用组相对优化更新初始噪声分布。

Result: 在 RoboCasa、VLABench 和真实世界操作任务上，CoRAct 相较强 ECoT-based VLA 基线提升了任务成功率、泛化能力和推理-执行对齐；还能将外部提供的 ECoT grounded 到可执行行为，借助强大 VLM 知识实现对新场景的零样本适应。

Conclusion: 显式奖励引导的推理-动作对齐能有效提升 VLA 的任务表现与执行一致性，并支持外部 ECoT 的可执行落地和零样本迁移。

Abstract: Reasoning informs decision-making by connecting intended actions with their expected consequences. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models incorporate embodied chains of thought (ECoTs) to guide action generation. However, such guidance remains implicit, as actions are conditioned on ECoTs rather than explicitly optimized to align with them. We introduce CoRAct, a consequence-grounded reasoning-to-action alignment framework that explicitly aligns reasoning and action through reward-guided optimization in action noise space. CoRAct formulates two physically grounded ECoT components as verifiable signals: movement specifies intended motion, while waypoints encode expected spatial consequences. A unified Mixture-of-Transformers jointly models ECoT reasoning, action generation, and action-conditioned future prediction. For candidate actions generated from sampled noise, CoRAct computes alignment rewards based on movement consistency and agreement between predicted outcomes and specified waypoints. These rewards guide group-relative optimization of the initial noise distribution, steering action generation toward consistency with intended actions and expected consequences. Experiments across RoboCasa, VLABench, and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate improvements in task success, generalization, and reasoning-execution alignment over strong ECoT-based VLA baselines. CoRAct also grounds externally provided ECoTs in executable behavior, enabling zero-shot adaptation to novel scenarios by leveraging knowledge from powerful vision-language models. Project page and dataset: this https URL

</details>


### [60] [PRIME W0: Physical Priors from World Models for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39528)
*Huabin Peng,Fagang Jin,Yancong Wei,Tengfei Ma,Chuhang Liu,Chao Yu,Liqing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIME W0 是一个分层条件流匹配 VLA 框架，通过层间 KV 注入、世界模型子目标条件与动作动态流匹配，弥补现有 VLA 仅依赖当前观测的前馈缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Vision-Language-Action 模型多为前馈式，仅以当前观测为条件，丢弃长时程时间上下文与 VLM 表示的分层结构，限制复杂长程任务表现。

Method: 提出三项协同设计：(1) 将预训练 VLM 的逐层 KV 注入自适应 MoE 动作专家；(2) 通过异步世界模型子目标图像预测与 DINOv2 历史帧语义桥实现动力学感知条件；(3) 采用协方差结构化噪声的 Action Dynamic Manifold 流匹配以尊重机器人运动学耦合。训练分四阶段：VLM 预训练、DAT/NTP 视觉运动预训练、带世界模型注入的 DiT 流匹配解码器组装、端到端 SFT。

Result: 在 LIBERO 与真实机器人基准上取得 state-of-the-art 结果，并通过系统性消融验证各架构组件的有效性。

Conclusion: PRIME W0 通过分层条件流匹配有效引入长时程时序上下文与 VLM 分层表示，为前馈 VLA 的时序与结构建模提供可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at instruction following, yet most remain feed-forward: they condition actions only on the current observation and discard both long-horizon temporal context and the hierarchical structure of VLM representations. We present PRIME W0, a hierarchical conditional flow-matching framework that closes this gap through three synergistic designs: (1)per-layer KV injection from a pretrained VLM into an Adaptive MoE action expert; (2)dynamics-aware conditioning via asynchronous world-model subgoal-image prediction and a DINOv2 historical-frame semantic bridge; and (3)Action Dynamic Manifold (ADM) flow matching with covariance-structured noise that respects robot kinematic coupling. PRIME W0 is trained in four stages---VLM pre-training, DAT/NTP visuomotor pre-training, DiT flow-matching decoder assembly with WM injection, and end-to-end SFT---and achieves state-of-the-art results on LIBERO and real-robot benchmarks, with systematic ablations validating each architectural component.

</details>


### [61] [Ask to Evolve: A Unified Harness for Lifelong Embodied Navigation with Hybrid Action Space and Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39446)
*Kai Sheng,Liuyi Wang,Jinlong Li,Haojie Dai,Chengju Liu,Qijun Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EvoLife 提出统一的具身导航框架，将多模态智能体的动作选择、主动交互与持久空间记忆结合，在多种导航任务中实现高成功率并验证经验复用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让具身智能体自主行动、主动发起交互并从经验中理解环境，需要动作、交互和记忆协同，使当前决策受益于过往经历并为未来任务积累经验。

Method: 构建 EvoLife 统一导航框架：采用混合动作空间支持航点导航、局部离散调整、像素级导航和目标导向导航；允许智能体在歧义或困难时主动请求澄清/帮助；并用持久空间记忆将观测与对话绑定到地点，以保留和复用经验。

Result: 在4类任务家族的8个基准上评估。每回合平均仅1.87次主动交互，在覆盖视觉语言导航和目标导航的4组100回合集合上首次访问成功率达100%；在50条LHPR-VLN和50条GOAT-Bench序列上完整序列成功率分别为98%和94%；R2R重访设置中保留经验使SPL从82.23%提升至93.31%；多日物理导航展示了探索、主动求助和重访记忆地点能力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，自主行动、主动交互以及经验的积累与复用可显著提升具身导航表现，并推进具身智能体在物理世界中“活起来”的愿景。

Abstract: Giving embodied agents the freedom to act autonomously, initiate interactions, and understand their surroundings through experience is a step toward bringing them to life in the physical world. Realizing this vision requires action, interaction, and memory to work together, so that each encounter can inform current decisions and contribute experience for future tasks. We present EvoLife, a unified navigation harness that enables a multimodal embodied agent to choose how to move, when to ask, and what experience to carry forward. Its hybrid action space supports waypoint navigation, local discrete adjustments, pixel-based navigation, and object-directed navigation. Active interaction allows the agent to seek clarification or assistance when it encounters ambiguity or difficulty, while persistent spatial memory binds observations and dialogue to places, retaining useful experience to guide subsequent visits. We evaluate eight benchmarks across four task families: vision-language navigation, object navigation, interactive instance navigation, and continuous multi-task navigation. With an average of only 1.87 active interactions per episode, our method achieves a first-visit success rate (SR) of 100% across four 100-episode sets spanning vision-language navigation and object navigation. Complete-sequence SR reaches 98% on 50 LHPR-VLN sequences and 94% on 50 GOAT-Bench sequences. In the R2R revisit setting, success weighted by path length (SPL) increases from 82.23% to 93.31% with retained experience. Multi-day physical navigation further demonstrates exploration, proactive help-seeking, and revisiting remembered places. Together, these results advance the vision of bringing embodied agents to life through autonomous action, proactive interaction, and the accumulation and reuse of experience.

</details>


### [62] [ScheduleGen: Demonstration Generation via Skill Scheduling for Multi-Arm Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39429)
*Neel Jawale,Stephan Pleines,Soha Pouya,Yuke Zhu,Ajay Mandlekar,Caelan Garrett*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ScheduleGen，一种面向多臂机器人演示生成的规划与调度一体化算法，通过显式处理多臂约束提升演示生成安全性与成功率，并训练可迁移的多臂操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂平台（双臂、人形）虽能缩短任务周期，但多臂约束使其安全控制更难；训练通用多臂策略依赖大规模演示数据，而现有自动演示生成方法忽略多臂约束，常导致不安全或失败行为。

Method: ScheduleGen 将规划与调度结合，用于多臂演示生成，从少量源演示合成新演示，并在生成过程中考虑多臂约束。

Result: 在28个仿真任务变体（含7个外部RoboDojo任务及先前系统无法处理的设置）上，平均数据生成成功率为79.6%，训练策略平均成功率为65.5%；优于最先进数据生成基线，并在8个真实世界变体中通过零样本sim-to-real及real-to-sim-to-real迁移达到50-80%成功率。

Conclusion: 显式建模多臂约束的规划与调度可安全、有效地扩展多臂演示数据生成，并支持多臂通用策略的训练与真实世界迁移。

Abstract: Multi-arm robot platforms, including bimanual and humanoid robots, have the capability to complete tasks with lower cycle times than single-arm systems, but they are more difficult to safely control due to the presence of multi-arm constraints. The paradigm of training generalist robot policies from massive datasets is a promising approach to address general-purpose multi-arm manipulation, but the acquisition of large multi-arm manipulation datasets remains a bottleneck. Automated demonstration generation can alleviate this burden by synthesizing new demonstrations from a handful of source demonstrations, but existing algorithms ignore multi-arm constraints and as a result often induce unsafe and unsuccessful behaviors. We address this limitation by introducing ScheduleGen, an integrated planning & scheduling algorithm for multi-arm demonstration generation. We evaluate ScheduleGen on 28 simulation task variants, including seven externally authored RoboDojo tasks and settings inaccessible to prior systems; it achieves 79.6% mean data-generation success and trains policies with 65.5% mean success. ScheduleGen policies outperform those trained on a state-of-the-art data generation baseline, and achieve 50-80% success across eight real-world variants through zero-shot sim-to-real and real-to-sim-to-real transfer. Videos and more at: this https URL

</details>


### [63] [Decoding Geometry, Not Tokens: Efficient VLMs for Robot Affordance and Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39416)
*Akila Ayanthi,Darshana Priyasad,Tharindu Fernando,Dimity Miller,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes,Peyman Moghadam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出VLM将抓取与轨迹预测当作坐标文本生成是主要瓶颈，并提出冻结主干、轻量几何预测头直接从中间隐藏状态回归连续2D几何量，在精度与效率间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型将空间坐标分词为文本并自回归解码，忽视空间结构、截断连续精度，且坐标token误差会累积；作者希望验证紧凑VLM隐藏状态是否已含丰富空间信息，并分离自回归解码瓶颈。

Method: 在0.5–2B参数的冻结VLM主干上附加轻量、几何兼容的预测头：读取中间隐藏层特征，以任务指令条件化可学习query，并通过视觉token交叉注意力直接回归affordance框和轨迹路点等连续值，不修改主干。

Result: 在ShareRobot-Bench和AGD20K上评估；2B变体相比自回归基线将轨迹误差降低60%以上，affordance mAP提升85%；轨迹预测接近RoboBrain（DFD 0.251 vs 0.191），吞吐量高3.8倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，紧凑VLM的中间空间表征可被轻量几何头有效利用，为指令条件化的2D几何预测提供了有利的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Predicting where to grasp and how to move is fundamentally a geometry problem. Vision-language models treat it as language generation instead. They tokenize spatial coordinates into text and decode them autoregressively. This mechanism ignores spatial structure and truncates continuous precision. Errors compound across coordinate tokens during decoding. In this paper, we show that compact off-the-shelf VLMs with 0.5-2B parameters already encode rich spatial information in their intermediate hidden states. Autoregressive decoding is the dominant bottleneck for trajectory prediction, while affordance prediction depends additionally on feature depth and decoder architecture. We expose the latent spatial information with a lightweight, geometry-compatible prediction head that attaches to a frozen backbone. It reads features from an intermediate hidden layer, conditions a learned query on the task instruction and refines it via cross-attention over the visual tokens to regress affordance boxes and trajectory waypoints directly as continuous values, without modifying the backbone. We evaluate on ShareRobot-Bench and AGD20K. Our 2B variant reduces trajectory error by over 60% and improves ShareRobot-Bench affordance mAP by 85% relative to the autoregressive baseline. It approaches RoboBrain on trajectory prediction (0.251 vs 0.191 DFD) at 3.8x higher throughput. These results demonstrate a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off for instruction-conditioned 2D geometric prediction.

</details>


### [64] [UMI-Live for In-The-Wild Bimanual Robotics Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39385)
*Govind Velamoor,Adrian Lin,Sam Jung,Spencer Mateega,Brandon Y. Yang,Amish Sethi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-Live 是一个全栈开源的双臂 UMI 系统，用于野外数据采集、双臂遥操作以及 VLA 策略训练与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMI 设备可让专家无需遥操作真实机器人即可演示任务，但将演示数据用于机器人学习仍依赖一致的硬件配置、标定和数据处理流程。

Method: 设计集成相机、LiDAR-惯性传感、板载计算与供电的定制 UMI-Live 硬件，无需外部跟踪设备或采集环境内标定；提供简单录制界面，并以异步流水线并行执行 SLAM 重建和发布可直接训练的数据集；同时发布支持双 YAM 遥操作及 VLA 策略训练与推理的软件。

Result: 通过微调 π0.5 和 MolmoAct2，在双臂抓取放置任务上分别达到 10/10 和 8/10 的成功率；发布覆盖三个任务、共 40 小时的 UMI-Live 数据，以及完整硬件设计、软件和复现指南。

Conclusion: UMI-Live 降低了双臂野外数据采集与 VLA 训练部署的门槛，并提供了可复现的开源硬件、软件和数据资源。

Abstract: Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) devices allow human experts to demonstrate tasks for robot learning without teleoperating physical robots. Using these demonstrations as training data still requires a consistent hardware setup, calibration, and data processing. We present UMI-Live, a full-stack open-source bimanual UMI system for in-the-wild data collection. We design our custom UMI-Live hardware to integrate camera and LiDAR--inertial sensing, onboard compute, and power, without external tracking equipment or calibration within the collection environment. We implement a simple user interface for recording data while an asynchronous pipeline performs parallel SLAM reconstruction and publishes training-ready datasets. We also release software that supports dual-YAM teleoperation and VLA policy training and inference. We validate UMI-Live data quality by fine-tuning $\pi_{0.5}$ and MolmoAct2, achieving success rates of 10/10 and 8/10, respectively on a bimanual pick-and-place task. Furthermore, we release 40 hours of UMI-Live data across three tasks, and complete hardware designs, software, and reproduction guides at our $\href{ this https URL }{\text{project website}}$.

</details>


### [65] [Information-Driven Collaborative Autonomy for Heterogeneous UAV/UGV Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39379)
*Joshua Yuriv Pilipovsky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向未知数量潜在危险目标的异构无人机（UAV）与无人地面车（UGV）协同搜索与路径规划框架，实现空中感知、地面风险评估和分散式闭环轨迹优化的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在包含未知数量潜在威胁目标的区域中，UAV与UGV需要协同完成搜索、目标定位与安全通行；现有方法难以同时处理动态感知几何、目标后验不确定性、机会约束避障以及多智能体分散式连续时间协调。

Method: 论文将空中搜索建模为遍历控制（ergodic control），依据空间信息密度分配感知努力以平衡探索与目标定位，并纳入动态相机视场足迹和位姿相关概率检测模型；对仅角度测量进行贝叶斯推断，并对数据关联假设边缘化，得到检测与未检测目标的泊松多伯努利（PMB）后验近似，以引导UAV感知。地面规划结合基于置信集的机会约束目标规避与空洞概率（void-probability）目标，偏好低威胁路线。各团队优化问题采用完全分散式的嵌套SCvx*--C-ADMM求解：外层为序列凸规划，内层为分布式共识优化，处理非线性、不可分目标与连续时间智能体间分离约束；闭环方案随信息更新双方轨迹，并在剩余路线风险低于阈值时允许地面运动。

Result: 数值案例研究了感知几何与目标不确定性，比较了分散式与集中式求解器，并展示了多UAV与多UGV之间的完整闭环协同。

Conclusion: 该框架能够利用UAV空中感知引导UGV安全规划，并通过分散式优化实现异构空地团队在不确定危险环境中的闭环协同搜索与通行。

Abstract: This paper considers coordinating a heterogeneous team of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) and unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) traversing a domain containing an unknown number of potentially hazardous targets. The UAVs provide aerial sensing using onboard optical cameras, adapting their trajectories to ground routes and target information. We formulate the aerial search problem using ergodic control, which distributes sensing effort according to a spatial information density to balance exploration and target localization. The formulation incorporates dynamic camera field-of-view footprints and pose-dependent probabilistic detection models. Angles-only measurements are processed through Bayesian inference and marginalization over data-association hypotheses to obtain a Poisson multi-Bernoulli (PMB) approximation of the target posterior, representing both detected and undetected targets. This belief directs aerial sensing toward unexplored regions along the ground routes and viewpoints that improve target localization. Ground planning combines chance-constrained target avoidance, enforced through confidence sets, with a void-probability objective that favors routes unlikely to contain threats. We solve each team's optimization problem in a fully decentralized manner using a nested SCvx*--C-ADMM scheme, with outer sequential convex programming iterations and inner distributed consensus optimization, accounting for nonlinearities, nonseparable objectives, and continuous-time inter-agent separation constraints. A closed-loop scheme updates both fleets' trajectories as information becomes available and permits ground motion when the remaining route risk falls below a prescribed threshold. Numerical case studies examine sensing geometry and target uncertainty, compare decentralized and centralized solvers, and demonstrate full closed-loop collaboration between multiple UAVs and UGVs.

</details>


### [66] [Utility-WAM: Correcting Future-Induced Action Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39367)
*Feihong Zhang,Xi Lin,Yulong Shi,Zuxing Lu,Yanghong Mei,Zhaopeng Gu,Tingyu Yuan,Zihao Liang,Yinuo Wang,Zhaowen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文系统分析世界动作模型（WAM）中“预测视觉未来”对机器人动作的实际影响，发现其平均收益有限且推理成本高，并会引发精度任务上的“未来诱导负迁移”；提出 Utility-WAM，通过残差适配器修正未来条件化速度增量，并用预生成门控按需跳过视频生成，在保持长程收益的同时显著降低延迟和失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 可用生成的视觉未来作为动作条件，但该额外上下文并非无成本且非普遍有益；现有聚合指标掩盖了其对不同任务类型的差异化影响，尤其是精度任务上的严重退化。

Method: 在同一 WAM 检查点下比较未来条件化与无未来路径的动作增量 Δa；提出 Utility-WAM：在冻结共享 WAM 上训练残差适配器，将未来诱导的速度增量与真实动态引导对齐，并惩罚超过无未来基线的额外误差；同时引入轻量预生成门控，复用反事实动作结果，选择性跳过视频生成。

Result: 未来条件化平均成功率变化很小（LIBERO +0.8，RoboTwin 2.0 -0.8），但延迟增至 1.7–1.9 倍；长程任务提升 +2.4，精度任务 Press Stapler 和 Place Can Basket 分别下降 54.0 和 34.0。Utility-WAM 将二者损失降至 6.0 和 14.0，并在 RoboTwin 上跳过 75.5% 查询的视频生成、降低 38% 延迟，平均成功率达 93.3%（RoboTwin 2.0）和 98.9%（LIBERO）；真实双臂三任务 60 次试验成功 53 次，优于 FAST-WAM 的 43 次。

Conclusion: WAM 的视觉未来条件化存在任务相关的负迁移与计算冗余；Utility-WAM 通过原位残差修正和预生成门控，可选择性消除精度任务退化、保留长程预见性，并提升效率与真实机器人操作成功率。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) can condition robot actions on generated visual futures, but this additional context is neither cost-free nor uniformly beneficial. We examine what a predicted future actually changes: the action delta $\Delta a$ between the future-conditioned and future-free execution paths of the same model. Under a shared WAM checkpoint, this delta barely shifts average benchmark success ($+0.8$ points on LIBERO, $-0.8$ on RoboTwin~2.0), while generating visual futures increases inference latency by 1.7--1.9$\times$. These aggregate metrics conceal a concentrated failure mode that we term \emph{future-induced negative transfer}: while predictive deltas benefit long-horizon tasks ($+2.4$ points on LIBERO-Long) and remain negligible on most routine manipulations, they induce severe degradation on precision tasks---with \emph{Press Stapler} and \emph{Place Can Basket} losing 54.0 and 34.0 points, recurring as top failure modes across independently trained WAM variants. We introduce \textbf{Utility-WAM} for in-place correction of future-conditioned control. Its primary learning component is a residual adapter that repairs future-induced velocity deltas under a frozen shared WAM by aligning them with ground-truth dynamic guidance and penalizing excess error beyond the future-free baseline. The adapter selectively eliminates the majority of severe task drops (reducing losses to 6.0 and 14.0 points) while preserving long-horizon foresight. To eliminate superfluous video computation, a lightweight pre-generation gate reuses counterfactual action outcomes to selectively bypass video generation on 75.5\% of RoboTwin queries, cutting latency by 38\% while improving average success to 93.3\% on RoboTwin~2.0 and 98.9\% on LIBERO. On physical bimanual manipulation across three real-world tasks, Utility-WAM succeeds in 53 of 60 trials, compared to 43 for FAST-WAM.

</details>


### [67] [SONIC-K1: Porting a Universal Humanoid Motion Tracker to a Small Humanoid on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39327)
*Kyrylo Kolesnichenko,Jong-Hoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向Booster K1人形机器人的开放权重全身运动跟踪器SONIC-K1，通过将SONIC框架适配到更小型机器人并以低算力从零训练实现；仿真表现强，但实机步态与转向仍受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将SONIC框架迁移至更小的Booster K1人形机器人，在显著降低训练算力的同时提供开放权重、代码与评测工具，推动低成本全身运动跟踪研究。

Method: 在单张GPU上从零训练策略，将SONIC适配至K1；在MuJoCo仿真中评估held-out运动片段和外部基准，并在真实K1上测试上半身、慢速运动、 stepping与turning，同时分析动作延迟敏感性。

Result: 仿真中几乎跟踪所有held-out运动片段，并在SONIC成功准则下的外部基准上表现良好；真实K1可跟踪上半身和慢速运动，但stepping与turning较不可靠，仿真实验显示对action latency敏感。

Conclusion: 结果表明在适度训练预算下可实现广泛的全身运动跟踪能力；主要下一步是开展定量硬件评估，代码、策略权重和评测工具已公开。

Abstract: We present SONIC-K1, to our knowledge the first open-weights whole-body motion tracker for the Booster K1 humanoid, obtained by adapting the SONIC framework to this smaller robot. The policy is trained from scratch on a single shared GPU with a small fraction of the compute reported for SONIC. In MuJoCo simulation, it tracks nearly all held-out motion clips and performs well on external benchmarks under SONIC's success criterion. On the real K1, it tracks upper-body and slow motions, while stepping and turning remain less reliable, and simulation experiments show a sensitivity to action latency. These results show broad motion-tracking capability on a modest training budget and point to quantitative hardware evaluation as the main next step. Code, policy weights and evaluation tools are publicly available.

</details>


### [68] [ProLoop: Progress-Aware Closed-Loop Framework via Structured State Relations for Long-Horizon Robotic Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39319)
*Runxin Zou,Qingmiao Liang,Jiatao Zhang,Xinyan Lv,Liangyu Qie,Meiyi Bu,Wei Song,Shiqiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProLoop 是一个进度感知的闭环框架，通过显式估计长程机器人任务中子目标进度来提升 LLM 规划执行的可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程机器人任务通常分解为语义子目标以简化规划，但基于 LLM 的规划多从交互历史隐式推断子目标进度；不准确的进度估计会误导执行，并使局部错误传播。

Method: ProLoop 从期望状态与执行状态之间的结构化关系中显式推导子目标进度：学习一个 State-Relation Monitor 评估状态关系，并将其聚合为 ongoing、deviated 或 completed 的子目标进度，进而指导执行自适应，实现进度感知的闭环控制。

Result: 在 AgentBoard ALFWorld 和真实机器人平台上的实验表明，ProLoop 总体成功率提升 12.94%，Hard 任务提升 15.46%，同时减少 32.3% 的环境交互。

Conclusion: 显式状态关系监控与子目标进度聚合能够有效抑制局部错误传播，提升长程机器人任务的成功率和执行效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic tasks are commonly decomposed into semantic subgoals to simplify planning. However, subgoal progress in LLM-based planning is typically inferred implicitly from interaction histories. Inaccurate estimates can misguide execution and allow local errors to propagate. To address this, we propose ProLoop, a Progress-Aware Closed-Loop framework that derives explicit subgoal progress from structured relations between desired and execution states. A learned State-Relation Monitor assesses these relations, which are then aggregated into subgoal progress as ongoing, deviated, or completed. The resulting progress guides execution adaptation, enabling reliable progress-aware control throughout execution. Experiments on AgentBoard ALFWorld and a real-world robotic platform show that ProLoop improves success rate by 12.94% overall and by 15.46% on Hard tasks, while reducing environment interactions by 32.3%.

</details>


### [69] [Language-Grounded Force/Torque Pretraining for Extrinsic Contact Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39314)
*Kazuki Takahashi,Hiroaki Murakami,Ryota Sakuma,Mitsuhiro Kamezaki,Matthew Ishige,Yoshihiro Kawahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将机器人双侧六轴力/力矩历史预训练为语义结构化物理表征：通过仿真抓放监督与冻结语言空间对齐，实现对已知物理状态的直接读出，并能高效适配新的外在接触事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细操作需要机器人识别抓取物体何时与环境发生或改变接触，但外在接触难以由视觉或局部接触几何直接观测；力/力矩信号虽包含相关信息，却会随物体重量、抓取构型和机器人运动变化，因此其是否能形成可复用的物理状态表征尚不明确。

Method: 作者使用仿真抓放交互生成监督，将双侧六轴力/力矩历史与冻结语言空间对齐，预训练出语义结构化的物理表征；随后在仿真未见物体上以文本原型读出机器人动作、物体重量和抓取交互，并在真实放置任务中用无标签校准识别接触与支撑状态，且不更新编码器；针对预训练未包含的连接器插入事件，采用低秩适配进行数据高效学习。

Result: 在仿真未见物体上，该表征支持对机器人动作、物体重量和抓取交互的文本原型读出；在真实世界放置中，无标签校准可在不更新编码器的情况下识别接触和支撑状态，并优于垂直力符号反转规则基线；在连接器插入任务中，低秩适配相比从头训练显著提升数据效率。

Conclusion: 力/力矩历史可作为可复用的物理状态表征，既支持对已知物理状态的直接读出，也支持对新外在接触事件的高效适配，表明其在机器人精细操作中的 extrinsic contact 感知方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Fine manipulation requires robots to recognize when a grasped object makes or changes contact with its environment, yet such extrinsic contact is not always directly observable from vision or local contact geometry. Force and torque signals carry this information, but also vary with object weight, grasp configuration, and robot motion, leaving it unclear whether they can provide a reusable representation of physical state. Here we show that bilateral six-axis force/torque histories can be pretrained as a semantically structured physical representation by aligning them with a frozen language space using supervision generated from simulated pick-and-place interactions. On unseen objects in simulation, the learned representation supports text-prototype readout of robot action, object weight, and grasp interaction. In real-world placement, unlabeled calibration enables contact and support-state recognition without updating the encoder, outperforming a vertical-force sign-reversal rule baseline. For connector insertion, an event absent from pretraining, low-rank adaptation improves data-efficient learning over training from scratch. These results show that force/torque histories can support both direct readout of known physical states and efficient adaptation to new extrinsic-contact events.

</details>


### [70] [OpenFlyV2: An onboard vision-language-action model for aerial perception and navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39287)
*Junli Liu,Zhongrui You,Chenhui Li,Zhen Li,Yuyang Tian,Qizhen Chen,Delin Qu,Haoming Song,Dong Wang,Xuelong Li,Zhigang Wang,Bin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenFlyV2 是一种统一的机载视觉-语言-动作架构，用共享视觉-语言表征同时支持语义理解、语言定位感知、空间推理与连续飞行控制；在 Jetson Orin NX 上以 10 Hz 机载生成 12 路点动作块，并从仿真零样本迁移到真实四旋翼，在四个真实环境中达到 48% 导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类认知可基于共享感知基础灵活完成理解、注意、空间推理与行动；现有空中机器人系统通常能力单一、依赖机外计算，或难以在资源受限平台上维持闭环运行，因此需要统一且机载的视觉-语言-动作模型。

Method: 提出 OpenFlyV2 统一机载 VLA 架构：共享视觉-语言表征支撑语义理解、语言 grounded 感知、空间推理与连续控制；紧凑动作专家将多模态上下文转换为短时域飞行路点；运动感知时序记忆与动作先验降低闭环执行计算成本；构建约 7 万条导航轨迹和 6 万条视觉-语言指令-响应对的仿真数据集。

Result: 模型在 NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX 上完全机载以 10 Hz 生成每块 12 个路点的动作块；无需真实空中训练数据即可从仿真零样本迁移到真实四旋翼，在四个真实环境中取得 48% 导航成功率，并验证广泛视觉-语言能力与连续闭环飞行可在单一模型中并存。

Conclusion: OpenFlyV2 表明，在资源受限的空中平台上，单一模型可同时具备广泛视觉-语言能力与连续闭环飞行控制能力；代码将公开。

Abstract: Human cognition flexibly draws on a shared perceptual foundation to understand environments, attend to referenced entities, reason about spatial relationships, and act. Endowing aerial robots with an analogous functional organization remains challenging, as existing systems are often specialized for individual capabilities, dependent on offboard computation, or unable to sustain closed-loop operation on resource-constrained platforms. We present OpenFlyV2, a unified onboard vision-language-action architecture that uses a shared vision-language representation to support semantic understanding, language-grounded perception, spatial reasoning, and continuous aerial control within a single model. A compact action expert converts the shared multimodal context into short-horizon flight waypoints, while motion-aware temporal memory and action priors reduce the computational cost of closed-loop execution. We construct \dataset{}, a simulation-based dataset comprising approximately 70,000 navigation trajectories and 60,000 vision-language instruction--response pairs. OpenFlyV2 generates action chunks, each comprising 12 waypoints, entirely onboard at 10 Hz using an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. It transfers zero-shot from simulation to physical quadrotor flight, achieving a 48\% navigation success rate across four real-world environments without real-world aerial training data. Extensive results demonstrate that broad vision-language competence and continuous closed-loop flight can coexist within a single model on a resource-constrained aerial platform. Code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [71] [Cross-Controller Transfer via In-Context Behavior Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39280)
*Jonathan Yang,Chelsea Finn,Dorsa Sadigh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种跨机器人本体与控制器迁移的通用机器人策略框架，通过建模“指令动作—实际运动”的行为交互上下文，使单一策略能在上下文中适应不同控制器的动作语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用机器人策略通常假设每个机器人使用单一控制器，但不同控制器会赋予相同动作不同物理含义，且完成任务所需策略不同，导致跨控制器数据迁移困难并产生动作解释冲突。

Method: 构建涵盖六类控制器及控制器增益、柔顺性变化的设定；用指令动作与机器人实际运动示例编码控制器下的行为交互，并由Transformer上下文编码器总结这些交互，使单一策略能够进行上下文自适应。

Result: 真实实验平均成功率较朴素数据合并提升33个百分点；在未见控制器模式上达到50%成功率，在未见控制器增益上达到52%；可迁移到控制器特定数据有限的任务；在USB插入和表面擦拭等接触丰富任务中，策略不仅能调整动作还能调整策略。

Conclusion: 结果表明，行为交互上下文可帮助通用机器人策略利用来自不同动作解释的数据，为跨本体与跨控制器的数据复用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Generalist robot policies increasingly learn from data collected across diverse tasks and embodiments, yet typically assume a single controller for each robot. This assumption limits transfer because different controllers can assign different physical meanings to the same action and may require different strategies to complete a task. We study how to transfer robot data across both embodiments and controllers without allowing conflicting action interpretations to interfere with learning. Our setting spans six controller types as well as variations in controller gains and compliance. We propose a framework that encodes behavioral interaction under a controller through examples of commanded actions and the resulting robot motions. A transformer context encoder summarizes these interactions, allowing a single policy to adapt in context. Across real-world experiments, our method improves average success by 33 percentage points over naive data pooling, achieves 50\% success on unseen controller modes and 52\% on unseen controller gains, and transfers to tasks with limited controller-specific data. On contact-rich tasks such as USB insertion and surface wiping, behavioral interaction context enables the policy to adapt not only its actions but also its strategy. Our results provide a path toward generalist robot policies that can leverage data collected across diverse action interpretations. For more information, visit \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [72] [CuBot-STT: Simultaneously Transport and Tighten for Lattice-type Modular Reconfiguration Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39279)
*Chongxi Meng,Da Zhao,Yanmin Zhou,Runjie Shen,Zhipeng Wang,Bin He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CuBot-STT 提出一种集模块搬运、接口锁紧与释放于一体的外部机器人，实现高承载、保留模块内部空间并支持晶格模块机器人自主重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶格型模块化机器人需跨任务重排模块；专用搬运机器人可去除模块级重构执行器以降低重量和成本，但现有系统存在连接容量低或内部紧固机器人占用功能组件空间的问题。

Method: 将模块运输、接口锁定与释放集成于一个外部机器人；立方模块提供结构支撑和 24 V 配电，每个面通过齿轮传动将单一路外部输入转换为四个螺旋锁紧点运动以保留内部空间；机器人采用交替足支撑、抓取器和拧紧头，控制器通过连续约束转移协调双足支撑与模块锁定；规划器将重定位成本分解为解析下界和由专家引导、目标条件 Q-learning 学习的残差，并用接触面 A* 搜索将重定位序列扩展为满足连通性、非对称操作和足部站姿约束的机器人基元。

Result: 实验实现 300 N 法向和 490 N 切向接口容量、六模块间电气连续性以及 11 模块晶格的自主重构；规划基准显示其搜索效率优于解析引导和双向启发式搜索，并可在不微调的情况下泛化到训练晶格尺寸之外。

Conclusion: CuBot-STT 通过外部机器人一体化完成搬运与接口驱动，在保持模块内部空间的同时实现高容量连接和高效自主晶格重构。

Abstract: Lattice-type modular reconfigurable robots rearrange standardized modules across tasks. Dedicated transport robots reduce module weight and cost by eliminating module level reconfiguration actuators. But existing systems use low capacity connections or internal fastening robots that occupy space for functional components. This paper presents CuBot-STT, which integrates module transport, interface locking, and release in one external robot. Cubic modules provide structural support and $24\,\mathrm{V}$ power distribution. On each face, a gear transmission converts one external input into motion of four helical locking points, preserving internal space. The robot uses alternating foot support, a gripper for module handling, and a tightening head for interface actuation. The controller coordinates two-foot support and module locking through continuous constraint transfers. The planner decomposes relocation costs into an analytical lower bound and a residual learned through expert-guided, goal conditioned Q-learning. Contact face $A^*$ search expands relocation sequences into robot primitives satisfying connectivity, asymmetric manipulation, and foot stance constraints. Experiments demonstrate normal and tangential interface capacities of $300\,\mathrm{N}$ and $490\,\mathrm{N}$, electrical continuity across six modules, and autonomous reconfiguration of an 11-module lattice. Planning benchmarks show superior search efficiency over analytical guidance and bidirectional heuristic search, with generalization beyond training lattice sizes without fine-tuning.

</details>


### [73] [RAIBO-Wagon: Real-Time Navigation System for a Legged Robot Towing a Wagon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39267)
*Insung Yang,Eunchae Kim,Jungwoo Hur,Seunghun Jeon,Donghyuk Choi,Jemin Hwangbo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将条件流匹配（CFM）与基于学习前向动力学模型（FDM）的MPPI控制相结合的导航框架，用于四足机器人RAIBO牵引欠驱动 wagon 在狭窄空间中的实时无碰撞导航，并在真实户外场景中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿足机器人自身控制已较成熟，但与外部尤其欠驱动物体耦合后，整体动力学更复杂；牵引 wagon 在狭窄空间导航需满足严格实时约束并找到狭窄可行路径，现有方法难以可靠处理。

Method: 构建CFM+MPPI+FDM的导航框架：CFM生成初始轨迹以缓解MPPI初始化问题并加速收敛；MPPI借助FDM迭代优化，恢复超出CFM训练分布的可行解；集成到RAIBO-Wagon全栈导航系统，全部感知与计算机载完成。

Result: 在15米长狭窄户外路线进行40次真实试验，系统成功率达85%，牵引总负载最高40 kg，约等于RAIBO自身体重。

Conclusion: 该框架能在机载感知与计算条件下实现四足机器人牵引欠驱动wagon的可靠狭窄空间导航，并具备处理耦合动力学与实时约束的能力。

Abstract: Legged robots have demonstrated advanced capabilities across a wide range of real-world tasks, yet much of this progress concerns the robot controlling only itself. Once the robot is coupled to an external object -- particularly an underactuated one -- the dynamics of the combined assembly become more complex. Towing a wagon is a common practical scenario, where coupled dynamics make reliable navigation challenging. This difficulty is amplified in confined spaces, where collision-free navigation requires finding a narrow set of feasible paths under strict real-time constraints. To address this, we propose a navigation framework that combines Conditional Flow Matching (CFM) with Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control over a learned Forward Dynamics Model (FDM). The CFM model mitigates the initialization problem of MPPI and accelerates its convergence, while MPPI with the FDM recovers feasible solutions beyond the CFM model's training distribution by iterative refinement. We integrate the framework into a full-stack navigation system for RAIBO-Wagon, a quadruped towing a custom wagon, and validate it in real-world outdoor experiments on 15 m-long courses through confined spaces, with all sensing and computation performed fully onboard.
