The system achieves an 85% success rate across 40 trials, towing a total load of up to 40 kg -- nearly 100% of RAIBO's body weight.

</details>


### [74] [Event2Reward: Learning Robotic Rewards from Progress Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39212)
*Junnan Nie,Zitian Fan,Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Likui Zhang,Chenghao Liu,Xiaoyang Lu,Yunbo Wang,Yixue Zhang,Linbo Zhai,Jiayi Li,Mengzhe Wang,Xichang Wu,Bin Wang,Chang Liu,Tianle Zhang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Event2Reward 利用演示中的归一化时间锚定绝对进度尺度，并利用轨迹内停滞、回退与恢复事件构造相对进度约束，学习可作为势函数用于稠密奖励塑形的进度模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将归一化演示时间当作进度标签，隐含假设任务进度单调递增；但在策略探索中进度可能停滞甚至回退，模型会把回退误判为进步，从而对造成进度损失的动作给予正奖励。真实次优与失败执行能提供纠错经验，但难以给这些状态标注绝对进度值。

Method: 作者提出 Event2Reward：以归一化时间标签提供绝对进度监督，以同一轨迹内停滞、回退和恢复事件建立的局部相对进度关系约束进度变化，联合绝对与相对进度监督训练进度预测器，并将其预测作为势函数进行奖励塑形。同时从约10万条真实机器人执行记录中构建事件标注数据集，覆盖成功、次优和失败执行，且不依赖逐帧绝对进度标签。

Result: 在相同训练预算下，Event2Reward 在四个 LIBERO 套件的40个任务上相较仅使用稀疏任务奖励的基线平均策略成功率提升18.16个百分点。真实机器人上，30分钟墙钟训练后，三个精细插入任务平均成功率达60.0%，三个物体操作任务达90.0%，分别比同预算稀疏奖励基线高36.7和23.3个百分点。消融表明绝对进度监督与相对进度关系互补，联合监督带来更好的下游策略学习。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合绝对进度尺度与轨迹内相对进度关系，可在缺少逐帧绝对标签的情况下更稳健地学习进度模型，并为机器人强化学习提供有效的稠密奖励塑形，尤其能利用失败和次优执行中的纠错信息。

Abstract: Learning task progress from robot demonstrations provides a practical way to construct dense shaping rewards for robotic reinforcement learning. A common approach uses normalized demonstration time as progress labels, implicitly assuming monotonically increasing task progress. During policy exploration, however, actual task progress can stall or even regress. Such models may mistake regressions for progress, assigning positive shaping rewards to actions that cause progress loss. Real suboptimal and failed executions offer corrective experience, but assigning absolute progress values to their states is difficult. Our key observation is that stalls, regressions, and recoveries establish local relative progress relations even when absolute progress is unknown. We introduce Event2Reward, which uses normalized temporal labels to anchor the progress scale and these within-trajectory relations to constrain local changes, combining absolute and relative progress supervision. Its progress predictions serve as potentials for reward shaping. We curate an event-annotated dataset from approximately 100K raw real-robot execution records, covering successful, suboptimal, and failed executions, without requiring framewise absolute progress labels. Under the same training budgets, Event2Reward improves average policy success by 18.16 percentage points over a baseline using only sparse task rewards across 40 tasks in four LIBERO suites. On real robots, average success after 30 minutes of wall-clock training reaches 60.0% on three precise insertion tasks and 90.0% on three object manipulation tasks, exceeding the sparse-reward baseline under the same budget by 36.7 and 23.3 percentage points, respectively. Ablation studies further show that absolute progress supervision and relative progress relations are complementary, with joint supervision yielding better downstream policy learning.

</details>


### [75] [EmbodiExp: Compositional Experience Transfer for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39180)
*Zhifeng Gu,Ying Chen,Rui Jiang,Lei Yi,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EmbodiExp 框架，通过以结果为锚定的子目标经验实现在线组合，提升具身智能体跨任务经验迁移与执行效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体需将高层计划落地为动作，但经验跨任务迁移困难：既有策略可能不适用于当前状态，或无法产生预期结果。

Method: 将经验表示为子目标经验，包含子目标、适用条件、可适配策略和可验证结果；执行时匹配经验指导动作生成，并利用已验证结果建立后续经验所需条件以实现在线组合；同时通过子目标级结果归因利用失败轨迹中的局部成功与需避免动作的条件性指导。

Result: 在多个具身基准上采用基于折叠且冻结经验库的协议进行实验，验证了 EmbodiExp 的有效性。

Conclusion: 以可验证子目标结果为经验单元并支持在线组合，可有效构建和迁移跨任务经验，并能从失败轨迹中提取可用信息。

Abstract: Experience can help embodied agents turn high-level plans into grounded actions, but transferring it across tasks remains challenging: previously effective strategies may be inapplicable to the current state or fail to produce their intended outcomes. We present EmbodiExp, a framework for constructing transferable experiences and composing them online during embodied task execution. Our key observation is that tasks often share subgoals whose completion can be assessed independently of overall task success. We therefore introduce an outcome-grounded subgoal experience that specifies a subgoal, applicability conditions, an adaptable policy, and a verifiable outcome. During execution, applicable experiences guide action generation, while verified outcomes can establish the conditions required by subsequent experiences, enabling online composition. Subgoal-level outcome attribution further enables failure utilization: failed trajectories can still contribute verified local successes and condition-specific guidance on actions to avoid. Experiments on multiple embodied benchmarks under a fold-based protocol with frozen experience banks demonstrate the effectiveness of EmbodiExp.

</details>


### [76] [EgoContact: Evaluating Physical Intelligence with Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39167)
*Adam Deryło,Alexey Gavryushin,Daniel Cremers,Marc Pollefeys,Xi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EgoContact 大规模第一人称视频数据集，用于通过未来接触点预测评估操作先验的泛化性与精度，并证明其作为机器人操作记忆库可显著提升抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一人称视频是学习可泛化操作先验的可扩展数据源，但在验证物理智能方面利用不足；物理智能的关键之一是理解应与物体何处接触以实现意图交互。现有方法在多样真实场景中预测精确接触点仍较困难。

Method: 构建 EgoContact 数据集，包含 22.5K 个人工验证交互事件，每个事件含接触前观察、语义意图和像素级接触位置；以未来接触点预测任务评估学习到的操作先验的泛化性与精度；并将 EgoContact 作为现有 affordance-transfer 策略的记忆库用于机器人操作。

Result: 当前方法常难以预测精确交互接触点，有时即使允许多个预测也无法定位目标物体；将 EgoContact 用作记忆库后，仿真中抓取成功率从 14.2% 提升至 31.2%，真实机器人上从 16% 提升至 58%。

Conclusion: EgoContact 可作为物理智能的验证工具和机器人操作的有效数据资源，其标注交互能提升 affordance 迁移策略的抓取性能。

Abstract: Egocentric video has emerged as one of the most scalable sources of data for learning generalizable manipulation priors. Yet, despite its growing use in pretraining robotic policies, it has been underutilized as a validation tool for physical intelligence, a key component of which is understanding where to make contact with an object to achieve an intended interaction. We present EgoContact, a large-scale egocentric dataset for evaluating the generalizability and precision of learned manipulation priors through future contact-point prediction. EgoContact contains 22.5K manually verified interaction events, each with a pre-contact observation, semantic intent, and pixel-precise contact locations. We find that current methods often struggle to predict precise interaction contacts and sometimes fail to localize the target object in diverse, in-the-wild scenes, even when allowed multiple predictions. Furthermore, we demonstrate the usefulness of EgoContact's annotated interactions for robotic manipulation: simply using EgoContact as the memory bank of an existing affordance-transfer policy improves grasp success from 14.2% to 31.2% in simulation and from 16% to 58% on a real robot.

</details>


### [77] [Actuator-Aware Observer Inputs for Cascade ADRC of Quadrotors Under Rotor Lag, Measurement Delay and Sensor Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39126)
*Xuan-Quy Dao,Ngoc-Bich Le,Le-Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出执行器感知（AA）观测器输入，用于四旋翼采样级联LADRC，使ESO输入与延迟测量及转子滤波后的加速度一致，并在仿真中显著降低设定点跟踪RMSE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统采样级联LADRC中，扩展状态观测器通常接收瞬时指令，但延迟测量反映的是更早、经转子滤波的加速度，导致观测器输入与真实执行器动态不匹配。

Method: 高度与姿态观测器采用经假定转子模型滤波、并与测量延迟对齐的受限虚拟输入；水平观测器采用由实测姿态重构的加速度。固定增益基于离散时间模型选取，满足灵敏度与阶跃响应要求，并对各通道控制量、增量及完整级联噪声诱发内环增量施加平稳协方差上限。

Result: 成对设定点试验中，PID-Budget、AA-Budget、AA-HalfGain平均RMSE分别为65.5、19.2、16.9 mm；更严格共同级联上限下，AA设计为29.9与26.4 mm，PID为72.3 mm且转子指令增量约为其两倍。PX4软件在环复现结果；EKF2后，拟合噪声模型对滚转/俯仰与偏航增量预测误差在5%内、总转子指令增量在14%内。AA-Budget-E将设定点RMSE从59.1 mm降至44.0 mm，并在每次成对设定点与八字航迹中转子指令增量更低；相对PID-E的平均S6转子指令增量削减为量化前45%、量化后15%；AA-HalfGain-E仅在八字航迹优于PID-E。

Conclusion: 证据基于仿真，协方差上限为名义整定约束；AA观测器输入能改善四旋翼级联LADRC的跟踪精度并降低转子指令增量，但需注意其仿真验证与调参假设限制。

Abstract: In sampled cascade linear active disturbance rejection control (LADRC) of quadrotors, the extended-state observer typically receives the instantaneous command, although the delayed measurement reflects an earlier, rotor-filtered acceleration. This paper proposes actuator-aware (AA) observer inputs: altitude and attitude observers use limited virtual inputs filtered by an assumed rotor model and aligned with the measurement delay, and horizontal observers use acceleration reconstructed from measured attitude. Fixed gains are selected on the discrete-time model under sensitivity and step-response requirements and stationary covariance ceilings on each channel's control variance and increment and on the noise-induced inner-loop increment of the full cascade. In paired set-point trials, PID-Budget, AA-Budget and AA-HalfGain reach mean RMSEs of 65.5, 19.2 and 16.9~mm; under a stricter common cascade ceiling, the AA designs reach 29.9 and 26.4~mm versus 72.3~mm for a PID that uses roughly double their rotor-command increment. PX4 software-in-the-loop runs reproduce these results. Behind EKF2, a noise model fitted to the estimation errors predicts the roll/pitch and yaw increments within 5\% on held-out and out-of-sample runs and the total rotor-command increment within 14\%. Redesigned for this stack under the same sensitivity and response specification and estimator-aware ceilings, AA-Budget-E reduces the set-point RMSE from 59.1~mm (PID-E) to 44.0~mm with a lower rotor-command increment in every paired set-point and lemniscate run (mean S6 rotor-command-increment reductions relative to PID-E: 45\% before and 15\% after output quantization); AA-HalfGain-E beats PID-E only on the lemniscate. The evidence is simulation-based, and the ceilings are nominal tuning constraints.

</details>


### [78] [Tracking Task Progress Through Errors and Recovery:A Benchmark and Recurrent Distributional Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39125)
*Chen Wang,Xinze Niu,Zihao Zhu,Keru Zhou,Yicheng Xu,Shihao Jin,Wenhao Wang,Bowen Jiang,Chang Zhou,Li Liu,Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文针对长时程机器人任务中进度评估与错误恢复数据稀缺的问题，构建并开源ReSTEP数据集，并提出在线进度估计器REMORA，在进度准确性和时间一致性上优于SARM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人任务需要可靠评估执行是在推进、停滞、回退还是从错误中恢复，但真实机器人数据中缺乏包含错误恢复和帧级错误标注的数据集；现有进度估计器难以区分视觉相似阶段，也难以在响应真实挫折时保持时间一致性。

Method: 作者构建ReSTEP数据集，包含两个操作任务共3156个episode，提供语义阶段标签和帧级错误标注，支持成功执行、错误和恢复场景下的训练与评测。提出REMORA在线进度估计器，结合循环视觉记忆、分布预测和因果峰值选择：记忆保留执行历史以缓解视觉歧义，分布预测表示多种进度假设，因果峰值选择随时间跟踪受支持的假设。

Result: 在ReSTEP的两个任务上，REMORA在进度准确性和时间一致性方面均优于最先进的SARM基线。

Conclusion: ReSTEP为机器人长时程任务中的错误恢复与进度监测提供了带帧级标注的开放数据集，REMORA通过记忆、分布预测与因果峰值选择实现了对错误和恢复更一致且更敏感的在线进度跟踪。

Abstract: Operational strategies for long-horizon robotic tasks require reliable evaluation to determine whether execution is progressing, stalling, regressing, or recovering from errors. Yet real- world robot datasets that capture error recovery and provide frame-level error annotations remain scarce. To address this gap, we introduce and open-source ReSTEP, a dataset of 3,156 episodes across two manipulation tasks with semantic stage labels and frame-level er- ror annotations. ReSTEP supports training and benchmarking progress monitors through successful execution, mistakes, and recovery. Existing progress estimators can struggle to distinguish visually similar task stages and maintain temporally consistent predictions while responding to genuine setbacks. We therefore propose REMORA, an online progress esti- mator that combines recurrent visual memory, distributional prediction, and causal peak selection. Memory retains execution history to help resolve visual ambiguity, while distribu- tional prediction represents alternative progress hypotheses and causal peak selection tracks a supported hypothesis over time. Together, these components enable consistent progress tracking that remains responsive to errors and recovery. Experiments on both ReSTEP tasks demonstrate that REMORA outperforms the state-of-the-art SARM baseline in progress ac- curacy and temporal consistency.

</details>


### [79] [CAPTURER: A Dexterous Aerial Manipulator for Dynamic and Precise Grasping across Diverse Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39119)
*Meng Wang,Jindou Jia,Sicheng Zhou,Zeshuai Chen,Kexin Guo,Xiang Yu,Lei Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAPTURER 是一种灵巧空中机械臂系统，可在室内外多样环境中对静态和移动目标实现厘米级空中抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中抓取多在固定场景下针对静态或慢速目标，跨条件动态精确抓取仍受目标运动、定位漂移、动态耦合和外部扰动等挑战限制。

Method: 系统集成机载感知用于状态估计与目标检测，引入漂移校正和全局位姿变换提升飞行中目标定位；提出协同运动规划器，根据相对目标状态协调空中平台机动性与机械臂灵巧性；设计抗扰控制器补偿动态耦合、模型不确定性和外部扰动。

Result: 通过室内外静态与移动目标实验验证，CAPTURER 在飞行速度超过 1.5 m/s 时对直径仅 1.5 cm 的记号笔实现厘米级抓取精度。

Conclusion: CAPTURER 验证了跨多样环境进行动态、精确空中抓取的可行性，并展示了感知、规划与抗扰控制协同设计在厘米级空中操作中的有效性。

Abstract: As a representative form of embodied robotic interaction, aerial grasping requires close coordination between the aerial platform and manipulator. Most existing systems perform aerial grasping with static or slowly moving targets under fixed scenarios. Dynamic and precise grasping across different conditions introduces additional challenges, including target motion, localization drift, dynamic coupling, and external disturbances. In this work, we present CAPTURER, a dexterous aerial manipulator for centimeter-level grasping across diverse environments. To achieve the challenging goal, we first develop an onboard perception system for state estimation and target detection. Drift correction and global pose transformation are introduced to improve target localization during flight. A cooperative motion planner is proposed to coordinate the mobility of the aerial platform with the dexterity of the manipulator according to the relative target state. To maintain grasping accuracy under disturbances, we design a disturbance-rejection controller to compensate for dynamic coupling, model uncertainty, and external disturbances. The capability of CAPTURER is validated through indoor and outdoor experiments with both static and moving targets. The results demonstrate that CAPTURER achieves centimeter-level grasping accuracy for a marker pen measuring only 1.5 cm in diameter at flight speeds exceeding 1.5 m/s.

</details>


### [80] [Tactus: Tactile & Pose Sensing Glove for Natural Dexterity Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39076)
*Li Guan,Rachael Granberry,Sahar Aseeri,Sahar Rostami,Taylor Niehues,Sai Dhanooshi Marri,Mia Huang,Dingtian Zhang,Ziyi Kou,Jason Pursel,Eric Whitmire,Vatsal Mehta,Ashish Deshpande,Tristan Trutna,Reed Belcher,Emelia Black,Julian Blake,Roger Boldu,Sean Bonaventure,Einar Boyovich,Jordan Brennan,Elizabeth Casillas,David Christensen,Tyler Christensen,Nicholas Colonnese,Stuart Dealey,Gaige DeHaven,Katie Doran,Chris Doughty,Robert Drury,Silvia Espino,Gabriel Geyne,Beth Goodrich,Kyra Gould,Joe Grisso,Susana Guerrero,Andrew Gwozdziowski,Monica Ha,Ehsan Hajiesmaili,Kate Healy,Rose Hope,Michael Hurst,Claire Ikemiya,Daisy Isibor,Rick James,Yashasvi Joginapally,Samuel Johnson,Tyler Jorgensen,Kristy Jost,Ben Kamath,Slava Karulin,Alexander Keener,Woo Kim,Alison Krauskopf,Ankit Kumar,Joshua Lang,Lizzy Li,Cameron Lynch,Daniel Ma,Hector Martinon,Christina Matos,Keagan McCurdy,Lee Mincey,Stuart Nezlek,Tiffany Nguyen,James O'Hara,Jason Osborne,Jacky Pai,Roham Pardakhtim,David Perek,Alexandre Poulin,Nick Raines,Salwa Rasheed,Leah Resneck,Kevin Rhodes,Ergys Ristani,Douglas Roberts,Anik Sarker,Harrison Scarborough,Jonas Schmidtler,Brian Shieh,Benjamin Shuman,Garrett Simard,Forrest Smith,Udara Somarathna,Eliot Sosin,Jonathan Stewart,Kristine Strom,Adam Swett,Hal Talbot-Mantooth,Julia Tanner,Sai Sharan Thirunagari,Curtis Torrey,Simge Uzun,Eileen Wachs,Rylan Wasson,Henry Wei,Jesse White,Beau Woodbyrne,Antonio Woods,Noah Young,Chandler Yount,Joseph Zhang,Sean Keller,et al. (4 additional authors not shown)*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出纺织集成传感手套Tactus，同时实现校准指尖力传感与抗遮挡手部追踪，并建立跨平台定量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然操作示范数据需要准确接触力与手姿，但现有可穿戴平台难以同时兼顾校准力感、遮挡鲁棒追踪和不干扰自然操作的形态。

Method: Tactus采用三大设计支柱：1.5 mm指尖堆叠的薄型共形3D针织结构；依据经验接触研究在操作相关区域布置电容触觉传感器；IMU融合姿态追踪以在手物遮挡下保持精度。平台包含跨手套一致性与每会话受试者校正的校准流程，并提出触觉力回归和手姿估计的标准化评测协议，对Tactus与两款商用触觉手套进行首次定量跨平台评估。

Result: Tactus在95%置信度下最小可检测力为0.59 N，较商用基线低1.6–2.7倍，跨参与者一致性为0.87；在遮挡最重的操作过程中，指尖关键点相对误差为11.0 mm；融合信号支持物体属性估计、滑动检测和手物交互代表性记录。

Conclusion: Tactus能提供高保真、接触感知的示范数据，适用于接触感知操作策略和世界模型学习。

Abstract: Collecting naturalistic human manipulation demonstrations with accurate contact force and hand pose is essential for robot dexterous manipulation policy and foundational world model learning - yet no existing wearable platform provides both calibrated fingertip force sensing and occlusion-resilient hand tracking in a form factor that preserves natural manipulation behavior. We present Tactus, a textile-integrated sensing glove that co-optimizes three design pillars: (1) a thin, conformal 3D-knit form factor (1.5 mm fingertip stackup) designed to minimize interference with natural manipulation, (2) strategically placed capacitive tactile sensors at manipulation-relevant regions informed by empirical contact studies, and (3) IMU-fused pose tracking that maintains accuracy through the hand-object occlusions where manipulation data is most valuable. Tactus includes a documented calibration pipeline for cross-glove consistency and per-session calibration for subject-dependent correction. To enable objective comparison, we introduce standardized benchmarking protocols for tactile force regression and hand pose estimation, and apply them to Tactus and two commercially available tactile gloves - to our knowledge the first quantitative cross-platform evaluation of this kind. Tactus resolves a minimum detectable force of 0.59 N at 95% confidence, 1.6 - 2.7x lower than the commercial baselines, and with 0.87 cross-participant consistency, while our pose tracking sustains 11.0 mm fingertip keypoint relative error during manipulation, when occlusion is heaviest. We show that these combined signals support object property estimation, slip detection, and representative recording of hand-object interaction - yielding demonstration data with the fidelity required for contact-aware manipulation policies and world models.

</details>


### [81] [Controlling Continuum Robot Shape in Enclosed Space with Embedded Sensing using IMU and Actuator Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39055)
*Jiahe Wang,Sultan Haidar Ali,Eric Weissman,Jiefeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于IMU与执行器反馈的嵌入式形状感知框架，用神经网络以400 Hz重建连续体机器人形状，并实现闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人在受限封闭环境中难以依赖外部观测进行形状控制；现有嵌入式形状感知在重建精度、计算速度和形状状态表示兼容性方面不足以支持实时闭环控制。

Method: 构建嵌入式形状感知框架，利用IMU和执行器反馈通过神经网络重建形状；系统研究形状状态表示、网络架构、训练目标和传感输入四类设计选择，并在单段与三段连续体机器人上训练超过5000个模型。

Result: 所选框架在单段和三段系统上的全局形状误差分别为0.9%L和1.7%L；闭环控制中，单段末端跟踪误差为6.1%L，三段形状控制全局误差为2.3%L，而使用未补偿测量的IMU-PCC重建失败；封闭空间实验验证了无外部观测运行。

Conclusion: 该工作为将易获取的嵌入式传感转化为连续体机器人闭环控制所需的高速率反馈提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Controlling continuum robots' shape to operate in confined, enclosed environments is challenging, since it cannot rely on external observation. Existing embedded shape sensing methods, although they can provide shape reconstruction, are insufficient to enable real-time shape control due to limitations in reconstruction accuracy, computational speed, and compatibility with shape-state representation. In this work, we present an embedded shape-sensing framework that uses readily available IMU and actuator feedback to reconstruct the continuum-robot shape with a neural network, providing accurate shape feedback at $400~\mathrm{Hz}$ for physical closed-loop control. We systematically investigate four key reconstruction design choices: shape-state representation, network architecture, training objective, and sensing inputs, through more than $5{,}000$ trained models on one- and three-segment continuum robots. The selected framework achieves global shape errors of $0.9\%L$ and $1.7\%L$ (of the robot length) for the one- and three-segment systems, respectively. In closed-loop control, one-segment tip tracking achieves a $6.1\%L$ error and three-segment shape control achieves a $2.3\%L$ global shape error, whereas IMU--PCC reconstruction using the same uncompensated measurements fails. Enclosed-space experiments further demonstrate operation without external observation. These results provide a practical framework for translating readily available embedded sensing into high-rate feedback for continuum robot closed-loop control.

</details>


### [82] [Robotic Data Inoculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39054)
*Qiyang Li,Zhiyuan Zhou,Charles Xu,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Data Inoculation（DI）共享控制数据采集框架：人类闭环遥操作，残差策略在线扰动指令，以扩大状态覆盖并生成恢复示范，从而提升下游策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖高覆盖高质量示范数据；覆盖不足时小误差会累积并进入不可恢复的分布外状态。现有数据采集依赖启发式指令，纯在线 RL 不安全且样本效率低，DAgger 式干预需策略先偶然失败，缺乏原则性高覆盖采集方法。

Method: DI 在数据采集时由人类提供闭环遥操作命令，同时用残差策略扰动这些命令；残差策略在增长的数据集上在线训练，采用与后续策略提取相同的奖励最大化目标，使扰动引导采集朝向数据训练策略可能产生的偏差。扰动扩大机器人经历的状态范围，人类的持续纠正提供恢复示范，在人类保持控制的情况下“接种”数据集以抵御学习器未来错误。

Result: 在三个具有挑战性的真实机器人任务上，DI 相比行为克隆和离线 RL 训练的策略，一致提升或匹配成功率。

Conclusion: DI 是一种有效的数据采集策略，可在人类控制下提高数据覆盖并注入恢复示范，从而改善下游策略性能。

Abstract: Modern robot learning systems feed on high-quality demonstration data, and their performance hinges on coverage. Without good coverage, small errors compound and the policy can quickly go into out-of-distribution states from which it cannot recover. Yet there is no principled way of collecting high-coverage data, and data collection often relies on heuristic instructions to human operators. Purely online reinforcement learning (RL) explores at the cost of safety and sample efficiency, while DAgger-style interventions require the policy to fail by chance before a human can correct it. In this paper, we ask instead whether we can modify how the robot behaves during data collection to collect more useful data. We propose Data Inoculation (DI), a shared-control framework in which a human provide closed-loop teleoperation commands while a residual policy perturbs these commands. The residual policy is trained online, on the growing dataset, with the same reward-maximizing objective later used for policy extraction, so its perturbations steer data collection toward the deviations a policy trained on the data would make. These perturbations expose the robot to a wider range of states, while the human's continuous corrections provide recovery demonstrations, "inoculating" the dataset against the learner's future mistakes while the human remains in control. On three challenging real-world robotic tasks, DI consistently improves or matches the success rate of policies trained with both behavioral cloning and offline RL, showing DI as an effective data collection strategy for improving downstream policy performance.

</details>


### [83] [Catch It All!: Generalizable Dynamic Catching with a Dexterous Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39044)
*Arth Shukla,Kehlani Fay,Huaze Liu,Darin Djapri,Alexiy Buynitsky,Nicklas Hansen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Catch It All!，一种面向人形机器人的 sim-to-real 抓接框架，仅用单目第一视角相机即可泛化并适应未见物体的上手与下手抓接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任意物体抓接需要泛化到新抛射物，并在噪声视觉观测下实时适应不可预测的接触动力学；现有方法难以同时处理物体几何、形变、弹性与物理属性变化。

Method: 学习可泛化到未见物体的策略，覆盖不同几何、可变形性、弹性及物理属性；通过精心设计抓取奖励获得实时适应物体位姿与形状的有效抓取；采用安全感知课程学习，使高频动作执行接近硬件极限仍安全，包括低至 5 Nm 的腕部力矩限制；在 Unitree G1 上零样本部署。

Result: 仅使用一个第一视角相机，Catch It All! 成功对 9 个训练中未见物体完成上手和下手抓接，包括低回弹毛绒玩具、半可变形玩具与薯片袋，以及接触时高回弹的刚弹性物体。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了单目视觉下 sim-to-real 人形机器人抓接策略对未见物体的泛化能力与对复杂接触动力学的实时适应能力，并兼顾硬件安全约束。

Abstract: Catching arbitrary objects requires generalization to novel projectiles and real-time adaptation to unpredictable contact dynamics under noisy visual observations. This paper presents Catch It All!, a sim-to-real catching framework for humanoid robots that generalizes and adapts to unseen objects with variable physical properties for underhand and overhand catches, using only one egocentric camera. We learn policies which generalize to unseen objects with varying geometry, deformability, elasticity, and physical properties. Effective grasps which adapt real-time to object poses and shapes are learned through careful grasp reward design. We provide safety-aware curricula which enable safe, high-frequency action execution near hardware limits, including wrist torque limits as low as 5 Nm. We validate our system by deploying zero-shot on a Unitree G1. With only one egocentric camera, Catch It All! successfully performs underhand and overhand catches on 9 unseen objects not seen during training, including low-rebound plushes, semi-deformable toys and chip bags, and rigid-elastic objects with high rebound on contact.

</details>


### [84] [Self-Supervised Doppler-Guided RF Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39005)
*Kunzhe Song,Jialuo Du,Jingkai Lin,Huacheng Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RFWalk 是一种仅依赖射频传感的自监督里程计框架，通过时空图随机游走与多普勒一致性约束实现机器人自运动估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频传感可在黑暗、烟雾和雾中实现机器人自运动估计，但多径和运动反射体导致跨帧匹配高度歧义；现有方法通常依赖惯性辅助传感器或真值位姿监督。

Method: 将距离-方位帧间的稠密对应建模为时空图上的随机游走，并利用时间循环一致性从无标签序列中自监督学习；引入多普勒一致性损失，用射频传感器测得的径向速度约束对应诱导运动，排除循环一致但物理不合理的对应；再通过鲁棒 SE(2) 配准恢复自运动，并抑制不确定对应及独立运动物体产生的对应。

Result: 无需位姿标签或辅助传感器，RFWalk 在静态场景中与监督基线相当；在含行人的动态场景中，绝对轨迹误差降低 58%。

Conclusion: RFWalk 证明了仅用射频传感、无需监督即可实现较鲁棒的里程计估计，并在动态场景中显著优于相关基线。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) sensing enables robot ego-motion estimation in darkness, smoke, and fog, yet RF odometry remains challenging because multipath and moving reflectors make cross-frame matching highly ambiguous. Existing methods often rely on auxiliary inertial sensors or ground-truth pose supervision. We present RFWalk, a self-supervised, RF-only odometry framework that models dense correspondences across range-azimuth frames as random walks on a space-time graph and learns them from unlabeled sequences through temporal cycle consistency. To rule out correspondences that are cycle-consistent but physically implausible, we introduce a Doppler consistency loss that constrains correspondence-induced motion using radial velocity measured by the RF sensor itself. Ego-motion is then recovered through robust $\mathrm{SE}(2)$ registration that suppresses uncertain correspondences and those arising from independently moving objects. Without pose labels or auxiliary sensors, RFWalk remains competitive with supervised baselines in static scenes and reduces absolute trajectory error by 58% in dynamic scenes with moving pedestrians.

</details>


### [85] [Open-World Object Motion Anticipation via Meta-Learned Semantic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38993)
*Mason Peterson,Gage MacLin,Camilla Tabasso,Jonathan P. How,Joshua Migdal,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种元学习图神经网络，利用物体的语义、几何和关系历史，在少量在线观测下预测新环境中物体何时开始运动，从而提升机器人导航的安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人导航需要预判附近物体运动；若不能理解哪些物体会移动，规划可能过于保守低效，或过于冒险导致碰撞。现有方法多关注已知物体的跟踪与预测，缺乏对新物体及物体间带动关系的在线适应能力。

Method: 构建元学习图神经网络，融合物体的语义、几何和关系历史信息，仅用仿真数据训练，基于少量在线观测预测新环境中物体开始运动的时机，并可泛化到未见物体和真实硬件。

Result: 方法能准确预判训练时未见物体的运动，无需微调即可迁移到硬件；在仿真和硬件实验中优于视觉语言模型和类别频率基线；在闭环导航仿真中实现无碰撞通行，且总耗时与最快但易碰撞的规划器相差不超过4秒。

Conclusion: 该工作使机器人能够在线适应新物体并预判其运动，包括物体间带动关系，为安全且高效的机器人导航提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Anticipating the motion of nearby objects is critical for safe and efficient robot navigation. Without a proper understanding of what may move, a robot may plan overly conservative, inefficient trajectories to guarantee safety, or it may be overly risky, resulting in unacceptable collisions. While tracking and predicting the motion of known objects has seen significant progress, little research addresses how a robot should adapt online to novel objects, including anticipating how one object may move another (e.g., a person operating a forklift to move a pallet). To enable this capability, we propose a meta-learned graph neural network that leverages a semantic, geometric, and relational history of objects to accurately predict, from a few online observations, when objects will begin to move in new environments. We demonstrate that our method, which is trained only in simulation, accurately anticipates the motion of objects unseen at training time, transfers to hardware without any fine-tuning, and outperforms a vision-language model and a class-frequency baseline in both simulation and hardware experiments. Our method's accurate prediction enables collision-free traversals in a closed-loop navigation simulation, while remaining within four seconds of the fastest, collision-prone planner.

</details>


### [86] [Learning Event-based Obstacle Avoidance via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38967)
*Chenlu Shou,Yunfan Ren,Jiaxu Xing,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于CUDA事件模拟器与可微分物理仿真的端到端训练框架，使四旋翼仅凭事件帧直接输出控制指令，并在真实环境中零样本实现鲁棒避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统帧相机在快速运动下受运动模糊、动态范围有限和延迟影响，难以可靠避障；事件相机虽具低延迟、高动态范围和低运动模糊优势，但现有闭环控制方法要么采用“先估深度再控制”的级联结构导致误差传播，要么依赖昂贵的离线预训练后再在线微调。

Method: 构建利用GPU并行性的CUDA事件模拟器，在训练循环内高效渲染事件，并集成到可微分仿真管线中，使梯度可直接通过可微分物理模型反传，提高样本效率；进而训练将事件帧直接映射为控制指令的端到端策略，无需中间深度估计。

Result: 在真实四旋翼上实现零样本部署，在有里程计辅助和无里程计两种设置下均取得鲁棒避障效果，并在传统相机通常失效的低光照条件下仍能工作。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合高效可微分事件仿真与端到端策略学习，可绕开深度估计级联和昂贵预训练，为事件相机驱动的四旋翼闭环避障提供可行且实用的方案。

Abstract: Reliable obstacle avoidance is essential for safe quadrotor flight in unknown environments, yet conventional frame-based cameras remain challenged by motion blur, limited dynamic range, and latency under fast motion. Event cameras, with sub-millisecond latency, high dynamic range, and negligible motion blur, offer a promising alternative, but leveraging them for closed-loop control remains difficult. Existing approaches either adopt cascaded architectures that first estimate depth and then map it to control commands, introducing error propagation, or achieve end-to-end control through costly offline pretraining before online fine-tuning can even begin. We address these challenges with a CUDA-based event simulator that exploits GPU parallelism to render events efficiently within the training loop, integrated into a differentiable simulation pipeline that enables direct gradient backpropagation through the differentiable physics model, improving sample efficiency. This allows us to train an end-to-end policy that maps event frames directly to control commands, without intermediate depth estimation. We validate our approach through zero-shot real-world deployment on a quadrotor, achieving robust obstacle avoidance in both odometry-assisted and odometry-free settings, including under low-light conditions where conventional cameras typically fail.

</details>


### [87] [Selective Sliding Mode Control Enhances Robustness of Aerial Robots Under Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38930)
*Anoop Kiran,Thales C. Silva,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于扰动反演与轴激励识别的级联四旋翼鲁棒控制部署方法，仅对受扰关键轴施加鲁棒控制，并在Crazyflie 2.1上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风扰和负载变化下的四旋翼控制仍具挑战；经典一阶滑模控制及匹配/非匹配不确定性区分无法指导实践者确定级联控制中哪些回路应鲁棒化、如何整定及嵌入式处理器额外代价，且全轴统一鲁棒控制会带来不必要的整定负担，欠驱动轴上扰动也无法直接抵消。

Method: 该方法在机载运行全部控制的前提下，从受扰PID悬停飞行数据中反演运动方程以恢复实际扰动信号，识别并量化被激励轴，再将鲁棒控制部署到真正需要的回路中。

Result: 在Crazyflie 2.1四旋翼的吊挂负载与斜风扰动实验中，跟踪性能提升集中在规则选定的轴上；增益扫频表明切换增益可降低匹配轴误差，但对被激励的非匹配轴无效，与分析预测一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明，按扰动激励轴选择性部署鲁棒控制可在级联架构中以更低整定负担获得针对性跟踪改善，并明确了切换增益在匹配与非匹配扰动下的作用边界。

Abstract: Quadrotor control under disturbances such as wind gusts and payload variations remains challenging. First-order sliding mode control (SMC) and matched/unmatched uncertainty distinction are classical approaches; however, neither tells a practitioner which loops in a cascaded controller should be made robust to sustain disturbances, how to tune those loops, nor what the additional costs are on an embedded processor. Applying robust control uniformly across all axes addresses disturbances indiscriminately, adding tuning effort on axes where no disturbance acts. On inherently underactuated vehicles, disturbances along some axes cannot be opposed directly at all. We propose a principled methodology for deciding where robust control belongs in a cascaded architecture, with all control running onboard the vehicle. We recover the acting disturbance from PID hover flights under disturbance by inverting the equations of motion, use the recovered signal to identify and quantify the excited axes, and deploy robust control where it counts. Experiments on a Crazyflie 2.1 quadrotor under slung-payload and oblique-wind disturbances show tracking improvements concentrated on the axes the rule selects, and gain sweeps confirm that the switching gain reduces error on matched axes but has no effect on an excited unmatched axis, as the analysis predicts.

</details>


### [88] [Inertial Steering and Motion Prediction of Tracked Vehicles on Low-Adherence Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38888)
*Mathis Turgeon-Roy,Mohamed Ounally,Veronica Vannini,Olivier Roy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用移动质量产生的惯性效应，在低附着表面上为履带车辆实现惯性转向，并通过动力学模型、混合模型和冰面实验验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统履带车辆运动模型通常依赖无滑移假设，在低附着表面上会失效；作者不把滑移引起的惯性效应仅视为需抑制的扰动，而是探索将其主动用于转向。

Method: 建立带被动拖车的铰接履带车辆动力学模型以捕捉拖车运动产生的惯性效应；在冰面进行实验并与运动学基线比较；提出混合动力学-运动学模型以结合两种预测；在无传统转向机构的单履带车辆上通过内部质量的受控移动实现惯性转向，并将内部质量建模为无摩擦拖车以用所提动力学模型预测。

Result: 冰面实验中动力学模型优于运动学基线；混合模型能根据表面和情境自适应预测；单履带车辆通过内部质量受控移动在冰面上产生转向运动，且该行为可由将内部质量视为无摩擦拖车的动力学模型预测，揭示两种构型的动力学类比。

Conclusion: 结果表明惯性转向可作为低附着环境下履带车辆的一种替代转向机制，并具有可行性。

Abstract: Tracked vehicles are widely used in off-road applications, but their conventional motion models often rely on no-slip assumptions that become inaccurate on low-adherence surfaces. Rather than treating inertial effects arising from slip as a disturbance to be minimized, this work investigates how inertial effects arising from a moving mass can be deliberately exploited for steering on low-friction surfaces. A dynamic model for an articulated tracked vehicle with a passive trailer is developed, allowing inertial effects from trailer motion to be captured. The approach is experimentally evaluated on ice, where our dynamic model outperforms the kinematic baseline. We also propose a mixed dynamic-kinematic model to adapt predictions to both surface and situation by exploiting predictions from both methods. Finally, we demonstrate inertial steering on a mono-track vehicle without a conventional steering mechanism, showing that controlled movements of an internal mass can generate turning motion on ice. The resulting behavior can be predicted using the proposed dynamic model by representing the internal mass as a frictionless trailer, revealing the analogous dynamics between the two configurations. These results demonstrate the feasibility of inertial steering as an alternative steering mechanism for tracked vehicles operating in low-adherence environments.

</details>


### [89] [AtomTree: A Hierarchical Framework for State-Aware Embodied Instruction Following with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38744)
*Haotian Zhai,Tianming Sha,Junnan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AtomTree 是一个基于大语言模型的分层、状态感知任务规划框架，通过增量构建子目标与动作树、记忆复用和分层回溯重规划，提升具身指令跟随的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器要么依赖大量标注轨迹，要么采用少样本/零样本 LLM 一次性串行生成完整计划；这些方法依赖语言常识、初始即承诺计划，且通常仅在失败后更新，导致状态感知和鲁棒性不足。

Method: AtomTree 增量构建由子目标和动作组成的树；LLM 根据当前符号状态逐步决定是继续分解子目标还是输出可执行的低层动作；轻量记忆模块总结历史结果供后续决策复用；当低层动作停滞时触发分层回溯重规划，返回父节点并基于最新状态重新生成子树计划。

Result: 在 ALFRED 基准的零样本评估中，AtomTree 优于先前的基于 LLM 的规划器。

Conclusion: AtomTree 通过状态感知的分层规划、记忆复用和失败触发的回溯重规划，避免了单次串行规划和沿错误轨迹持续漂移的问题，从而提升了具身任务规划的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Embodied instruction following in interactive environments requires agents to interpret natural language commands and complete long-horizon tasks. Existing planners either rely on large labeled trajectories, or use few-shot and zero-shot LLM prompting that produces a whole plan in a single serial reasoning process. These approaches depend on linguistic common sense, commit to a plan from the initial instruction, and only update after failures, which weakens robustness and state awareness. We present AtomTree, a hierarchical framework for state-aware task planning with LLMs. AtomTree incrementally builds a tree of subgoals and actions, where the LLM decides at each step, from the current symbolic state, whether to further decompose or to output a grounded low-level action. A lightweight memory module summarizes past outcomes for reuse in later decisions. When a low-level action stalls, AtomTree triggers hierarchical backtracking replanning to return to the parent node and regenerate the subtree plan from the latest state, preventing continued drift along an incorrect trajectory. In zero-shot evaluation on the ALFRED benchmark, AtomTree outperforms prior LLM-based planners. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [90] [Value Function-Guided MPPI for Hierarchical Planning and Control in Off-Road Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38731)
*Anoushka Alavilli,Raymond Song,Guanya Shi,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双向信息流的层级规划与控制框架，用强化学习控制器的价值函数作为MPPI规划的隐式代价，并通过在线策略评估适应分布偏移，实现无显式代价图的越野自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统越野导航采用层级架构，依赖显式代价图和从高层规划器到低层控制器的单向信息流，导致规划与真实执行代价解耦。

Method: 高层采用MPPI风格规划器确定最优轨迹并将路点传给预训练低层RL控制器；MPPI不依赖显式代价图，而将RL控制器的价值函数作为隐式代价表示，使MPPI用于轨迹探索、价值函数引导搜索；针对训练与评估间的分布偏移，提出在线策略评估以自适应更新价值函数。

Result: 在高保真越野驾驶仿真器的多种挑战地形中，与基线MPPI控制器相比，该方法能更安全、更有效地导航至目标位置，同时无需显式代价图。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了规划与控制更紧密的耦合，并验证了利用RL价值函数进行隐式代价规划及在线适应未见驾驶经验的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in off-road terrain is commonly achieved using hierarchical control and planning architectures that rely on explicit cost maps and one-way information flow from a high-level planner to a low-level controller, decoupling planning from the true execution cost experienced during control. We propose a hierarchical planning and control framework in which information flows bidirectionally, allowing the low-level controller to guide trajectory selection at the planning level. The highest level of the hierarchy is a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control-style planner that determines optimal trajectories and passes these waypoints to a pretrained low-level reinforcement learning (RL) controller. Rather than drawing from an explicit cost map, our MPPI planner leverages the value function of the RL controller as an implicit cost representation. In this formulation, MPPI serves as a trajectory exploration mechanism, while the low-level controller's value function guides the search. To account for the distribution shift seen by the value function between training and evaluation, we propose an online policy evaluation process that adapts the value function to unseen driving experiences, and we demonstrate this adaptation in unseen off-road simulation environments. We evaluate our approach in a high-fidelity off-road driving simulator across a range of challenging terrain conditions and compare its performance against a baseline MPPI controller. The results demonstrate that our method achieves safer and more effective navigation to goal locations while eliminating the need for explicit cost maps and enabling tighter coupling between planning and control.

</details>


### [91] [Quantize by Doing: Task-Guided Mixed-Precision Quantization of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38661)
*Jiuyi Xu,Xiao Hu,Meida Chen,Song Wang,Yang Ye,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QbD-VLA 提出一种以任务成功率为引导的 VLA 模型混合精度训练后量化方法，通过短闭环评估分配权重与激活位宽，并在多个 VLA 模型和基准上验证其低比特下的任务性能与真实部署风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTQ 可降低视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型内存占用，但在降低位宽的同时保持任务性能仍很困难。现有方法多依赖校准数据估计敏感性来分配位宽，而直接用任务成功率指导混合精度分配仍未被充分探索，原因是闭环评估成本高，且某一组件量化效果会受其他组件精度影响。

Method: 提出 QbD-VLA，一种任务引导的混合精度量化方法：利用短闭环评估得到的任务成功率来分配权重和激活位宽，并在分配过程中考虑模型组件之间的交互影响。

Result: 实验覆盖四个 VLA 模型和全部四个 LIBERO 套件，在权重平均约 4 bit、激活少于 6 bit 时仍保持较强任务性能；在 SIMPLER 上相对均匀量化的增益较小；在 Franka Research 3 机械臂上，仿真选择的分配在 X-VLA 上未检测到成功率损失，但在 π_{0.5} 上失败。

Conclusion: 结果表明用任务成功率指导 VLA 混合精度量化是可行的，同时强调部署前仍需进行真实世界评估。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) reduces the memory footprint of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but lowering bit widths while maintaining task performance remains challenging. Existing work has used sensitivity estimates from calibration data to assign bit widths to different model components. However, using task success directly to guide mixed-precision allocation is underexplored because closed-loop evaluations are costly and the effect of quantizing one component depends on the precision of other components. To this end, we propose \textbf{QbD-VLA}, a task-guided mixed-precision quantization method that uses task success from short closed-loop evaluations to assign weight and activation bit widths while accounting for interactions between model components. Experiments covering four VLA models and all four LIBERO suites show strong task performance at average precisions of approximately 4 bits for weights and fewer than 6 bits for activations. On SIMPLER, the gain over uniform quantization is small. On a Franka Research 3 robot arm, simulation-selected allocations show no detected success-rate loss on X-VLA but fail on $\pi_{0.5}$. These findings support using task success to guide mixed-precision VLA quantization, while highlighting the need for real-world evaluation before deployment. Code is available at \href{ this https URL }{\texttt{ this http URL }}.

</details>


### [92] [Explore Where It Matters: Targeted Exploration for Efficient Rover Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38657)
*Jacob Levy,Arthur Bouton,Tristan D. Hassler,Hari Nayar,David Fridovich-Keil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TERRA 是一种面向行星漫游车在可变形陡坡地形上的在线自适应框架：用奖励加权预测损失修正仿真训练的世界模型，并训练损失预测器指导 MPC 探索，以少量真实试验弥合 sim-to-real 差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来行星漫游车需穿越陡峭可变形地形，滑移、沉陷和地形变形威胁移动性；仿真难以建模全部轮-土效应，存在 sim-to-real 差距，而实际任务又不允许大量试错，因此需要部署时高效自适应。

Method: TERRA 先用奖励加权预测损失对仿真训练的世界模型进行在线适配；同时训练一个损失预测器，估计未来与性能相关的模型误差可能出现的位置，并将该信号用于指导模型预测控制（MPC）中的探索，从而主动采集暴露任务相关模型误差的部署数据并修正模型。

Result: 在仿真中，TERRA 在严重分布外预训练条件下约 5 次试验内达到约 90% 爬坡成功率，并在奖励、完成时间和首次成功所需能量上优于探索基线；在 ERNEST 漫游车上，TERRA 在 3 至 5 次真实世界试验内恢复了可变形火星模拟坡面上的协调主动悬架运动。

Conclusion: TERRA 通过面向奖励相关误差的目标探索与在线模型修正，实现了少样本、部署时的高效自适应，展示了在真实行星漫游车可变形地形移动中弥合 sim-to-real 差距的可行性。

Abstract: Future planetary rovers will traverse steep, deformable terrain where slip, sinkage, and terrain deformation can threaten mobility. Although simulation can capture some wheel-soil effects, unmodeled deformable-terrain phenomena create sim-to-real gaps requiring deployment-time adaptation. Yet, rover operations leave little room for extensive trial-and-error. We introduce TERRA (Targeted Exploration for Reward-Relevant Adaptation), an online adaptation framework that efficiently seeks deployment data exposing task-relevant model errors and corrects them. TERRA adapts a simulation-trained world model with a reward-weighted prediction loss and trains a loss predictor to estimate where future performance-relevant errors are likely to occur, using this signal to guide exploration during MPC. In simulation, TERRA reaches approximately 90% slope-climbing success within five trials under severe out-of-distribution pretraining conditions, outperforming exploration baselines in reward, completion time, and energy to first success. On the ERNEST rover, TERRA recovers coordinated active-suspension locomotion on a deformable Mars-simulant slope within three to five real-world trials. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [93] [A Multi-Layer Navigation System for Smart Wheelchairs with a Limited Input Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38569)
*Piero Simonetto,Anna Polato,Francesco Pasti,Muhammad Rameez ur Rahman,Sebastiano Vascon,Nicola Bellotto,Emanuele Menegatti,Stefano Tortora,Luca Tonin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于智能轮椅的多层导航系统（MLNS），可在未知环境中理解并安全地结合用户意图，即使指令有限或存在错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能轮椅依赖人机接口或脑机接口解析高层用户意图，但在未知环境中导航和准确理解用户命令仍具挑战；误判命令或环境会导致用户挫败、不信任系统甚至不安全情况。

Method: 该系统集成基于虚拟传感器驱动的势场法（ViSPF）进行考虑轮椅实际尺寸的障碍物检测，使用模型预测控制（MPC）生成轨迹，采用开放词汇计算机视觉算法识别目标，并通过基于优先队列（PQ）的系统进行目标选择。

Result: 在真实智能轮椅上验证，实验涉及10名无残疾参与者，并包含输入接口故障场景；结果表明系统在真实环境中具有鲁棒性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了在辅助技术实际应用中的潜力，能够在未知环境中安全地解释和情境化用户意图。

Abstract: Smart wheelchairs are robotic devices that gather information from the surroundings and allow people to safely drive through various environments. They usually rely on the interpretation of high-level user's intentions, typically decoded through human-robot interface (HRI) or brain-machine interface (BMI). However, navigating in an unknown environment and accurately understanding user commands remain significant challenges. Misinterpretation of commands or the environment can lead to user frustration, distrust of the system, or even unsafe situations. In this paper, we present a novel Multi-Layer Navigation System (MLNS) for a smart wheelchair that enables navigation in unknown environments while interpreting and safely contextualizing user's intentions even in the case of limited and faulty commands. The stack relies on a Virtual Sensors-Driven Potential Fields (ViSPF) method for obstacle detection that accounts for the wheelchair's actual size, a Model Predictive Control (MPC) module for trajectory generation, and an open-vocabulary computer vision algorithm for target identification. Finally, a system based on Priority Queue (PQ) was implemented for target selection. We validated our system on a real smart wheelchair with experiments involving 10 participants without disabilities, including scenarios with faulty input interfaces. The results demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of our system in real-world environments, proving its potential for practical applications in assistive technologies.

</details>


### [94] [Force-Driven Hook Disassembly via Constraint-Aware Object-Centric Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38513)
*Nadeen Elhadad,Takuya Kiyokawa,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出约束感知强化学习框架，利用接触力在线推断可行拆卸方向，并结合粒子滤波信念实现零样本真实机器人拆卸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报废产品增多使自动拆卸需求上升，但钩型连接方向多变、受载变形且几何被遮挡，仅靠视觉无法确定可行的非破坏性拆卸方向。

Method: 在机器人无关、以物体为中心的简化孔轴配合仿真中训练策略，从接触力推断提取方向；因瞬时接触力噪声大且局部歧义，耦合粒子滤波信念提供时间积分方向信息并减少冗余探索；随后零样本部署到真实机器人并迁移至未见的多阶段约束结构。

Result: 在四类真实任务（孔轴配合至报废空调内螺旋桨和前盖）上验证，完整方法成功率分别为100%、100%、90%和75%，且每项任务路径效率最高；相较无信念策略，空调前盖成功率由45%提升至75%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，力驱动强化学习可在无需产品特定模型或演示的情况下实现拆卸。

Abstract: The growing volume of end-of-life products has intensified the need for automated disassembly, yet hook-type connections remain challengin: their orientations vary, they deform under load, and their geometry is hidden, so vision alone cannot identify a feasible non-destructive disassembly direction. This work proposes a constraint-aware reinforcement learning framework that infers feasible extraction directions online from contact forces. The policy is trained in a robot-agnostic, object-centric simulation on a simplified peg-in-hole pair and deployed zero-shot on a physical robot, where it transfers to real connections with unseen, multi-stage constraint structures. Because instantaneous contact forces are noisy and locally ambiguous, the policy is coupled with a particle filter belief that provides the policy with temporally integrated directional information and reduces redundant exploratory motion. The approach is validated on four real-world tasks, from peg-in-hole pairs to the inner propeller and front cover of an end-of-life air-conditioning unit. The full method achieves success rates of 100%, 100%, 90%, and 75% with the highest path efficiency on every task, and raises the success rate on the air-conditioning cover from 45% to 75% relative to a policy trained without the belief. These results indicate that force-driven reinforcement learning can enable disassembly without product-specific models or demonstrations.

</details>


### [95] [SaiVLA-JEPA: Evaluating Future Latent Prediction in JEPA-Conditioned VLA Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38508)
*Rufeng Xiao,Ruihao Xiao,Wei Chen,Xinhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究检验在VLA策略已使用V-JEPA特征时，JEPA式表示预测目标能否提升闭环控制，并提出SaiVLA-JEPA；结果显示当前JEPA条件化成功率约82.5%，显著高于无JEPA的71.6%，但未来表示监督未带来可检测的总体增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA通过预测表示而非像素来紧凑建模视觉场景演化；作者追问这种预测目标在视觉-语言-动作策略已采用V-JEPA特征时，是否还能改善闭环控制性能。

Method: SaiVLA-JEPA将视觉语言模型与扩散Transformer动作专家结合，并接收冻结V-JEPA编码器特征；训练时用两个轻量辅助头预测未来表示，推理时移除且不改变动作采样器。在10,030个配对的LIBERO-Plus回合上比较无JEPA特征、当前JEPA条件化和额外未来监督；另评估适配潜在上下文的SaiVLA-JEPA-TCA与适配部分参数的SaiVLA-JEPA-TPA。

Result: 当前JEPA条件化达到约82.5%成功率，而无JEPA为71.6%；在评估训练预算下，未来监督未显示可检测的总体增益。TCA的上下文重试增益是在更大尝试预算下的系统级结果；TPA相对冻结策略和目标对照没有一致优势。

Conclusion: 应通过配对的闭环 rollout 评估潜在预测目标，而不能仅依赖潜在损失。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn to predict representations rather than pixels, offering a compact way to model how visual scenes evolve. We ask whether this predictive objective improves closed-loop control when a vision-language-action (VLA) policy already uses V-JEPA features. We call our JEPA-conditioned flow policy family SaiVLA-JEPA. It combines a vision-language model with a diffusion transformer action expert and receives features from a frozen V-JEPA encoder. Two lightweight auxiliary heads predict future representations during training and are omitted at inference, leaving the action sampler unchanged. We compare policies without JEPA features, with current JEPA conditioning, and with additional future supervision on 10,030 paired LIBERO-Plus episodes. Current JEPA conditioning reaches about 82.5% success versus 71.6% without JEPA, while future supervision provides no detectable aggregate gain at the evaluated training budget. We separately evaluate SaiVLA-JEPA-TCA, which adapts latent context, and SaiVLA-JEPA-TPA, which adapts selected parameters. The context-retry gain is a system-level result under a larger attempt budget; parameter adaptation shows no consistent advantage over the frozen policy and target controls. These results motivate assessing latent-prediction objectives through paired closed-loop rollouts rather than latent loss alone.

</details>


### [96] [OmniQuad: a Wheeled-Legged Hybrid Robot with Omnidirectional Wheels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38502)
*Francesco Iotti,Alok Ranjan,Franco Angelini,Manolo Garabini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合机器人OmniQuad，将麦克纳姆轮与腿式运动结合，以兼顾腿式机器人的地形适应性和轮式机器人的平地效率及全向机动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人对崎岖地形适应性强但室内速度较低；轮式机器人平地效率高但难以跨越台阶等障碍。因此需要融合二者优势的混合平台。

Method: 设计带麦克纳姆轮的自定义轮足，构建由轮足与EM-Act执行器组成的OmniQuad整机，并开发相应控制方案，在室内外环境中开展实验评估。

Result: 摘要仅说明进行了室内外实验评估，未给出具体定量性能指标或对比结果；强调麦克纳姆轮赋予全向移动能力，可在狭窄空间灵活运动并在任务执行中保持朝向。

Conclusion: OmniQuad展示了轮腿混合与全向轮足结合的可行性，有望利用全向机动性和腿式越障能力提升复杂环境下任务执行表现，但需具体实验数据进一步验证其性能优势。

Abstract: Combining wheeled and legged locomotion within a single robotic platform offers the potential to harness the advantages of both systems. The development of such hybrid systems remains an active area of research. While legged robots exhibit remarkable adaptability for traversing uneven and rugged terrains, their relatively low speed limits their practicality in indoor environments. Conversely, wheeled robots demonstrate higher efficiency on flat surfaces but often encounter difficulties when navigating obstacles such as steps. This paper introduces OmniQuad, a novel hybrid robot that integrates mecanum wheels with legged locomotion to exploit the benefits of both modalities. Mecanum wheels provide omnidirectional mobility, enhancing the robot's manoeuvrability by enabling agile movement in confined spaces and the ability to maintain a consistent orientation during task execution. In this work, we first present the design of a custom wheeled-foot featuring mecanum wheels. Subsequently, we describe the complete robot assembly, comprising these wheeled feet and EM-Act actuators. Finally, we detail the control scheme developed to govern the OmniQuad and evaluate its performance through experimental trials conducted in both indoor and outdoor environments.

</details>


### [97] [Skeleton-Guided Geometry-Aware Auto-Labeling for Robotic Grasping: From 4-DoF Learning to 6-DoF Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38443)
*Ali Sabzejou,Farbod Azimmohseni,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于骨架的自动抓取标注方法：4-DoF 用骨架生成双指抓取关键点与稳定可行区域标签，并训练 SkelGG-CNN；6-DoF 则将广义准三维骨架化扩展到点云，实现无需人工的几何感知抓取标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人抓取依赖大规模人工标注数据集，标注成本高；需要自动化、一致且可扩展到 4-DoF 与 6-DoF 的抓取标注流程，并支持轻量模型在小数据集上高效训练。

Method: 1）提出基于直骨架的两指抓取关键点生成与稳定可行抓取区域识别流程，自动产生一致的 4-DoF 抓取标注；2）提出 SkelGG-CNN，一种 4-DoF 网络架构，从骨架引导中学习几何抓取特征；3）为扩展到 6-DoF 抓取，开发直接在点云上运行的广义准三维骨架化算法，实现自动 6-DoF 标签生成。

Result: 使用自动生成的 4-DoF 标签，SkelGG-CNN 在 Jacquard V1、Jacquard V2 和 Cornell 上分别达到 95.42%、96.55% 和 97.1% 的准确率；在 GraspNet novel split 上，6-DoF 自动标注取得 18.33 的平均精度，与先进模型方法相当；仿真中 6-DoF 方法在 Dex-Net 和 EGAD! 上成功率分别为 98.4% 和 89.1%；Delta 并联机器人真实实验在单一家居物体和杂乱场景中成功率分别为 97.4% 和 95.0%，无需域适应即优于先进方法。

Conclusion: 4-DoF 流程可跨环境适应，自动标注接近人类精度，使轻量模型能基于小数据集高效训练；广义 6-DoF 方法为杂乱三维场景提供可扩展、几何感知的抓取标注，并展现出较强的仿真与真实抓取性能。

Abstract: Robotic grasping relies on large manually annotated datasets, which are costly to create. We introduce a straight-skeleton-based algorithm for generating two-finger grasp keypoints and identifying regions suitable for stable, feasible grasps. Our automated pipeline produces consistent grasp annotations without human intervention. We also propose the Skeleton-based Generative Grasp Convolutional Neural Network (SkelGG-CNN), a 4-DoF architecture that learns geometric grasp features from skeleton-based guidance. To extend auto-labeling to 6-DoF grasping, we develop a generalized quasi-3D skeletonization algorithm that operates directly on point clouds. Using automatically generated 4-DoF labels, SkelGG-CNN achieves accuracies of 95.42%, 96.55%, and 97.1% on Jacquard V1, Jacquard V2, and Cornell, respectively. On the GraspNet novel split, 6-DoF auto-labeling achieves an average precision of 18.33, comparable to state-of-the-art model-based approaches. In simulation, the 6-DoF method achieves success rates of 98.4% on Dex-Net and 89.1% on EGAD!. Real-world experiments with a Delta parallel robot show success rates of 97.4% on single household objects and 95.0% in cluttered scenes, outperforming state-of-the-art methods without domain adaptation. The 4-DoF pipeline adapts across environments and produces annotations approaching human-level precision, enabling efficient training of lightweight models on small datasets. The generalized 6-DoF method provides scalable, geometry-aware annotations for cluttered 3D scenes.

</details>


### [98] [SpaceAct: Benchmarking 3D Spatial and Temporal Reasoning via Closed-Loop Action and Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38405)
*Chenglong Shi,Zekai Yuan,Yue Song,Jon Pu,Will Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SpaceAct 交互式基准与 SAPA 时空主动探测智能体框架，通过闭环物理动作与主动感知评估 MLLM 的 3D 时空推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 要在 3D 空间中解决问题，需具备从遮挡 2D 视图重建 3D 几何、预测物理动态并执行精确闭环动作的 3D 时空推理能力；现有研究多在静态图像上做被动 VQA 或符号规划，无需执行、常提供特权坐标，且忽略碰撞、静力学与时间约束，无法充分评估真实 3D 交互能力。

Method: 构建 SpaceAct 交互式基准，基于互锁积木与机械谜题，包含 6 类 36 个任务族共 108 个验证实例，不提供特权坐标，并设 +plan、+state、no-camera、blind、L7 levers 等诊断模式；任务如搭桥、穿孔要求模型旋转相机解决遮挡、读取场景内标尺、在扫掠碰撞与静力学检查下输出 6-DoF 位姿，并通过分屏回滚反馈自我纠正，动态任务在思考时冻结时间并提供五帧频闪；同时提出 SAPA 时空主动探测智能体框架。

Result: 在 44 个最难实例上评估 10 个前沿 MLLM，得分范围 4.8–79.1，其中 7 个模型最多仅解决 27.3% 的 6-DoF 任务；对前三名模型，+plan 缩小 44% 差距但每回合仍有 5.2 次碰撞，+state 缩小 90% 并将碰撞降至 0.5；对另外四个模型，+plan 将平均分从 10.9 提至 35.3，+state 提至 81.8；46% 的失败为碰撞或观察循环。

Conclusion: SpaceAct 与 SAPA 将评估推进到闭环物理交互，表明即使给定有效计划，MLLM 仍受限于 3D 视觉 grounding 与动作细化，并为主动多视图探索和显式 3D grounding 提供测试平台。

Abstract: To solve problems in 3D space rather than merely describe images, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need 3D spatiotemporal reasoning: reconstructing 3D geometry from occluded 2D views, anticipating physical dynamics, and executing precise, closed-loop actions. Prior studies test passive VQA on static images or symbolic planning, requiring no execution or supplying privileged coordinates while ignoring collision, statics, and timing. We introduce SpaceAct, an interactive benchmark evaluating 3D spatiotemporal reasoning via closed-loop physical action and active perception, with SAPA, a Spatiotemporal Active-Probing Agent framework. Built on interlocking bricks and mechanical puzzles, it has 108 verified instances across 36 task families in six categories, without privileged coordinates, and diagnostic modes (+plan, +state, no-camera, blind, L7 levers). In brick-bridging or aperture-threading, models rotate the camera to resolve occlusion, read in-scene rulers, output 6-DoF poses under swept-collision and statics checks, and self-correct via split-screen rollback feedback (dynamic tasks freeze time during deliberation and give five strobe frames). Across 10 frontier MLLMs on the 44 hardest instances, scores span 4.8-79.1 (seven solve at most 27.3% of 6-DoF tasks); for the top three, a reference plan (+plan) closes 44% of the gap while leaving collisions at 5.2 per episode, whereas adding exact 3D state (+state) closes 90% and cuts collisions to 0.5; for four others, +plan lifts the mean score from 10.9 to 35.3 and +state to 81.8; and 46% of failures are collision or observation loops. SpaceAct and SAPA advance evaluation to closed-loop physical interaction, showing that even with a valid plan, MLLMs remain bottlenecked by 3D visual grounding and action refinement, while offering a testbed for active multi-view exploration and explicit 3D grounding.

</details>


### [99] [When "No Worries" Went Wrong: HRI Lessons for Foundation-Model Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38400)
*Eliahu Khalastchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文基于三年ROS 2人机交互联合项目的工程经验，提出机器人基础模型能力不等于情境变化能正确影响行动，并给出五项设计经验与轻量审查方法TRACE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型让机器人具备更强的感知、推理与行为生成能力，但能力本身不能保证变化的情境会塑造最终到达人的行动；证据可能未被获取，目的或约束可能在规划与执行中丢失，系统甚至可能在实际行动已不再服务目的时报告成功。

Method: 作者总结联合项目经验，提出关于注意力、知识、承诺、具身时机和行动权威的五项设计经验；引入TRACE，一种轻量审查方法，通过改变一个交互前提并追踪至决策或执行边界，产出可操作的架构发现；并对一个已发表的LLM生成行为树系统进行有界分析。

Result: 案例表明，系统中可用的澄清机制可能未被使用；TRACE能够从交互前提变化追踪到架构层面的责任与执行问题，并形成可操作发现。

Conclusion: 工程教训是保留问责边界，而不是保留过去曾暴露这些边界的计算流水线。

Abstract: When a person declined our robot's invitation to play, Alice turned her shoulder away and said "No worries, it's all good." Only after a brief pause did she drive off. The words and gestures were plausible but their order changed the message. In another episode, Alice informed a host that his visitor had arrived---while the visitor stood right beside him. Both came from a three-year ROS 2 HRI consortium that integrated large language and vision models as they emerged. Today's foundation models give robots broader capabilities to perceive their surroundings, reason about situations and generate behavior. Yet capability alone does not ensure that a changed situation shapes the action that reaches a person. Evidence may never be acquired, or a purpose or constraint may fail to survive planning and execution. A system may even report Success when an action no longer serves its intended purpose. Drawing on the consortium's engineering experience, we develop five design lessons about attention, knowledge, commitments, embodied timing and action authority. We then introduce TRACE, a lightweight review that changes one interaction premise, follows it to a decision or enforcement boundary, and produces an actionable architecture finding. A bounded analysis of a published LLM-generated behavior-tree system illustrates how an available clarification mechanism can go unused. The engineering lesson is to preserve accountability boundaries, not the computational pipelines that once exposed them.

</details>


### [100] [ELEVATE: Agentic Controller Acquisition from Task Failure for Quadrupedal Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38388)
*Shiqi Liu,Radu Corcodel,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ELEVATE 通过任务程序精炼中的持续性失败识别缺失的全身体控制能力，并用编码智能体自动合成强化学习控制器，扩充能力库以完成复杂四足操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机械臂可通过全身体交互扩展工作空间，但任务规划智能体的能力受限于现有控制器库，缺少自动获取缺失全身体控制技能的机制。

Method: ELEVATE 在任务程序精炼过程中利用持续性失败判断缺失的物理能力；当判定为能力缺失时，由编码智能体构建并迭代精炼强化学习问题，指定行为特定奖励、课程和终止条件；验证后的控制器加入能力库，并与已有控制器组合重试任务。

Result: 从单一预训练 loco-manipulation 控制器出发，ELEVATE 在七个仿真任务中自主获得墙支撑爬升、支撑操作和墙下降控制器，并复用于后续到达与物体操作任务；在超出初始控制器工作空间的五个任务上，五次独立运行平均成功率为 98.4%-100%，固定控制器库的编码智能体基线失败；合成任务程序与控制器无需真实机器人训练或微调即可迁移到物理四足机械臂，在货架取物和地面到货架放置中分别达到 18/20 和 16/20 成功。

Conclusion: ELEVATE 通过失败驱动的强化学习问题合成与控制器库扩展，使任务规划智能体能够自主获取并复用缺失的全身体控制能力，并实现从仿真到真实四足机械臂的有效迁移。

Abstract: Quadrupedal manipulators can extend their workspace through whole-body interaction, but task-planning agents' capabilities remain limited by their available controllers. We present ELEVATE, a framework that uses persistent failures during task-program refinement to identify and acquire missing whole-body control capabilities. When these failures suggest a missing physical capability, a coding agent formulates and iteratively refines a reinforcement-learning problem, specifying behavior-specific rewards, curricula, and termination conditions. Validated controllers are added to the capability library and composed with existing controllers to retry the task. Starting from a single pretrained loco-manipulation controller, ELEVATE autonomously acquires controllers for wall-supported elevation, supported manipulation, and wall descent across a sequence of seven simulation tasks. These controllers are reused in subsequent reaching and object-manipulation tasks. On the five tasks beyond the initial controller's workspace, ELEVATE achieves mean success rates of 98.4%-100% across five independent runs, while coding-agent baselines with a fixed controller library fail to complete these tasks. The synthesized task programs, together with the acquired controllers, also transfer to a physical quadrupedal manipulator without real-robot training or fine-tuning, achieving 18/20 successes in rack retrieval and 16/20 in floor-to-rack placement. Videos and demonstrations are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [101] [CSO: Refining Robotic Policies via Skill Distribution Alignment and Skill-Grained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38357)
*Zhiyuan Xiang,Xiang Deng,Wanjie Tao,Xu Chen,Zhao Jielun,Weili Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段后训练框架CSO，用于改进基于VQ-VAE等离散化技能的机器人操作策略：先拒绝采样监督微调对齐观测到技能映射，再冻结观测编码器进行技能级策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续动作离散化为技能虽有效，但量化误差导致技能先验分布次优；直接用强化学习精炼面临编码器更新不稳定和重要性采样粒度困境。

Method: CSO包含两阶段：1）Rejection-Sampling Supervised Fine-tuning，利用成功在线轨迹分布，通过监督微调对齐模型的观测到技能映射；2）Skills Policy Optimization，在冻结观测编码器后，为每个完整技能计算独立的裁剪重要性比率，实现稳定高效更新。

Result: 采用轻量QueST风格策略，CSO将LIBERO总体成功率从80.1%提升至97.5%，在RoboTwin2.0上性能提升，并在五个真实单臂任务上达到61.0%成功率；与FAST、离散BEAST和固定缓冲区IQL的对比进一步验证有效性。

Conclusion: CSO通过两阶段后训练有效缓解离散技能先验次优与RL精炼不稳定问题，在仿真和真实机器人任务上显著提升离散技能策略的性能与稳定性。

Abstract: Discretizing continuous actions into discrete skills using methods like VQ-VAE has emerged as a powerful paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, quantization errors yield a suboptimal distribution for the skill prior, degrading its performance. While reinforcement learning offers a path for refinement, its direct application faces unstable encoder updates and a granularity dilemma in importance sampling. To address these challenges, we introduce Cascaded Skills Optimization (CSO), a two-stage post-training framework. First, Rejection-Sampling Supervised Fine-tuning aligns the model's observation-to-skill mapping with the distribution of successful online trajectories via supervised fine-tuning. Second, with the observation encoder frozen, Skills Policy Optimization computes an independent, clipped importance ratio for each complete skill, enabling stable and efficient updates. With a lightweight QueST-style policy, CSO raises overall LIBERO success from 80.1% to 97.5%, improves performance on RoboTwin2.0, and achieves 61.0% success across five physical single-arm tasks. Additional comparisons with FAST, discrete BEAST, and fixed-buffer IQL further validate the effectiveness of our two-stage post-training strategy.

</details>


### [102] [Proprioceptive Finger Force Estimation for Reliable Box Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38351)
*Junha Cha,Chanwoo Kim,Kisang Park,Yeoncheol Jang,Doyoung Kim,Seungmin Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FEEL方法，仅利用本体感知历史估计每指外力大小，并将估计力与预测不确定性用于连续每指力适配，从而提升箱体抓取放置的可靠性与无损性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠箱体操作需在避免损坏的同时维持足够接触力且不过载；作者假设相较开环手指指令，每指力反馈能提高操作可靠性。

Method: FEEL是一种不确定性感知的残差方法：将校准的虚拟弹簧力估计与混合密度网络（MDN）预测的残差分布结合，部署时无需专用接触传感器；估计的每指外力用于连续每指适配，预测不确定性作为本体感知力估计歧义性的经验信号。

Result: 在十指、0–20 N接触力范围内，平均RMSE为0.251 N，相较虚拟弹簧降低92.8%，相较端到端MDN降低9.4%；在箱体抓取放置中，力适配保持任务成功率10/10，并将无损结果与可靠性从7/10提升至10/10。

Conclusion: 基于本体感知的每指外力估计及不确定性信号可支持无接触传感器的连续力适配，实现更可靠且无损的箱体操作。

Abstract: Reliable box manipulation requires successful placement without damage, which depends on maintaining sufficient contact force without excessive loading. We hypothesize that per-finger force feedback improves manipulation reliability relative to open-loop finger commands. To this end, we present Finger External-Force Estimation from Proprioceptive SignaLs (FEEL), an uncertainty-aware residual method that estimates per-finger external force magnitude from proprioceptive histories. Our method combines a calibrated virtual-spring estimate with a residual distribution predicted by a Mixture Density Network (MDN) and requires no dedicated contact sensors during deployment. The estimated force is used for continuous per-finger adaptation, while predictive uncertainty is evaluated as an empirical signal of ambiguity in proprioceptive force estimation. Across ten fingers and contact forces spanning 0-20 N, our method achieves a mean RMSE of 0.251 N, reducing the error by 92.8% relative to the virtual spring and by 9.4% relative to an end-to-end MDN. In box pick-and-place, force adaptation maintains task success at 10/10 and increases both damage-free outcomes and reliability from 7/10 to 10/10. The project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [103] [What Should a Navigator Remember? Spatiotemporal Memory for Zero-Shot Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38318)
*Yuhang Peng,Zhikai Wang,Ziyi Wang,Jiaru Zhong,Xiaoji Zheng,Jingzhi Huang,Yi Wang,Jiangtao Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作研究8B规模零样本VLN系统应保留何种信息及如何组织，通过任务、时序、空间三类互补记忆提升导航决策与真实环境迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本视觉语言导航要求智能体无需导航专用训练即可利用历史观测；核心问题是小型开放模型应保留哪些信息、如何组织以供后续决策，并区分保留证据、供推理器访问与主动获取新观测。

Method: 系统由8B observer总结观测，8B reasoner基于互补记忆选择路点意图或请求额外视角，几何控制器执行目标。记忆包括指令进度（任务记忆）、以位姿为锚的分层压缩观测历史（时序记忆）和累积几何（空间记忆）；从语义信息视角组织证据保留、可访问性与主动观测获取。

Result: 在R2R-CE val-unseen上，完整系统在评估的Qwen3系零样本基线中成功率最高；移除时序记忆会降低SR并使平均路径长度从16.9m增至39.1m，移除空间地图与规划组件也降低SR。Unitree Go2真实实验中，零样本方法在走廊和办公室环境成功率高于后训练基线；LAW2成对指令干预评估显示其指令跟随行为响应。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将任务进度、观测历史与几何信息保存在结构化记忆中，可支持较小开放模型的零样本导航，并展现更好的真实环境迁移潜力。

Abstract: Zero-shot vision-and-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to use past observations without navigation-specific training. We study what information an 8B-scale navigation system should retain and how it should be organized for subsequent decisions. Our agent combines instruction progress, hierarchically compressed observation history anchored to poses, and accumulated geometry in complementary task, temporal, and spatial memories. An 8B observer summarizes observations, and an 8B reasoner uses the memories to select waypoint intents or request additional views, a geometric controller executes the selected targets. A semantic information perspective motivates the distinction between retaining evidence, making it accessible to the reasoner, and acquiring further observations. On R2R-CE val-unseen, the complete system achieves the highest among the evaluated Qwen3-based zero-shot baselines. Removing temporal memory reduces SR and sharply increases average path length from 16.9 to 39.1m, removing the spatial-map and planning component reduces SR as well. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 show that our zero-shot method achieves higher success rates than the post-trained baseline in both corridor and office environments, suggesting better transfer to the evaluated real-world settings. As a complementary evaluation, LAW2 uses paired instruction interventions to examine behavioral responses, instruction following ability beyond endpoint success. These results support preserving task progress, observation history, and geometry in structured memory for zero-shot navigation with smaller open models.

</details>


### [104] [Compositional Diffusion Planning with Chunk Selection and Reachability Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38291)
*Chanyoung Park,Youngjoong Kim,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出选择性组合扩散规划器 SCD，通过可达性建模与图搜索改进长时程扩散规划中的轨迹块组合，减少模式平均与不可行拼接，并生成桥接轨迹提升平滑性和成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合式扩散规划通过采样短轨迹块并拼接成长轨迹来处理长时程任务，但重叠块平均会引发模式平均问题，导致轨迹不连续；现有方法虽尝试解决，仍有大量规划不可行。

Method: SCD 首先以自监督方式训练可达性分类器，联合使用离线数据与生成的轨迹块以缓解分布不匹配并降低生成数据偏差；随后构建以相邻块可达性为边代价的图，遍历图寻找高效可行路径；最后生成桥接轨迹并细化组合轨迹以实现平滑过渡。

Result: 在 OGBench stitching 任务上，SCD 相比所研究基线实际减少了不兼容模式组合与模式平均问题，并在多个任务上提升成功率，在 HumanoidMaze-Large 与 Giant 任务上达到 state-of-the-art。

Conclusion: SCD 通过显式可达性判断、图搜索和桥接细化，有效提升组合式扩散规划的长时程拼接可行性与整体性能。

Abstract: Compositional diffusion planners have demonstrated remarkable success on long-horizon diffusion planning by sampling short trajectories, namely chunks, and composing them into a long trajectory. However, averaging overlapping chunks can induce the mode-averaging problem, resulting in discontinuous trajectories. Several works aim to address this problem, whereas a non-negligible portion of plans remains infeasible. In this work, we propose Selective Compositional Diffuser (SCD), handling this problem by measuring reachability. To this end, we present a self-supervised learning method for the reachability classifier. This is trained on offline data and generated chunks jointly to address potential distributional mismatch, while reducing the bias inherited from the generated data. Based on this, we construct a graph whose edge costs represent whether consecutive chunks are reachable, and traverse the graph to find effective yet efficient routes. Lastly, we generate bridging trajectories and refine the composed trajectory for smooth transitions. We evaluate our method on OGBench stitching tasks. It practically reduces incompatible mode compositions and the mode-averaging problem compared to the studied baselines. Across tasks, SCD improves success rates, reaching state-of-the-art results on HumanoidMaze-Large and Giant tasks.

</details>


### [105] [Recursive Self-Improvement in Physical AI: Mechanisms, Evidence, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38266)
*Xiaofeng Han,Kaize Luo,Zenghuang Fu,Zichong Zhu,Zhe Feng,Changwei Wang,Rongtao Xu,Weiliang Meng,Xiaopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述从“持久能力与其改进过程之间的关联”切入物理AI/具身系统，系统梳理从策略更新到递归自我改进（RSI）的研究，并提出区分适应、持久增益、过程继承与验证改进器增益的L0-L3分级及评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身系统可从部署经验中更新策略、世界模型、技能和可执行程序，使一次交互惠及后续任务；当系统 further 修订并复用数据获取、反馈或学习流程时，经验会改变能力生产过程，形成递归自我改进。现有方法、物理运行条件和评估证据分散，亟需统一视角连接持久能力与改进过程。

Method: 作者先区分学习器更新（learner updates）与改进器（improvers）的功能差异，再比较策略学习、想象经验、记忆与技能积累、可执行harness等途径，并分析其与课程、奖励和研究程序修订的联系；随后讨论接触有效性、恢复、保持和资源成本对部署中增益累积的影响，提出L0-L3分级框架，并结合代表性基准与同研究内对比进行映射；进一步提出共同数据与交互式比较，使参考改进器与修订改进器从共享学习器起点评估，以分离经验的使用与经验的获取。

Result: 论文给出L0-L3分级：任务局部适应、持久能力增益、继承式过程修订、经验证的改进器增益；并通过代表性基准和同研究内对照将上述区分与现有结果关联，说明接触有效性、恢复、保持和资源成本决定增益能否在持续部署中累积。

Conclusion: 该视角将多样方法、物理运行条件和评估证据统一起来，为具身递归自我改进（embodied RSI）提供概念框架与评估基础，并强调需通过共享学习器起点的共同数据和交互式比较来区分经验利用与经验获取。

Abstract: Embodied systems can update policies, world models, skills, and executable programs from deployment experience, allowing one interaction to benefit later tasks. When a system also revises and reuses its data-acquisition, feedback, or learning procedures, experience changes the process that produces capability, introducing recursive self-improvement. This paper surveys research in physical AI through the connection between persistent capability and its improvement process. We first explain the functional distinction between learner updates and improvers, then compare policy learning, imagined experience, memory and skill accumulation, and executable harnesses. These approaches connect to revisions of curricula, rewards, and research programs in different ways. Contact validity, recovery, retention, and resource costs determine whether the resulting gains accumulate during continued deployment. An L0-L3 rubric distinguishes task-local adaptation, persistent capability gain, inherited process revision, and verified improver gain. Representative benchmarks and within-study contrasts relate these distinctions to existing results. We further propose common-data and interactive comparisons that evaluate reference and revised improvers from shared learner starts, separating the use of experience from its acquisition. This perspective connects the diverse methods, physical operating conditions, and evaluation evidence, underpinning embodied RSI. A comprehensive list of studies in this survey is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [MATCH: Execution-Aware Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38248)
*Shuhao Ye,Yufei Wei,Demao Wang,Shichao Zhai,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MATCH 提出联合学习语言条件规划器与目标条件底层动作执行器的 VLN-CE 框架，通过执行感知拓扑图、密集宏奖励和同步 on-policy 课程训练，在仅 0.5B 参数下取得 R2R-CE 与 RxR-CE 的最优性能，并初步验证真实机器人部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有许多分层 VLN-CE 方法主要优化高层规划器，而将底层执行器固定，导致规划与可执行性脱节；在连续环境中同时满足指令一致规划与碰撞约束下的可靠执行仍需联合建模。

Method: MATCH 联合学习语言条件规划器和目标条件原始动作执行器。其执行感知拓扑图表示依赖起点的执行选项，并融合执行器价值估计与已实现结果记忆，使规划器能够选择执行请求并通过有界连续偏移细化目标。方法引入过渡感知的密集宏奖励，在参考路线上的有序进展与执行残差之间平衡，鼓励可执行请求且避免以局部成功替代指令跟随。训练采用分阶段课程，最终进行同步 on-policy 更新：执行器在规划器生成的请求上学习，规划器则根据执行器当前行为调整请求。

Result: MATCH 以 0.5B 参数在 R2R-CE 和 RxR-CE 基准上达到 state-of-the-art 性能；初步真实机器人部署进一步展示了其实际应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将规划器与执行器进行执行感知的联合学习，并结合合适的奖励设计与同步 on-policy 训练，可有效提升连续环境视觉语言导航的指令一致性与可执行性，并具备向真实机器人迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires instruction-consistent planning and reliable execution under collision constraints. Yet many hierarchical approaches optimize the planner while keeping execution fixed. We introduce MATCH, a framework that jointly learns a language-conditioned planner and a goal-conditioned primitive-action executor. An execution-aware topological graph represents start-dependent execution options and integrates executor value estimates with realized outcome memory, enabling the planner to select execution requests and refine their targets through bounded continuous offsets. A transition-aware dense macro reward balances ordered progress along the reference route against execution residual, encouraging executable requests without substituting local success for instruction following. A staged curriculum culminates in synchronized on-policy updates, with the executor learning on planner-generated requests and the planner adapting its requests to the executor's current behavior. With 0.5B parameters, MATCH achieves state-of-the-art performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks. Preliminary real-robot deployments further demonstrate its practical potential.

</details>


### [107] [SimRetract: Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robotic Assistance for Retractor Take-Over in Open Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38234)
*Xucheng Wang,Zhizhou Yang,Sung Eun Kim,Romain Hardy,Michael P. Sampson,Aakash Reddy,Ankit Pal,Xiaoman Zhang,Luyang Luo,Pranav Rajpurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimRetract 首次实现开放手术中外科医生-机器人协作任务（牵开器接管）的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，仅用仿真演示训练即可在真实机器人上完成抓取与交接保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实演示的模仿学习受采集与标注成本限制，且环境变化会导致性能下降；因此需要无需人类演示和真实微调的零样本 sim-to-real 方法，用于手术辅助协作。

Method: 提出 SimRetract：生成面向迁移的仿真演示，策略输入掩码器械图像与抓取几何，学习从非名义状态恢复，并以执行漂移裕度行动；策略仅在仿真演示上训练，不使用人类演示或真实世界微调。

Result: 零样本部署在50次外科医生定义的体模呈现中抓取成功率为90%，交接时保持牵开成功率为78%；对未见牵开器显示相似抓取几何的泛化潜力。在10种策略配置、1200次仿真与真实试验中定位sim-to-real差距：仿真抓取率80-96%，真实机器人仅移除器械掩码即降至抓取22%、保持12%。

Conclusion: 面向迁移的设计使无需人类演示的零样本 sim-to-real 迁移在外科辅助任务中可行。

Abstract: We demonstrate the first zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for a surgeon-robot collaboration task in open surgery: retractor take-over. A robot arm takes a retractor from the surgeon's hand and preserves retraction at handoff, using a policy trained only on simulated demonstrations, without human demonstrations or real-world fine-tuning. Imitation learning from real demonstrations is limited by collection and labeling costs, and by performance degradation when the environment changes. Our framework, SimRetract, generates simulated demonstrations designed for transfer: the policy observes masked instrument images and grasp geometry, learns to recover from off-nominal states, and acts with margin for execution drift. Deployed zero-shot, it grasps the retractor in 90% of 50 surgeon-defined phantom presentations and preserves retraction at handoff in 78%. Results on unseen retractors suggest potential for generalization to instruments with similar grasp geometry. Across ten policy configurations and 1,200 simulated and real trials, we localize the sim-to-real gap to components: in simulation, grasp rates across ablations span 80-96%, while on the real robot removing the instrument mask alone drops performance to 22% grasped and 12% held. Transfer-oriented design thus makes zero-shot sim-to-real transfer feasible for a surgical assistance task without human demonstrations. Code is available here: this https URL .

</details>


### [108] [HERON: Real-Time Obstacle-Aware Reachability Maps for Mobile Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38233)
*G. Dupoiron,F. Michaud,J.P. Roberge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HERON：一种基于 3D U-Net 的障碍物感知可达性地图预测方法，可从实时深度相机在嵌入式硬件上以 2 cm 分辨率预测机械臂周围的可达性，用于移动操作机器人在线重定位决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作机器人因缺少实时、障碍物感知的机械臂可达性信息，通常不敢在操作过程中移动底座；现有可达性地图方法速度慢、分辨率粗（常为 5 cm）或忽略真实障碍物，难以在线使用。

Method: HERON 使用 3D U-Net，先以 cuRobo 的 GPU 加速逆运动学生成可达性地图作为监督，在合成场景训练后再用真实深度数据训练；模型部署于 Jetson AGX Orin，结合 TensorRT 与 INT8 量化，从实时深度图预测机械臂周围 2.56 m 立方体区域的障碍物感知可达性地图。

Result: HERON 平均 115 ms 预测整张地图，系统频率 8.57 Hz，接近障碍物地图 10 Hz 更新率；而 cuRobo 计算同一地图约需 90 分钟。相对 cuRobo 真值，FP32 模型下 95% 体素落在 51 类中的 1 类误差内，INT8 模型下在 3 类误差内；Pearson 相关系数 0.99，二次加权 kappa 高于 0.9，精度主要在 workspace 边界下降。

Conclusion: 神经网络可在嵌入式硬件上以接近在线障碍物地图更新率的速度，从实时深度图预测高分辨率、障碍物感知的可达性地图，为移动操作机器人操作中重定位底座提供可行基础。

Abstract: Mobile manipulator robots rarely reposition their base during manipulation, because doing so requires real-time knowledge of how well the arm can reach its surroundings among obstacles. Reachability maps (RMs) encode this knowledge, but existing methods are slow, coarse (typically 5 cm), or ignore real obstacles. This article asks whether a neural network can predict an obstacle-aware RM at 2 cm resolution from a live depth camera, on embedded hardware, fast enough for online use. We present HERON, a 3D U-Net trained on RMs computed with the GPU-accelerated inverse kinematics of cuRobo, first on synthetic scenes and then on real depth data, and deployed on a Jetson AGX Orin with TensorRT and INT8 quantization. HERON predicts the RM of a 2.56 m cube around the arm in 115 ms on average, for a measured system rate of 8.57 Hz, close to the 10 Hz update rate of the obstacle map, whereas computing the same map with cuRobo takes about 90 min. Against the cuRobo ground truth, 95% of the voxels lie within 1 class out of 51 with the FP32 model and within 3 classes with the INT8 model, with a Pearson correlation of 0.99 and a quadratic weighted kappa above 0.9.
