Accuracy degrades mainly at the workspace boundaries.

</details>


### [109] [CuraEval: Evaluating Demonstration Curation on Mixed-Skill Robot Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38221)
*Jiuyi Xu,Meida Chen,Yang Ye,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CuraEval 对混合技能演示数据的选择方法进行受控评估，发现许多评分/选择策略与简单基线（尤其 episode 长度）高度相关，且经严格统计校正后无 curated 条件显著优于全量数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习常使用不同技能水平操作员的演示，但如何选择训练数据仍困难；现有基于平滑性、多样性或策略性能影响估计的评分方法因选择与评估协议不一致，难以判断是否真正优于简单基线。

Method: 提出 CuraEval：对演示评分进行数学分析，并在混合技能 robomimic 数据上用 Diffusion Policy 与 BC-RNN 做固定样本量匹配的随机子集和全量数据受控比较；还评估适配的几何熵 LOO 选择器，并联合改变 checkpoint 报告、rollout 数量与评估种子，使用 Holm 校正。

Result: PSD 分数仅依赖 episode 长度和位置幅值平方，在 square 上与 episode 长度排序几乎一致（ρ=0.999）。Diffusion Policy 下选最短 50% 演示使 square 成功率从随机选择的 70.7% 提升到 76.5%；但 25% 保留率下几何熵 LOO 使 can 从 94.7% 降至 74.0%，square 为 41.7%，低于三个独立随机子集均值 59.8–62.5%。长度选择在 75% 保留率下于原始协议使 square 从全量 76.7% 提升到 86.7%，但联合改变协议后从 80.5% 降至 79.5%。Holm 校正后无 curated 条件显著优于全量训练，尽管置信区间仍允许有用增益。

Conclusion: 演示选择方法的收益高度依赖评估协议且与 episode 长度等简单基线强相关；需要受控、样本量匹配和统计校正的评估。当前证据不支持 curated 选择稳定显著优于全量数据训练，但潜在实用增益仍不能排除。

Abstract: Robot imitation learning uses demonstrations from operators of varying skill, but selecting training data remains challenging. Existing methods score demonstrations using smoothness, diversity, or estimated influence on policy performance, but differences in selection and evaluation protocols make it difficult to determine whether they outperform simple baselines. We introduce \textbf{CuraEval}, a framework combining mathematical analysis of demonstration scores with controlled comparisons against fixed size-matched random subsets and full-data training on mixed-skill robomimic data using Diffusion Policy and BC-RNN. We show that the evaluated PSD score depends only on episode length and squared position magnitudes, and produces nearly the same ranking as episode length on \textit{square} ($\rho=0.999$). With Diffusion Policy, selecting the shortest 50\% of demonstrations improves \textit{square} success from 70.7\% under random selection to 76.5\%. Compared with random selection, our adapted geometric-entropy LOO selector reduces success at 25\% retention from 94.7\% to 74.0\% on \textit{can} and achieves 41.7\% on \textit{square}, below the mean success rates of all three independently sampled random subsets (59.8--62.5\%). On the same 3 \textit{square} Diffusion Policy training seeds, length-based selection at 75\% retention improves success over full-data training from 76.7\% to 86.7\% under the original protocol, but lowers it from 80.5\% to 79.5\% after checkpoint reporting, rollout count, and evaluation seed change jointly. No evaluated curated condition significantly outperforms full-data training after Holm correction, although confidence intervals still permit useful gains. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [110] [Embodied at Sea: Imitation Learning for Multi-Task Underwater Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38199)
*Greta Perez-Haiek,Luca Macesanu,Kevin Angers,Mohammed Imam,Timothy Gambin,Christopher Clark*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究通用视觉-语言-动作模型在水下机器人操作中的域偏移问题，通过泳池与大范围 marine 现场实验验证行为克隆/VLA 的可行性，并开源 BLUEMBODIED KIT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陆地上行为克隆与通用机器人策略已展现较好泛化能力，但水下视觉畸变、动态水流和现场环境造成严重域偏移，是将通用架构迁移到水下现场操作的主要瓶颈。

Method: 收集平静与湍流水域共1616条水下操作数据，训练14个采用不同本体感知观测配置的通用VLA模型（DynamicVLA）；在泳池3个任务上进行1260次可评分硬件rollout。另开展案例研究，在ROV上比较单任务ACT与通用SmolVLA在海洋考古与保护中的抓取和系泊任务，试验位于马耳他Manoel Island Marina及二战沉船Coralita X127周边，共收集748条episode并进行508次可评分硬件rollout。

Result: 泳池实验中10个模型优于基线，最佳模型总体成功率93.3%（含部分成功）。现场案例中ACT显著优于SmolVLA，汇总成功率47.9%对7.9%；ACT在海洋考古现场最成功任务上达到66.7%成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明通用VLA在水下操作中具备潜力，但单任务专用策略在海洋现场仍明显更强；开源BLUEMBODIED KIT可支持数据采集、模型训练与ROV策略部署，促进海洋现场机器人具身智能研究。

Abstract: Behavior Cloning has demonstrated promising generalization capabilities for robotic manipulation in terrestrial environments. However, marine visual distortions and dynamic currents create a severe domain shift that remains a key bottleneck in adapting generalist architectures to underwater field manipulation. To address this, 1616 episodes of underwater manipulation data were collected in calm and turbulent waters and were used to train 14 generalist Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models (DynamicVLA) with varying proprioception observation configurations. Across three tasks on 1260 gradable on-hardware rollouts in a pool, 10 models outperformed the baseline, with the highest-performing model achieving a 93.3% overall success rate, including partial successes. Results are also introduced from a case study evaluating the performance of a single-task Action Chunking Transformer (ACT) and a generalist VLA (SmolVLA) policy for two tasks in marine archaeology and conservation: grasping and mooring. Experiments are performed on an ROV on and around the WWII wreck Coralita X127 and at Manoel Island Marina, Malta. Across 748 collected episodes and 508 gradable on-hardware rollouts, the ACT policy substantially outperformed SmolVLA (47.9% vs. 7.9% pooled success rate), earning a 66.7% success rate on the most successful task at the marine archaeological site. To enable further contributions to embodied intelligence in marine field robotics, the BLUEMBODIED KIT used to collect data, train models, and deploy policies on the ROV has been open sourced.

</details>


### [111] [Minimum Sufficient Communication for Independent Behaviour Tree Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38176)
*Sherry Jiang,Oliver Obst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种实验框架，用于在运行固定、独立行为树且使用私有黑板（blackboard）的机器人团队中判定“充分通信”策略，并以接收者加权每机器人决策比特数衡量通信量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队需要减少通信开销，但需明确在给定策略族和运行条件下，多少通信仍能保持与全状态通信接近的任务成功率。

Method: 在声明策略族内改变消息内容与准入频率，稠密扫描候选策略并与全状态通信比较；在新配对场景上重测候选与参考策略，用单侧同时置信界判断成功率是否保持在全状态参考的5个百分点内，并判断更便宜策略是否可被排除为不充分；根据证据报告精确最小值、正区间、充分上界或未解决边界。

Result: 在4种仓库条件下测试8种内容策略；低争用条件下零通信为精确最小值；扩展条件下gate q=0.992222的no-release策略被确认充分，成功率达96.33%，估计通信率为738.55 bits/robot decision，仅为全状态通信的0.544%；正最小值仍未解决。

Conclusion: 该框架可给出通信充分性的统计判定，但结论仅适用于所声明策略族、采样gate值和运行条件；低争用下零通信充分，扩展条件下特定no-release策略充分且极低通信，但正最小值未定。

Abstract: We present an experimental framework for determining sufficient communication in robot teams running fixed, independent Behaviour Trees with private blackboards. Within a declared policy family, we vary message content and admission frequency and measure traffic in recipient-weighted bits per robot decision. A dense sweep identifies candidate policies for comparison with full-state communication. We retest selected candidates and the reference on fresh, paired scenarios, using one-sided simultaneous confidence bounds to assess whether success remains within five percentage points of the full-state reference. The bounds also assess whether tested cheaper policies can be ruled out as insufficient. Depending on the evidence, the framework reports an exact minimum, a positive bracket, a sufficient upper bound, or an unresolved boundary. Experiments with eight content policies across four warehouse conditions identify zero communication as the exact minimum under low contention. In the scaled condition, the no-release policy at gate $q=0.992222$ is confirmed sufficient, achieving 96.33% success at an estimated rate of 738.55 bits per robot decision, or 0.544% of full-state traffic. The positive minimum remains unresolved. These claims apply only to the declared policy family, sampled gate values and operating conditions.

</details>


### [112] [SKiD-SLAM: Resource-Aware Distributed Multi-Robot LiDAR SLAM for Planetary Missions with Field Deployments at Analog Sites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38141)
*Hogyun Kim,Juwon Kim,Dongjin Cho,Jiwon Choi,Geonmo Yang,Seokhwan Jeong,Jiyun Lee,Miryeong Park,Hyungtae Lim,Younggun Cho*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SKiD-SLAM 是一种面向行星探索的分布式多机器人 LiDAR SLAM 系统，在通信受限、机载计算受限且无中心基础设施条件下实现协同建图与定位，并在洞穴与行星模拟地表两类任务中完成现场部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来行星探索需要机器人团队在严重通信限制、受限机载计算和无中央基础设施的环境中协同构建未知环境地图，因此需要适配真实任务通信与计算预算的分布式多机器人 LiDAR SLAM 方案。

Method: 作者调研实际行星任务的通信与计算预算，并据此选择用于机器人间地点识别、相对位姿估计和分布式轨迹融合的模块；部署前在类行星公开数据集和基于 NASA JPL Open Source Rover 的模拟器中验证系统；随后在韩国开展现场部署，包括济州岛 Majang 熔岩管洞穴中的轮式机器人团队探索，以及莲川 KICT 行星模拟地形中的四足机器人陨石坑与岩石科学场景。

Result: 在两类现场部署中，SKiD-SLAM 均在 250 kbps 的机器人间通信预算内可靠完成分布式多机器人 LiDAR SLAM，表现出在挑战性现场条件下的可行性。

Conclusion: SKiD-SLAM 证明了面向行星探索的分布式多机器人 LiDAR SLAM 在严格通信与计算约束下的现场可行性，并提供代码与现场数据等补充材料。

Abstract: Future planetary exploration missions require teams of robots to collaboratively map unknown environments under severe communication limits, constrained onboard computation, and no central infrastructure. In this field report, we present SKiD-SLAM, a distributed multi-robot LiDAR simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) system for such environments, and report its deployment across two mission concepts: cave exploration and scientific surface exploration. We survey the communication and computation budgets of actual planetary missions and select modules that fit these budgets for inter-robot place recognition, relative pose estimation, and distributed trajectory fusion. Before field deployment, we verify the assembled system on public datasets resembling planetary environments and in a simulator built on NASA JPL's Open Source Rover. We then validate SKiD-SLAM through field deployments in South Korea: a team of wheeled rovers explores the Majang lava-tube cave on Jeju Island, and quadruped robots carry out a crater-and-rock science scenario on a planetary analog terrain at the Korea Institute of Civil Engineering and Building Technology (KICT) in Yeoncheon. Across both deployments, SKiD-SLAM reliably performed distributed multi-robot LiDAR SLAM within a 250 kbps inter-robot communication budget, demonstrating its feasibility under challenging field conditions. Supplementary materials, including code and field data, are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [113] [Minimum-Time Escape from the Conic Field of View of a Reeds--Shepp Pursuer: the Faster Evader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38125)
*Ubaldo Ruiz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 完整求解更快全向逃逸者逃离Reeds--Shepp追捕者前向锥形视场的最小时间逃逸博弈，覆盖所有速度比 ν>1 与半角 0<α<π/2，并给出逃逸时间刻画及双方最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将此前关于较慢逃逸者的研究推广到更快逃逸者，需在所有速度比和视场半角参数下确定博弈值、特征结构及最优策略。

Method: 将逃逸时间表征为显式特征与标量方程根的选择，并证明其为非预见性策略下每个状态的值；分析从可用部分边界出发的特征为半径 ν 的圆渐开线并终止于尖点，给出两个显式阈值判断其是否在尖点前到达轴、以及逃逸时间跳跃是否在尖点前结束；追捕者采用较慢情形的 relay 策略，逃逸者沿 tributaries 并切换至 primary，或预先按 primaries 的标量方程认证航向；最后用 level-set 与 semi-Lagrangian 计算与值函数比较。

Result: 对 ν>1、0<α<π/2 的全部参数完成求解；在逃逸者扩散线端点 E 上方，逃逸者沿由 tributaries 供给的 universal line 追赶反向追捕者；边界特征为半径 ν 的圆渐开线并在尖点终止；两个阈值决定轴到达与逃逸时间跳跃的结束位置，后一情形中 primaries 会在 barrier 附近折回；数值计算与理论值一致。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了更快逃逸者情形的最小时间逃逸博弈，将结果扩展到所有速度比与半角，并验证了特征结构、最优策略及数值方法的一致性。

Abstract: We continue the study of minimum-time escape of an omnidirectional evader from the forward conic field of view of a Reeds--Shepp pursuer, now for a faster evader, and solve the game for every speed ratio $\nu>1$ and every half-angle $0<\alpha<\pi/2$: the escape time is characterized by explicit characteristics and the selection of roots of a scalar equation, and it is the value of the game in nonanticipative strategies at every state. Above the end $E$ of the evader's dispersal line on the front axis, the evader chases the reversing pursuer along a universal line fed by tributaries. The characteristic from the boundary of the usable part is an involute of a circle of radius $\nu$ ending at a cusp, and two explicit thresholds decide whether it reaches the axis before the cusp and whether the jump of the escape time across it ends before the cusp or runs to it; in the latter case primaries fold back next to the barrier. The pursuer's optimal strategy is the relay of the slower case. The evader follows the tributaries and switches once to a primary, or commits in advance to a heading certified by the scalar equation of the primaries. Level-set and semi-Lagrangian computations are compared with the value.

</details>


### [114] [Same Label, Different Interface: Beyond Action-Space Labels in Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38104)
*Arshia Sangwan,Zhongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 位置/速度动作接口的比较不能只按标签归因；执行通路中的尺度、滤波、反馈和保持动态会显著影响策略学习结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习常把position与velocity action当作单一实验处理，但动作标签并不决定机器人实际执行什么；需要量化被忽略的执行通路属性如何影响接口归因。

Method: 在PegInsert、GearMesh、NutThread三个接触丰富Factory任务上，固定同一学习器和训练预算，比较5种关节空间接口，每条件10个独立训练策略；先在相同位置反馈律下仅改动物理动作尺度或移除EMA，再跨反馈律把速度尺度校准到位置接口的局部原始力矩指令增益。

Result: 仅替换物理动作尺度即使样本中位数评估成功率变化0.645–1.000；移除EMA呈任务依赖变化。校准速度尺度后GearMesh和NutThread样本中位数提高、PegInsert无变化，但PegInsert和GearMesh仍有明显样本中位数残差，NutThread区间包含零。

Conclusion: 即使显式匹配局部增益，反馈与保持动作动态仍未匹配，不能证明位置-速度接口的训练结果等价；动作接口比较应报告实际执行通路并说明所匹配的物理量。

Abstract: Robot-learning studies often compare position and velocity actions as though each label identified a single experimental treatment, yet the label alone does not determine what the robot executes. Before affecting robot motion, bounded policy output passes through filtering, physical scaling, reference construction, feedback, timing, and effort limits. We study how these omitted properties affect attribution under one shared learner and training budget. Across three contact-rich Factory tasks (PegInsert, GearMesh, and NutThread), we compare five joint-space interfaces, with ten independently trained policies per condition. We first remain within the same position-feedback law and vary the executed interface: substituting only the physical action scale as the directly modified component changes sample-median evaluation success by 0.645-1.000 across tasks, while removing an exponential moving average produces task-dependent changes. We then move to a cross-law comparison and choose a velocity scale that exactly matches the position interface's local raw-torque command gain. This calibration raises the sample median on GearMesh and NutThread, with no median change on PegInsert. Yet clear residual sample-median gaps remain on PegInsert and GearMesh, while the NutThread interval includes zero. Thus, even this explicit equality leaves feedback and held-action dynamics unmatched and does not establish position-velocity trained-outcome equivalence. Action-interface comparisons should therefore report the executed pathway and state the physical quantity they match.

</details>


### [115] [Efficacy of Monocular Depth Estimation in Lunar Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38080)
*Michael W. Lanighan,Kenji Brameld,Seth Gee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究评估多种先进单目深度估计方法在分布外月面环境中的三维重建能力，并证明部分方法可生成高保真相对三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计在地球域内已实现高精度三维重建，但其在月球等分布外、非结构化环境中的有效性尚不明确，需要验证跨域泛化能力。

Method: 使用NASA Ames POLAR立体数据集和Apollo 17月面影像，构建从模拟月面到实地月面的验证流程：对物理真值模拟数据进行定量评估，对历史实地数据进行定性评估，并测试多种最先进方法。

Result: 部分最先进技术能够成功泛化到非结构化月面，生成高保真的相对三维重建。

Conclusion: 先进单目深度估计方法在分布外月面场景具有应用潜力；所建立的模拟到实地验证流程可用于跨域性能评估。

Abstract: Recent advances in monocular depth estimation have demonstrated highly accurate 3D reconstructions within in-distribution terrestrial domains. This study investigates the efficacy of several state-of-the-art approaches to reconstruct out-of-distribution lunar environments using the NASA Ames Polar Optical Lunar Analog Reconstruction (POLAR) stereo dataset and Apollo 17 surface imagery. By leveraging these datasets, we establish a robust analog-to-field validation pipeline that evaluates quantitative performance against ground-truth physical analogs and qualitative performance against historical field data. Our results indicate that select state-of-the-art techniques can successfully generalize to produce high-fidelity relative 3D reconstructions of the unstructured lunar surface.

</details>


### [116] [EARS-Info: Information-Aware Event-Driven Reallocation for Multi-Robot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38040)
*Kabeh Mohsenzadegan,Vahid Tavakkoli,Kyandoghere Kyamakya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EARS-Info 是面向 OpenStreetMap 派生栅格多机器人探索的信息感知型事件驱动蚁群重分配搜索扩展，在 168,000 个回合中取得最高平均运行得分，但组件贡献与泛化性仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人探索需要协调感知收益、重定位可行性、返程路由、感知预留与路径复用，以提升探索效率、发现覆盖和能耗表现。

Method: 在 Event-Driven Ant Reallocation Search 基础上加入边际感知增益、可行重定位、返程感知路由、感知预留和缓存最短路径；在八张城市地图上比较 30 种实现，共 168,000 个回合。

Result: EARS-Info 在每个评估面板中平均运行得分最高；八机器人、3% 通信丢包下较 EARS-Safe 得分提升 5.43%，加权发现率由 88.88% 升至 98.46%，平均能耗降低 2.62%；八城市得分差均为正（p=0.0078125，城市自助法 95% 区间 [0.0159,0.0694]），5,600 个 EARS-Info 回合无模拟事故。

Conclusion: 结果支持完整控制器的整体有效性，但各单独组件优势仍不确定；泛化性受限于重复使用地理环境、单一训练重复、粗粒度地图及适配后的对比方法。

Abstract: We introduce EARS-Info, an information-aware extension of Event-Driven Ant Reallocation Search for multi-robot exploration on OpenStreetMap-derived grids. It combines marginal sensing gain, feasible relocation, return-aware routing, sensing reservations, and cached shortest paths. Across 168,000 episodes comparing 30 implementations on eight city maps, EARS-Info achieves the highest mean operational score in every evaluation panel. With eight robots and 3% communication dropout, it improves the score over EARS-Safe by 5.43%, increases weighted discovery from 88.88% to 98.46%, and reduces mean energy use by 2.62%. Score differences are positive in all eight cities ($p=0.0078125$; descriptive 95% city-bootstrap interval $[0.0159,0.0694]$), with no simulated incidents across 5,600 EARS-Info episodes. These results support the complete controller; individual component advantages remain uncertain. Generalization is limited by reused geography, one training replicate, coarse maps, and adapted comparison methods.

</details>


### [117] [METATRON: A Promptable Whole-Body Execution Layer for Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38027)
*Chen Tessler,Yifeng Jiang,Alec Otto,Yi Shi,Umar Iqbal,Gal Chechik,Xue Bin Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Metatron 是一种可提示（promptable）的人形机器人全身执行层，将生成式基础策略与可编程技能层结合；基础策略冻结后，技能以普通代码形式表达任务意图，并由编码智能体迭代优化，无需任务特定策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人控制器通常需要针对每个任务单独训练，尽管不同任务共享平衡与全身协调能力；作者希望复用已学习的运动能力，通过代码快速构建新技能。

Method: 基础策略接收由定时身体部位目标、速度指令和风格线索组合而成的 prompt，生成多样且一致的全身运动；训练数据包括动作捕捉、合成恢复轨迹和重定向场景交互运动，训练后策略冻结。技能是有状态程序，在每个控制步将任务意图表达为 prompt，并给出粗略几何草图，由策略转化为协调运动；技能为普通代码，可由编码智能体分析运动数据并拟合紧凑规则来改进。

Result: 在物理 Unitree G1 上，单一冻结检查点支持行走、恢复、遥操作物体拾取和坐家具；对比评估显示其到达成功率和抗推鲁棒性高于测试的参考跟踪流水线。同一方法在仿真角色上产生攀爬和杂技动作。

Conclusion: Metatron 使编码智能体能够通过代码将已学习的运动能力转化为新技能，而无需进一步训练策略。

Abstract: Humanoid controllers often require task-specific training despite sharing the need for balance and whole-body coordination. We present Metatron, a promptable whole-body execution layer combining a generative base policy with a programmable skill layer. The base policy receives partial goals, collectively called a prompt, that can combine timed body-part targets, velocity commands, and style cues. It produces diverse whole-body motions consistent with these goals. Training combines motion capture, synthetic recovery trajectories, and retargeted scene-interaction motions. After training, the policy is frozen. Stateful programs called skills express task intent as prompts at each control step, drawing rough geometric sketches that the policy turns into coordinated movement. Skills are ordinary code, refined by coding agents that analyze motion data and fit compact rules without task-specific policy training. On the physical Unitree G1, one frozen checkpoint supports walking, recovery, teleoperated object pickup, and sitting on furniture. Comparative evaluations show higher reaching success and push robustness than the tested reference-tracking pipelines. The same recipe produces climbing and acrobatics on a simulated character. Metatron enables coding agents to turn learned motor capabilities into new skills through code, without further policy training.

</details>


### [118] [Self-Labeled Preference Transfer for Steerable Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38000)
*Kaiwen Hong,Shaoting Peng,Xiangyu Zeng,Pengcheng Wang,Boyuan Tian,Haonan Chen,Yifan Zhu,Yixiao Wang,Katherine Driggs-Campbell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出自标注偏好迁移（SPT），在仅有源任务偏好标注、目标任务无偏好标注的条件下，将VLA策略的偏好控制能力迁移到新任务，并保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略可从带偏好标注的示范中学习执行偏好（如抓取位置、绕障方式），但为新任务逐一标注偏好成本高昂；核心问题是能否将已学习的偏好控制迁移到有目标行为示范但缺少偏好标注的新任务。

Method: 先分析发现：带偏好标注训练的VLA策略在新任务上可初步遵循偏好但任务成功率有限；在无偏好标注的目标示范上微调可提升成功率但削弱偏好控制。据此提出SPT：在带标注源示范上联合训练共享视觉-语言骨干进行偏好条件动作预测与偏好识别；随后冻结偏好识别器以保留跨任务偏好识别能力，并用其为目标示范自标注偏好，用于策略微调。

Result: 在仿真中覆盖五类执行偏好，识别器达到96.4%准确率；SPT达到84.8%偏好遵循率和89.2%任务成功率，朴素微调为59.4%和88.0%。扩展到多偏好设置，SPT偏好遵循率达93.8%，基线为42.5%。真实世界两次任务迁移中，SPT将偏好遵循率从朴素微调的52.5%提升至92.5%。

Conclusion: SPT能够在无需额外人工偏好标注的情况下实现跨任务、跨物体的偏好适应，并在保持任务成功率的同时显著提升偏好遵循能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies can learn to follow execution preferences, such as where to grasp an object or how to move around an obstacle, from demonstrations annotated with preference labels. Providing these annotations for every new task, however, is costly. We study whether learned preference control can transfer to new tasks whose demonstrations contain the desired behaviors but lack preference annotations. We find that a policy trained with preference annotations can follow preferences on a new task before adaptation but achieves limited task success. Fine-tuning on target demonstrations without these annotations improves task success while weakening preference control. Motivated by this finding, we introduce Self-Labeled Preference Transfer (SPT). SPT jointly trains a shared vision-language backbone for preference-conditioned action prediction and preference recognition on annotated source demonstrations. It then freezes the recognizer to preserve its cross-task preference recognition capability and uses it to self-label target demonstrations for policy fine-tuning. Across five types of execution preferences in simulation, the recognizer achieves 96.4% accuracy, and SPT reaches 84.8% preference following and 89.2% task success, compared with 59.4% and 88.0% for naive finetuning. We further extend to multiple preferences, achieving 93.8% preference following compared with 42.5% of baselines. In real-world experiments, SPT increases preference following from 52.5% to 92.5% relative to naive finetuning across two task transfers. These results show that SPT can support preference adaptation across tasks and objects while maintaining success, without additional manual preference annotations.

</details>


### [119] [Active Constraints for Simultaneous Guidance and Safety in Handheld Micromanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37981)
*Roger Ho,Robert A. MacLachlan,Cameron N. Riviere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出面向手持显微操作机器人的基于优化的主动约束框架，可同时实现任务引导与安全保障，并在医学相关人工任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手持机器人的主动约束常难以同时提供引导和强制安全；现有优化实现多面向独立机器人，忽略手持机器人相对操作者手部驱动且工作空间受限的特点。

Method: 构建适用于手持显微操作的优化式主动约束框架，将任务相关约束与安全约束统一处理，并考虑机器人相对手部运动及有限工作空间，在手持机器人平台上实现并开展医学相关人工任务实验。

Result: 相比基线，所提方法将安全违规率降低两个数量级以上，同时恢复了仅执行安全保障时约三倍的精度损失。

Conclusion: 优化式主动约束可同时兼顾手持显微操作中的引导与安全，并适用于相对手部驱动、工作空间受限的手持机器人场景。

Abstract: In micromanipulation, erroneous hand motion limits unaided manipulation accuracy, which can affect task performance and safety in critical applications like microsurgery. Handheld robots (micromanipulators) enable motion accuracy beyond human limits by providing robotic assistance in a low-cost, compact, intuitive form factor. Particularly, active constraints are an effective form of robotic assistance, which aids the operator by restricting the robot's permissible motion based on task-specific requirements or objectives, thereby providing task guidance and enforcing safety. However, existing formulations of active constraints for handheld systems are limited, most notably because of the inability to provide guidance and enforce safety simultaneously. Implementing active constraints with optimization-based methods can address these limitations, but existing implementations are designed for free-standing robots, and neglect that handheld robots actuate relative to the operator's hand with a limited workspace. In this work, we propose an optimization-based active constraint framework for handheld micromanipulation that enables simultaneous guidance and safety enforcement. The formulation was implemented in a handheld robot and demonstrated on a medically relevant artificial task. Results show that the proposed formulation reduced safety violation rate by more than two orders of magnitude relative to baselines, while recovering the threefold accuracy loss from enforcing safety alone.

</details>


### [120] [FORGE: Feasible Online Reduced-Order Reference Generation and Whole-Body Execution for Agile Humanoid Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37974)
*Adrian B. Ghansah,Yujin An,Jeeseop Kim,Soon-Jo Chung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段学习框架，将在线降阶模型（ROM）参考生成与全身跟踪控制耦合，实现多才且敏捷的人形机器人运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需要在广泛指令空间和多种运动策略下实现灵巧运动，但直接在全身动力学上学习统一策略较困难；利用简化ROM生成运动学-动力学一致的参考可降低学习难度并统一框架。

Method: 第一阶段使用单刚体动力学结合完整人形运动学，训练指令条件化的全身参考生成器；第二阶段在线滚动冻结的ROM策略生成参考，并在全阶动力学模型上训练全身跟踪策略。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上实现纵向速度-2.0 m/s至2.8 m/s、横向速度±1.0 m/s、角速度±3.0 rad/s、单腿跳跃以及指令间连续切换，且仅使用一个统一的参考策略和一个全身跟踪策略。

Conclusion: 结果表明，ROM参考生成与全身跟踪相结合的两阶段耦合框架能够以统一策略覆盖广泛指令和多种运动模式，实现敏捷且连续的人形机器人运动。

Abstract: This paper presents a two-stage learning framework for versatile and agile humanoid locomotion that couples online reduced-order model (ROM) reference generation with whole-body tracking control. In the first stage, we train a command-conditioned whole-body reference generator using single rigid body dynamics combined with full humanoid kinematics. In the second stage, the frozen ROM policy is rolled out online to generate references for training a whole-body tracking policy on the full-order dynamics model. Learning a kino-dynamically consistent reference generator in the simplified ROM space enables us to generate references over a broad command space and over multiple locomotion strategies using a single unified framework. We demonstrate the approach on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot, achieving longitudinal velocities from -2.0 m/s to 2.8 m/s, lateral velocities up to +-1.0 m/s, angular velocities up to +-3.0 rad/s, one-legged hopping, and continuous transitions across the commands, all using one unified reference policy and one whole-body tracking policy.

</details>


### [121] [3D Occupancy-Language Fields from mmWave Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37929)
*Haowen Lai,Zhiwei Zheng,Mingmin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TextRadar 从全景雷达观测构建场景级 3D 占用-语言场，使重建场景可被开放式自然语言查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达可恢复场景几何并识别预定义类别，但缺乏面向开放式语言查询的持久空间表示，难以将不同视角观测关联到同一物理位置。

Method: 提出 TextRadar 框架：在表面位置结合占用与尺度相关语言特征，构建场景级 3D 占用-语言场；用单帧雷达蒸馏模型从每次雷达观测预测对齐的距离与语言特征，并基于雷达估计位姿配准预测，从而仅用雷达在新场景中重建该场。

Result: 在七栋建筑约 2 万条同步观测上，实现场景级自然语言查询和无提示语义可视化；相机到雷达的概念迁移分析显示，结构、材质和人体姿态的一致性高于颜色和印刷文字。

Conclusion: TextRadar 证明仅凭雷达即可构建可查询的占用-语言场，为开放词汇语义建图和跨视角空间查询提供可行方案，但颜色与文本等概念迁移仍受限。

Abstract: Radar systems can recover scene geometry and recognize predefined categories, but these outputs do not provide a persistent spatial representation for open-ended language queries. Such queries require linking observations from different viewpoints to the same physical locations. We present TextRadar, a framework that constructs a scene-level 3D occupancy-language field from panoramic radar observations. The field combines occupancy with scale-dependent language features at surface locations, allowing the reconstructed scene to be queried with new descriptions. A single-frame radar distillation model predicts aligned ranges and language features from each radar observation. Using radar-estimated poses, we register these predictions to reconstruct the field from radar alone in a new scene. Experiments on approximately 20k synchronized observations across seven buildings demonstrate scene-level natural-language querying and prompt-free semantic visualization. Analysis of camera-to-radar concept transferability shows stronger agreement for structures, materials, and human poses than for colors and printed text.

</details>


### [122] [What Actually Matters? A Paired Evaluation of Inference Settings for $π_{0.5}$ on BEHAVIOR-1K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37917)
*Ruben Hammele,Samira Huber,Mohammad Nazeri,Amirreza Payandeh,Alexandyr T. Card,Aaron Nguyen,Anuj Pokhrel,Daeun Song,Xuesu Xiao,Sören Pirk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 对100项BEHAVIOR任务系统配对评估VLA推理与数据采集策略，发现小规模策略难以均匀迁移；微调π0.5模型公开测试Q-score为0.39。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型有望支撑通用家庭机器人，但在小规模场景中部署的推理与数据采集策略未必适用于大规模、多样化任务套件，需要系统验证其泛化性。

Method: 基于2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge获胜方案微调π0.5模型，在全部100个任务上对已有推理与数据采集策略进行系统配对评估，并在公开测试集上报告Q-score。

Result: 微调π0.5模型在公开测试集取得0.39的Q-score；所评估策略在新环境和新任务上表现不一致，不能均匀迁移。

Conclusion: 现有小规模推理与数据采集策略不能直接推广到大规模多样任务，需针对新环境和任务重新评估与设计VLA方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising paradigm for general-purpose household robotics. However, inference and data-collection strategies that have been deployed in smaller-scale settings do not necessarily generalize to large-scale, diverse task suites. To address this gap, we conduct a systematic, paired evaluation of previously proposed inference and data-collection strategies across all 100 tasks. Our evaluation uses a fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ model based on the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge winner, which achieves a Q-score of 0.39 on the public test set. These results show that proposed strategies do not transfer uniformly to new environments and tasks. Project page, videos, and detailed analysis: this https URL

</details>


### [123] [When More Planning Hurts: Learned Planners for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37891)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在 PushT 的 16 个保留场景上评估一个仅 1,505 参数的固定动作更新器，并与 Adam、iCEM、LePlanner 比较成功率及规划+编码时间；该更新器解出 8/16，但未满足预设任务成本标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估轻量固定动作更新器相对现有规划器的任务成功率与计算成本，并检验增加更新预算是否能挽救失败任务。

Method: 在 PushT 的 16 个场景（8 个场景簇）上进行配置选择后的保留评估；比较 Selected Adam、adapted iCEM、released LePlanner 与固定动作更新器的解出任务数和总规划+编码时间；另做匹配实验，仅重放每个上下文前十条命令，然后给后续求解 4、8 或 12 次更新（后续均为 8 次）。

Result: 固定动作更新器解出 8/16，总规划+编码时间 59.079s，但未满足预设任务成本标准；Selected Adam 解出 7 个、218.440s，adapted iCEM 解出 2 个、49.309s，released LePlanner 解出 16 个、5.652s。更新预算 4/8/12 分别取得 9/8/7 次成功；额外更新未挽救任何失败任务，成功/失败变化均发生在一个场景簇中，额外更新降低了所选潜世界模型轨迹成本，但原本成功任务变为失败。

Conclusion: 该轻量动作更新器未达到预设成本标准，且增加更新次数并不带来稳定收益，甚至可能损害成功任务；作者随论文提供代码、权重、评估器、执行轨迹与成本核算。

Abstract: On PushT, our fixed action updater (1,505 parameters) solves 8 of 16 scenes reserved from our configuration selection. The comparison planners perform as follows: Selected Adam (7 tasks solved, total planning and encoding time 218.440s), adapted iCEM (2 tasks solved, total planning and encoding time 49.309s), released LePlanner (16 tasks solved, total planning and encoding time 5.652s). The total planning and encoding time for our action updater is 59.079s. Note that our action updater does not satisfy the task-cost criterion we fixed before evaluation. We also conduct another matched experiment where we only replay the first ten commands in each context and then allow the next solve 4, 8 or 12 updates (subsequent solves all get 8 updates). Among all 16 contexts (8 scene clusters), the 3 budgets get 9, 8 and 7 successes, respectively. Interestingly, the extra updates did not rescue any failing task in this experiment. All the changes in success/failure happen in one scene cluster, where we observe that the extra updates decrease the costs of the selected latent world-model trajectories for our updater, while the tasks that used to be successes become failures. The code, weights, evaluators, execution traces and cost accounting accompany the paper.

</details>


### [124] [Echo: Scaling Visual Reinforcement Learning with Off-policy Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37865)
*Gagan Khandate*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出单阶段离策略视觉强化学习框架 Echo，通过逆向蒸馏让状态辅助策略模仿不断演化的视觉策略并采集无渲染经验，从而在不按比例增加视觉计算的情况下扩展经验收集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 加速的视觉强化学习因渲染和视觉策略推理成本高而计算昂贵；常用两阶段教师—学生蒸馏虽可训练视觉策略，但会引入模仿差距。

Method: Echo 是单阶段 off-policy 框架：状态辅助策略通过逆向蒸馏模仿视觉策略，收集廉价、未渲染经验；这些经验用于离策略价值学习，并通过共享的 privileged critic 直接改进视觉策略。作者以 FastTD3 实例化 Echo，并在 Genesis 中于三个视觉控制任务和两个灵巧操作任务上评估。

Result: 在评估配置下，相较 SDPG，Echo 在三个 locomotion 任务上将达到参考回报的时间平均减少 60%，在两个 hand 任务上平均减少 56%；峰值平均回报分别提升 62% 和 12%。两个 hand 任务在相同 simulator-transition 预算下也取得更高平均回报。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明，扩展离策略经验是提升直接视觉强化学习效率的有前景方向。

Abstract: Scaling visual reinforcement learning (RL) with GPU-accelerated simulation is computationally expensive due to rendering and visual-policy inference costs. Consequently, two-stage teacher--student distillation is commonly used to train visual policies, but introduces an imitation gap. In this preliminary study, we introduce \echomethod{}, \textbf{a single-stage off-policy RL framework that scales experience collection without proportionally increasing visual computation}. Through \textbf{inverse distillation}, a state-based auxiliary policy imitates the evolving visual policy and collects inexpensive, unrendered experience. Echo leverages this experience for off-policy value learning, directly improving the visual policy through a shared privileged critic. We instantiate \echomethod{} with FastTD3 and evaluate it on three visual control tasks and two dexterous manipulation tasks in Genesis. Relative to \sdpgmethod{} under the evaluated configurations, Echo reduces time to reference returns by an average of \textbf{60\% across the three locomotion tasks} and \textbf{56\% across both hand tasks}, while improving peak mean returns by \textbf{62\% and 12\%}, respectively. Both hand tasks also achieve higher mean returns at matched simulator-transition budgets. These initial results suggest that scaling off-policy experience is a promising approach to efficient direct visual RL.

</details>


### [125] [TACOS: Terrain-Aware COntact Supervision for Visual Footstep Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37806)
*Jean-Michel Fortin,William Larrivée-Hardy,Gabriel Jeanson,Olivier Gamache,Daniele De Martini,François Pomerleau,Philippe Giguère*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TACOS：基于腿式状态估计因子图残差构建足步不稳定评分，并投影到前序图像以监督密集视觉预测器；硬评分与因果软评分两种变体在接触检测、严重度排序和户外视觉预测中均优于基线，并在50 m森林轨迹上验证泥泞、植被等非几何地形处理与倒枝绕行能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类依靠视觉预判地面响应再迈步；自监督学习有望赋予腿足机器人同样能力，但监督信号通常凭直觉选取，其对模型学习内容的影响缺乏系统检验。

Method: TACOS从腿式状态估计的因子图残差定义一致且有意义的足步不稳定评分，将连续评分投影到前序图像监督稠密视觉预测器；提出利用后见信息的硬评分和可即时使用的因果软评分两个变体，并在无共享数据或标签的两项独立评估中与当前替代方法比较。

Result: 在1716个人工标注接触上，两变体在检测不稳定和排序严重度方面均优于所有评估替代方法，池化严重度排序较最强基线提升28%；在三个户外测试运行中，该指标训练的视觉预测器最准确，秩相关较最强替代提升58%，加入板载上下文后再提升25%；50 m森林真实实验中，训练后的预测器可处理泥泞和植被等非几何地形属性，并绕开倒枝等几何障碍。

Conclusion: TACOS提供了有意义且一致的足步接触监督信号，可有效训练地形感知视觉预测器，并在真实户外环境中验证其对非几何地形属性的适应和对几何障碍的绕行能力。

Abstract: Humans navigate their environment based on their vision system, anticipating how the ground will respond before committing to a step. Self-supervised learning could provide legged robots the same ability, but the supervisory signal is typically chosen on intuition, and its effect on what the model learns is rarely examined. We present Terrain-Aware COntact Supervision (TACOS), which proposes a meaningful and consistent footstep instability score, based on the residual of a factor graph in legged state estimation. It projects the continuous score into preceding images to supervise a dense visual predictor. We propose two variants, a hard score that exploits hindsight and a causal soft score available immediately. Both metrics are compared against state-of-the-art alternatives in two independent evaluations that share no data or labels. On 1716 manually annotated contacts, both variants outperform every evaluated alternative at detecting instability and ranking its severity, improving pooled severity ranking by 28 % over the strongest baseline. On three outdoor test runs, our metric also trains the most accurate visual predictor, improving rank correlation by 58 % over the strongest alternative, and by a further 25 % when supplemented with onboard context. In a real-world experiment over a 50 m forest trajectory, the trained predictor handles non-geometric terrain properties such as mud and vegetation, while stepping around geometric obstacles like fallen branches. Code is available for reproducibility at this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [An Empirical Study of Wrist-Target Construction and Joint-Angle Limits in Humanoid Arm Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37801)
*MinJun Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究表明，在Unitree H1-2机械臂运动重定向中，将期望腕位构建与姿态匹配分开处理，并使用按可达距离归一化的腕目标，可显著减少关节角触及限位，同时降低限位帧的跟踪误差与执行力矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动重定向需将人类示教动作映射为机器人关节运动；作者关注期望腕位的构建方式是否会导致计算出的手臂关节角达到允许限位，并强调应将目标构建与姿态匹配偏好作为不同决策来考察。

Method: 使用示教手臂方向与机器人连杆长度构建“重构目标”，其肩到腕距离受示教段间夹角影响；另构建“可达归一化目标”，保持方向不变而重标定距离，二者采用相同示教处理与关节角求解方法。在Unitree H1-2上进行前伸扫描、25条单参与者记录、第二参与者重复砌砖与举升比较及硬件回放，并分析跨身伸手与臂间间隙。

Result: 前伸扫描中，重构目标在20次重复中有10次使关节角达界，归一化目标为0次；25条记录中，伸手臂对应计数为8与6，支撑臂为14与5；硬件回放中，方向匹配的手臂记录16条有12条命令关节到限位，归一化记录16条为0条，且合格记录中这些限位帧的跟踪误差与执行力矩高于同次运行其他帧。跨身伸手与臂间间隙揭示了径向调整的限制。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持将目标构建与姿态匹配视为相互独立的运动重定向决策，并应与操作任务所需的腕位和间隙一起评估。

Abstract: Motion retargeting maps a demonstrated human movement onto a robot to compute its joint motion. This study examines how the construction of the desired wrist position affects whether computed arm joint angles reach their permitted limits on a Unitree H1-2. A reconstructed target is formed from demonstrated arm directions and robot segment lengths, so its shoulder-to-wrist distance depends on the demonstrated angle between the segments even when no posture-matching preference is applied. A reach-normalized target retains that direction while rescaling the distance, using the same processed demonstrations and the same joint-angle method. Across a forward-reach sweep the reconstructed target places a joint angle at a bound in 10 of 20 repetitions and the reach-normalized target in none. Across 25 recordings from one participant the corresponding counts are 8 and 6 on the reaching arm and 14 and 5 on the supporting arm, and a second participant repeats the bricklaying and lifting comparison. In hardware replay a joint is commanded to a bound in 12 of 16 direction-matching arm-records and in none of the 16 normalized records, and within the eligible records both tracking error and executed torque at those frames exceed their values elsewhere in the same run. Cross-body reach and arm-to-arm clearance identify limits of radial adjustment. The findings support treating target construction and posture matching as separate retargeting decisions, and evaluating both alongside the wrist positions and clearance that manipulation requires.

</details>

### [127] [The Reality Gap in Automated Driving Systems Testing: Landscape, Challenges, and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38628)
*Stefano Carlo Lambertenghi,Oliver Weissl,Johannes Betz,Andrea Stocco*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对235项自动驾驶系统仿真测试“现实差距”研究进行系统性映射，分析其目标、方法、实验设置与评估证据，指出多数研究聚焦缓解差距但缺乏将差异与测试结论因果关联的多指标证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真对自动驾驶系统测试至关重要，但仿真与现实之间的差异可能削弱测试结论；现有文献究竟处理哪些差异、又如何证明仿真与现实对应仍不清晰。

Method: 从4195篇文献中筛选235项测量或缓解ADS测试现实差距的原始研究，系统刻画其目标、对象、方法、实验设置和评估证据；按六个ADS测试组件区分仿真与真实执行，并区分用于构建方法的真实世界信息与用于评估其主张的证据。

Result: 76%的研究关注缓解现实差距，主要针对场景与交通参与者真实性和感知；94%报告验证或测量程序；仅61项研究报告覆盖传感器/感知对应、ADS行为、车辆动力学或安全结果等多个指标族的指标，且这种覆盖不足以建立差异与测试判定之间的因果关系。

Conclusion: 真实世界内容和物理执行需结合所评估的具体主张来解释；研究结果呼吁采用主张特定评估和组件级报告，以确定哪些差异会影响测试结论，以及仿真证据何时能支持关于真实世界ADS行为的结论。

Abstract: Simulation is essential for testing automated driving systems (ADS), but discrepancies between simulation and reality can undermine testing conclusions. It remains unclear which discrepancies the literature addresses and what evidence supports correspondence claims. We present a systematic mapping of 235 primary studies that measure or mitigate the reality gap in ADS testing, selected from 4,195 publications. We characterise objectives, targets, approaches, experimental setups, and evaluation evidence. Our classification distinguishes simulated and real-world execution across six ADS test components, separately for each approach and its evaluation reference. This distinguishes real-world information used to construct an approach from evidence used to evaluate its claims. Of the included studies, 76% address mitigation, primarily targeting scenario and actor realism and perception, while 94% report validation or measurement procedures. Only 61 studies report metrics from multiple families covering sensor/perception correspondence, ADS behaviour, vehicle dynamics, or safety outcomes; this coverage does not establish causal links between discrepancies and test verdicts. The synthesis highlights that real-world content and physical execution must be interpreted against the claim evaluated. The findings motivate claim-specific evaluation and component-level reporting to establish which discrepancies affect testing conclusions and when simulation evidence supports conclusions about real-world ADS behaviour.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Influence of Agent Number and Behavior on Users' Psychological Responses in VR Evacuation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38536)
*Naoki Matsumura,Kensuke Sugihara,Tomoko Yonezawa*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过虚拟灾害仿真与多智能体结合，评估智能体数量及其情绪和行为对用户疏散决策与心理的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建有效的演练软件，并探究虚拟疏散中周围智能体的数量、情绪表达和行为动作如何影响用户。

Method: 构建包含多个可表达情绪智能体的虚拟灾害仿真环境，开展用户实验，评估疏散过程中智能体数量及其情绪与行为动作对用户的影响。

Result: 用户在许多智能体共同疏散时表现得更自信；智能体选择的疏散路线会影响用户的决策。

Conclusion: 用户疏散行为不仅受环境本身影响，也受周围智能体集体行为影响，因此演练训练需考虑智能体行为对用户心理状态的作用。

Abstract: This study aims to build effective drill software by combining a virtual simulation of a disaster with multiple agents capable of expressing emotions. We evaluated how the number of agents and their emotional and behavioral actions during evacuation influence users in a virtual space. The results showed that users tended to act more confidently when many agents evacuated with them, and that the evacuation route chosen by the agents influenced the users' decision-making. These findings indicate that users' evacuation behavior is shaped not only by the environment itself but also by the collective behavior of surrounding agents, underscoring the need to consider how agent behavior affects users' psychological states during training.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Kairos: Semantics-Aware Orchestration of Planning Engines across Classical, Numeric, Temporal and Hybrid PDDL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39454)
*Francisco Martín Rico,Esther Aguado González,Francisco Miguel Moreno Olivo,Rodrigo Pérez Rodríguez,José Miguel Guerrero Hernández,Francisco J. Rodríguez Lera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kairos 根据任务语义选择并调度五种异构规划引擎，在统一时间/内存预算下覆盖从 STRIPS 到 PDDL+ 的规划，并用独立验证器保证返回计划可信、不可解结论有证明依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运行时重规划要求规划器能读取用户编写的完整 PDDL（持续动作、数值资源、时间窗、连续变化），快速求解且只返回已验证计划；专用规划器虽在 PDDL 子集上强，但覆盖范围、延迟和失败含义不一致。论文研究按任务语义选择异构引擎能否兼顾广覆盖、与专用规划器竞争，并明确每种答案的保证。

Method: Kairos 对每个任务进行 profiling，决定五种引擎是否适用：ground heuristic search、compressed and snap-action temporal search、hybrid dynamics 的 time steps、lifted search，并在统一时间与内存预算下调度。所有候选计划由独立于规划器变换的验证器检查；“不可解”仅在穷尽搜索可作为证明且搜索空间耗尽时报告。

Result: 在九个比较套件的 1,756 个任务上，Kairos 在完整限制下比各套件最强被评估规划器解出更多任务，数值、时序、PDDL3 和 PDDL+ 规划差异最大；在 100 ms 和 1 s 限制下也解出最多任务。按域重采样，九个套件中六个增益大于零；经典规划对 LAMA 在噪声范围内。额外 450 个特征导向任务和 26 个单特征探针显示 Kairos 处理全部 17 个探测特征，其他规划器最多处理 8 个。消融表明压缩时序搜索和 novelty fallback 是主要贡献；与 VAL 比较将所有分歧追溯到 PDDL+ 约定或已记录的 VAL 缺陷。规划器与基准开源。

Conclusion: 语义驱动的异构规划器组合加独立验证，可在保持与专用规划器竞争力的同时显著拓宽 PDDL 覆盖，并使计划有效性与不可解结论的保证更加明确；Kairos 和基准开源。

Abstract: Planning at run time, as in robots that re-plan while they act, needs a planner that reads the PDDL its users write (durative actions, numeric resources, time windows, continuous change), answers quickly, and delivers only validated plans. Specialised planners are strong on individual fragments of PDDL, but they differ in coverage, latency and in what their failures mean. We study whether selecting heterogeneous engines from the semantics of each task can give broad coverage of PDDL, from STRIPS to PDDL+, while remaining competitive with specialised planners and making explicit what each answer guarantees. Kairos profiles each task to decide which of five engines apply (ground heuristic search, compressed and snap-action temporal search, time steps for hybrid dynamics, lifted search) and schedules them under one budget of time and memory. Every candidate plan is checked by a validator independent of the planner's transformations, and "unsolvable" is reported only when a search whose exhaustion is a proof exhausts its space. On 1,756 tasks from nine comparative suites, Kairos solves more tasks at the full limits than the strongest evaluated planner of each suite, with the largest differences on numeric, temporal, PDDL3 and PDDL+ planning, and it also solves the most tasks at 100 ms and 1 s. Resampling over domains, the gain is above zero in six of the nine suites, while on classical planning against LAMA it is within the noise. A further 450 feature-oriented tasks and 26 one-feature probes show that Kairos handles all seventeen features probed, where no other planner evaluated handles more than eight. Ablations identify compressed temporal search and the novelty fallback as major contributors, and a comparison with VAL traces every disagreement to a PDDL+ convention or to a documented VAL defect. Planner and benchmark are open source.

</details>
